(muṭipu) tōḻi! talaivar ceṉṟa tēettu maḻaimiṉṉi niṉṟatu; nī niṉ nōyt talaiyaiyum allaiyāy, eṉ aḻipum iraṅkāniṉṟāy, aṅṅaṉam iraṅkum niṉṉoṭu yāṉum nam paṭar tīra avar varuvatu kāṇiya vammō.
taii, cāay, eṉ aḻipukkum iraṅkum niṉṉoṭu eṉka.
(vi-rai) nōkki yaṉṉavāyk katirviṭṭu eṉka. vaṇaṅkuṟu kaṟpu eṉap pirittu, ēṉōr vaṇaṅkutaṟkuriya kaṟpu eṉalumām. niṉṉoṭu yāṉum kāṇa vā eṉavum, iruvēm paṭartīrakkāṇa vā eṉavum iyaiyum. vam - vā eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ō: acai. kaiyatu - kaiyatai eṉat tirintu niṉṟatu. piṭi - piṭikkum iṭam. piṭi kaiyamainta eṉṟu pāṭam koḷḷutal poruntum; piṭittaṟku iṭam vaittu aḵtallāta iṭam cuṭṭuk koḷḷi yākkutal iyalpu.
(mē-ḷ) `iḷivē iḻavē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `aṇaṅkuṟu kaṟpoṭu...... niṉṉoṭi yāṉum' eṉṉum pakutiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu talaimakaṉ piriviṟkut tōḻi paṭarkūrntāḷ eṉac colliṉamaiyiṉ piṟaṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟiya iḻivupaṟṟi avalam piṟantatām eṉpar, pērā.
74. mullai
(talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ aḻinta kiḻatti, vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		viṉaivalam paṭutta veṉṟiyoṭu makiḻciṟantu
		pōrval iḷaiyar tāḷvalam vāḻttat
		taṇpeyal poḻinta paituṟu kālaik
		kuruti uruviṉ oṇcem mūtāy
	5.	peruvaḻi maruṅkil ciṟupala varippap
		paiṅkoṭi mullai meṉpatap putuvī
		veṇkaḷar arimaṇal naṉpala tāay
		vaṇṭupō taviḻkkum taṇkamaḻ puṟavil
		karuṅkōṭ ṭiralai kāmar maṭappiṇai
	10.	maruṇṭamāṉ nōkkam  kāṇṭoṟum niṉṉiṉaintu
		tiṇṭēr valava kaṭaveṉak kaṭaii
		iṉṟē varuvar āṉṟikam paṉiyeṉa
		vaṉpuṟai iṉcol naṉpala payiṟṟum
		niṉvalit tamaikuvaṉ maṉṉō alkal
	15.	puṉkaṇ mālaiyoṭu poruntik koṭuṅkōl
		kallāk kōvalar ūtum
		valvāyc ciṟukuḻal varuttāk kālē.
- maturaik kavuṇiyaṉ pūtattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14-17. alkal puṉkaṇ mālaiyoṭu porunti - nāṭōṟum varuttattait tarum mālaip poḻutuṭaṉ kūṭi, koṭuṅkōl kallāk kōvalar ūtum - vaḷainta kōliṉai yuṭaiya tam toḻilaṉṟip piṟatoḻilaik kallāta āyar ūtum, valvāyc ciṟu kuḻal varuttākkāl - valiya vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya ciṟiya kuḻal varuttātoḻiyiṉ;
3-5. taṇpeyal poḻinta paituṟu kālai - kuḷirnta maḻai poḻintatāṟ pacumaiyuṟṟa kālattē, kuruti uruviṉ oṇcem mūtāy - kurutiyaip pōlum civanta niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya oḷḷiya tampalappūcci, peruvaḻi maruṅkil ciṟupala varippa - periya vaḻikaṭōṟum pala ciṟiya varikaḷākap parakka, paiṅ koṭi mullai meṉpatap putuvī naṉ pala - paciya mullaikkoṭiyiṉ metteṉṟa cevviyai yuṭaiya meṉmaip patam vāynta naṉṟākiya mikappala putiya malarkaḷ, veṇkaḷar arimaṇal tāay - veḷḷiya kaḷar ākiya aṟalpaṭṭa maṇalil tāvik kiṭappa, vaṇṭu pōtu aviḻkkum taṇkamaḻ puṟavil - vaṇṭukaḷ arumpiṉai malarttum taṇṇiya kamaḻum mullai nilattil ;
9-10. karuṅkōṭṭu iralai - kariya kompiṉaiyuṭaiya āṇ māṉiṉatu, kāmar maṭappiṇai māṉ maruṇṭa nōkkam kāṇṭoṟum - aḻakiya iḷaiya peṇmāṉiṉ maruṭciyuṟṟa pārvaiyiṉaik kāṇuntōṟum, niṉ niṉaintu - niṉṉai eṇṇi;
1-2. viṉai valampaṭutta veṉṟiyōṭu - viṉaiyai veṟṟiyuṟac ceyta mēmpāṭṭuṭaṉ, makiḻ ciṟantu - makiḻcci mikku, pōrval iḷaiyar tāḷvalam vāḻtta - pōril valla vīrar taṉatu muyaṟciyiṉ valimaiyiṉai vāḻttivara;
11-14. valava tiṇ tēr kaṭavu eṉak kaṭaii - pākaṉē tiṇṇiya tēriṉai iṉṉum viraintu celuttuvāyāka eṉak kūṟic celuttik koṇṭu, iṉṟē varuvar - iṉṟē (nam talaivar) vantuṟuvar, āṉṟikam paṉi eṉa - nām naṭukkattai amaivēm eṉṟu kūṟum, vaṉpuṟai iṉcol naṉ pala payiṟṟum - vaṉpuṟaiyākiya pala iṉiya nalla coṟkaḷaik kūṟivarum, niṉ valittu amaikuvaṉ - niṉ collait tuṇintu amaintiruppēṉ, maṉ - atu māṭṭukilēṉ.
(muṭipu) (tōḻi,) alkal mālaiyoṭu poruntik kōvalar ūtum kuḻal varuttākkāl, (talaivar,) `puṟavil iralai maṭappiṇai nōkkam kāṇṭoṟum, niṉ niṉaintu, tiṇṭēr kaṭaii, iṉṟē varuvar āṉṟikam paṉiyeṉa vaṉpuṟai payiṟṟum niṉvalit tamaikuvaṉ maṉṉō.
iḷaiyar vāḻtta iṉṟē varuvar eṉak kūṭṭuka.
(vi-rai) tāḷ - muyaṟci. tāay - tāva eṉat tirikka. paṉi - naṭukkam. vaṉpuṟai - vaṟpuṟuttal. ciṟu kuḻaliṉ iṉṉicaiyum tuṉpattiṉai mikuvittaliṉ ataṉai valvāyc ciṟukuḻal eṉṟāḷ. mālaiyum kuḻalum varuttākkāl eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `iṉpattai veṟuttal'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kallāk kōvala rūtum, valvāyc ciṟukuḻal varuttāk kālē' eṉpuḻi iṉpattai veṟuttaṉaḷāyiṉum puṇarccikku ētuvām eṉpatu karuttu eṉṟār, pērā.
75. pālai
(poruḷvayiṟ pirivareṉa vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaṭkup piriyēṉeṉat 
talaimakaṉ colliyatu.)
		aruḷaṉ ṟāka āḷviṉai āṭavar
		poruḷeṉa valitta poruḷal kāṭciyiṉ
		maintumali uḷḷamoṭu tuñcal cellātu
		ericiṉan tavaḻnta iruṅkaṭaṟ ṟaṭaimutal
	5.	karikutir maratta kāṉa vāḻkkai
		aṭupuli muṉpiṉ toṭukaḻal maṟavar
		toṉṟiyal ciṟukuṭi maṉṟuniḻal paṭukkum
		aṇṇal neṭuvarai āmaṟap pularnta
		kalneṟip paṭarkuva rāyiṉ naṉṉutal
	10.	ceyirtīr koḷkaic ciṉmoḻit tuvarvāy
		avirtoṭi muṉkai āyiḻai makaḷir
		āram tāṅkiya alarmulai ākattu
		ārāk kātaloṭu tāriṭaik kuḻaiyātu
		ceṉṟupaṭu viṟaṟkaviṉ uḷḷi eṉṟum
	15.	iraṅkunar allatu peyartan tiyāvarum
		tarunarum uḷarōiv vulakat tāṉeṉa
		māri īṅkai māttaḷir aṉṉa
		ammā mēṉi aitamai nucuppiṉ
		palkācu niraitta kōṭēn talkul
	20.	melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pulantupala kūṟi
		āṉā nōyai āka 1 yāṉē
		piriyac cūḻtalum uṇṭō
		aritupeṟu ciṟappiṉ niṉvayi ṉāṉē.
- maturaip pōttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 17-21. māri īṅkai māttaḷir aṉṉa - mārik kālattē oḷirum iṇṭaiyiṉ kariya taḷirai yotta, am mā mēṉi - aḻakiya māmai niṟattaiyuṭaiya mēṉiyiṉaiyum, aitu amai nucuppiṉ - nuṇṇitāy amainta iṭaiyiṉaiyum, palkācu niraitta kōṭu ēntu alkul - pala maṇikaḷ kōtta mēkalaiyaiyuṭaiya  pakkam uyarnta alkuliṉaiyum uṭaiya, melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ - meṉmait taṉmaiyuṭaiya kuṟumakaḷē nī;
1-3. āṭavar - āṭavarkaḷ, poruḷ - uṇmaip poruḷāvatu, aruḷ aṉṟāka - aruḷ aṉṟākiṭa, āḷviṉai eṉa - āḷviṉaiyē yākumeṉa, valitta - tuṇinta, poruḷal kāṭciyiṉ - uṇmaiyaṟṟa aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya, maintu mali uḷḷamoṭu - valimikka uḷḷattāl, tuñcal cellātu - maṭital illātu;
4-9. eri ciṉam tavaḻnta - eriyum tīp paranta, iruṅ kaṭaṟṟu aṭaimutal - peruṅkāṭṭiṟ pukumiṭattē, kariku utir maratta - ilaikaḷ karintu utirappeṟṟa maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya, kāṉa vāḻkkai - kāṭṭiṉ vāḻkkaiyaiyuṭaiya, aṭu puli muṉpiṉ toṭu kaḻal maṟavar - kollum pulipōlum valiyaiyum kaṭṭappeṟṟa kaḻalaiyumuṭaiya maṟavarkaḷ, toṉṟu iyal ciṟu kuṭi maṉṟu niḻal paṭukkum - paḻaiyatāy varukiṟa iyalpaiyuṭaiya tamatu cīṟūriluḷḷa maṉṟattu niḻalilē kaṇpaṭukkum, aṇṇal neṭuvarai - perumaiyaiyuṭaiya neṭiya malaiyiluḷḷa, ām aṟap pularnta - nīraṟakkāynta, kal neṟip paṭarkuvar āyiṉ - kal vaḻiyiṟ celvārāyiṉ;
9-24. nal nutal - nalla neṟṟiyiṉaiyum, ceyirtīr koḷkai- kuṟṟamaṟṟa koḷkaiyiṉaiyum, ciṉ moḻi - cilavāya coṟ kaḷaiyum, tuvar vāy - pavaḷam pōṉṟa vāyiṉaiyum, avirtoṭi muṉ kai - viḷaṅkum vaḷaiyalai yaṇinta muṉ kaiyiṉaiyum, āyiḻai - āynta aṇikaḷaiyumuṭaiya, makaḷir āram tāṅkiya alarmulai ākattu - makaḷiratu muttāram pūṇṭa paranta mulaiyaiyuṭaiya ākattil, ārāk kātaloṭu - amaiyāta viruppuṭaṉ, tār iṭaikkuḻaiyātu - taṅkaḷ tārai iṭaiyē kuḻaiyac ceyyātē, ceṉṟu paṭu viṟal kaviṉ uḷḷi - ceṉ ṟoḻinta mikka aḻakiṉai niṉaittu, eṉṟum iraṅkunar allatu - eṉṟum iraṅkuvārāvarē yallātu, ivvulakattāṉ peyartantu yāvarum tarunarum uḷarō eṉa - ivvulakattē peyarttu avvaḻakiṉait taruvār evarēṉum uḷarāvarō eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam, pulantu pala kūṟi - veṟuttup palavum kūṟi, āṉā nōyai āka - amaiyāta nōyuṭaiyai ākavum, yāṉ - nāṉ, aritu peṟu ciṟappiṉ niṉ vayiṉāṉ - aritākap peṟṟa ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya niṉṉiṭat tiṉiṉṟum, piriyac cūḻtalum uṇṭō - piriyumāṟu karututalum uḷatō? illaiyākum.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷē! nī, āṭavar poruḷal kāṭciyiṉ uḷḷamoṭu tuñcal cellātu, kaṉṉeṟip paṭarkuvarāyiṉ, ceṉṟupaṭu viṟaṟkaviṉ uḷḷi iraṅkunar allatu peyartantu tarunarum uḷarō eṉap pulantu pala kūṟi āṉā nōyai yāka, yāṉ niṉvayiṉ piriyac cūḻtalu muṇṭō? (cūḻēṉ.)
(vi-rai) āḷviṉai - poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟci; aruḷ poruḷaṉṟāka, āḷviṉaiyē poruḷāka valitta eṉka. `āṅkavaiyorupālāka'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `aruḷal eṉpatu, makkaḷ mutaliya cuṟṟattārai aruḷal' eṉṟār pērāciriyar. piriviṉāl taṉakku varum ētaṅ kuṟittu iraṅkāmaiyiṉ, aruḷaṉṟāka eṉat talaivi kūṟiṉāḷ. ik karuttu, `poruḷē kātalar kātal, aruḷē kātalar eṉṟi nīyē' (53) eṉa muṉṉar innūluḷḷum, `poruḷē maṉṟa poruḷē, aruḷē maṉṟa vārumil latuvē'2 eṉak kuṟuntokaiyuḷḷum vantuḷḷamaiyuṅ kāṇka. ciṉam - neruppu : ākupeyar. karikupu eṉṉum eccam kariku eṉa vikārappaṭṭatu. karinta kutir pōlum eṉṟumām. `tāriṭaik kuḻaiyātu' eṉpatu iṭak karaṭakku. `avarē piriyac cūḻtalum' eṉṟum pāṭam uḷatu. itaṟkut talaivar niṉṉaip pirik karutalum uṇṭō veṉṟu tōḻi colliyatāka uraikka. itaṟkēṟpak, karuttiṉum, talaimakaṭkup piriyāreṉat tōḻi colliyatu eṉṟa pāṭamum uḷatu. aka nāṉūṟṟuk kuṟippuraiyāciriyar, yāṉē piriyac cūḻtalum uṇṭō eṉṟa pāṭattiṟkē, `niṉṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum avar piriya, avaroṭu yāṉ vicārippatuṇṭō' eṉat tōḻi kūṟṟākki valintu poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
76. marutam
(talaimakaṉai nayappittukkoṇṭā ḷeṉṟu kaḻaṟak kēṭṭa 
parattai talaimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		maṇkaṉai muḻavoṭu makiḻmikat tūṅkat
		taṇṭuṟai ūraṉem cēri vanteṉa
		iṉkaṭuṅ kaḷḷiṉ aḵtai kaḷiṟṟoṭu
		naṉkalaṉ īyum nāṇmakiḻ irukkai
	5.	avaipuku porunar paṟaiyiṉ āṉātu
		kaḻaṟupa eṉpavavaṉ peṇṭir antil
		kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ tēntār mārpiṉaṉ
		vakaiyamaip polinta vaṉappamai teriyal
		curiyalam porunaṉaik 3kāṇṭi rōveṉa
	10.	āti manti pētuṟ ṟiṉaiyac
		ciṟaipaṟain turaiic ceṅkuṇak koḻukum
		antaṇ kāviri pōlak
		koṇṭukai valittal cūḻnticiṉ yāṉē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. maṇ kaṉai muḻavoṭu makiḻmikat tūṅka - mārccaṉai ceṟinta mattaḷattoṭu (kāṇpārkku) makiḻcci mika (yāṅkaḷ) kūttāṭa, taṇtuṟai ūraṉ em cēri vanteṉa - taṇṇiya nīrttuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya ūraṉ em cērikkaṇ ataṉaik kāṇa vantaṉaṉāka (avvaḷaviṟkē);
3-6. avaṉ peṇṭir - avaṉ peṇṭir, iṉ kaṭuṅ kaḷḷiṉ aḵtai - iṉiya kaṭiya kaḷḷiṉaiyuṭaiya aḵtai eṉpāṉatu, kaḷiṟṟoṭu naṉkalaṉ īyum nāṇmakiḻirukkai - yāṉaikaḷaiyum nalla aṇikaḷaiyum (paricilarkku) vaḻaṅkum makiḻcci poruntiya nāḷōlakkattaiyuṭaiya, avai puku porunar paṟaiyiṉ - avaiyiṟ pukum porunaratu paṟaiyaippōla, āṉātu kaḻaṟupa eṉpa - oḻiyātu eṉṉai ikaḻvar eṉṟu kūṟuvar;
7-13. kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ tēm tār mārpiṉaṉ - kacciṉaiyum kaḻaliṉaiyum tēṉoḻukum tāraṇinta mārpiṉaiyum uṭaiyavaṉum, vakai amaip polinta vaṉappu amai teriyal - kūṟupāṭṭiṉ amaitiyōṭu viḷaṅkiya aḻaku amainta mālaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉum ākiya, curiyal am porunaṉai - kuḻaṉṟa mayiriṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya kūttiṉaṉākiya āṭṭaṉattiyai, kāṇṭirō eṉa - kaṇṭīrō eṉṟu (viṉāvi), ātimanti pētu uṟṟu iṉaiya - avaṉ kātaliyākiya āti manti mayakkamuṟṟu varuntiṭa, ciṟai paṟaintu uraii - karaiyiṉai mōtip paravi, ceṅkuṇakku oḻukum - nēr kiḻakkē ōṭum, am taṇ kāviripōla - aḻakiya kuḷirnta kāviri taṉ tiraikkaiyāl vauvik koṇṭamaipōla, koṇṭu kai valittal - cūḷiṉai mēṟkoṇṭu kaiyāṟ paṟṟik kōṭalai, yāṉ cūḻnticiṉ - yāṉ eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ.
(muṭipu) ūraṉ, yāṅkaḷ muḻavoṭu tūṅka, em cēri vanteṉa, avaṉ peṇṭir porunar paṟaiyiṉ āṉātu kaḻaṟupa eṉpa; ātimanti porunaṉaik kāṇṭirō eṉap pētuṟṟu iṉaiya, kuṇakku oḻukum kāviri pōlac cūḷmēṟ koṇṭu kaivalittalai yāṉ cūḻnticiṉ.
(vi-rai) kaṉaital - ceṟital; olittal eṉak koṇṭu, mārccaṉai yamaintu olikkum eṉalumām. tūṅkal - āṭutal. peṇṭir eṉṟatu talaimakaḷai.
antil : acai. kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ mārpiṉaṉ eṉṉum kuṟippu muṟṟukkaḷ eccamāyp porunaṉ eṉṉum peyarkoṇṭu muṭintaṉa. porunaṉ - āṭṭaṉatti. itaṉai, `āṭṭaṉatti nalaṉayan turaiit, tāḻiruṅ katuppiṟ kāviri vavvaliṉ, mātiran tuḻaii matimaruṇ ṭalanta, ātimanti'1 eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika.
uraii - parantu. kāviripōla - kāviri taṉ tiraikkaiyāl vauvik koṇṭamaipōla: itu toḻiluvamam.
āṭaṟ ṟakaiyāṉātal pāṭaṟkuralāṉātal, peṟṟēṉeṉṟu colluvā ḷāyiṉ, yāṉ ippaṭic ceyvēṉ eṉṟāḷ.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, talaivaṉiṉ kāmak kiḻattiyāya iḷamaip paruvattāḷorutti, taṉpāṟ ṟalaivaṉ varuvatu paṟṟit talaivi pulantatākak kēṭṭavaḻip perumitam koṇṭu kūṟiyatākum eṉpar, nac.
77. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriyak karutiya neñciṟkuc collic celavu aḻuṅku vittatu.)
		naṉṉutal pacappavum āḷviṉai tarīiyar
		tuṉṉaruṅ kāṉan tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉap
		piṉṉiṉṟu cūḻntaṉai yāyiṉaṉ ṟiṉṉāc
		cūḻnticiṉ vāḻiya neñcē veytuṟa
	5.	iṭiyumiḻ vāṉam nīṅki yāṅkaṇum
		kuṭipatip peyarnta cuṭṭuṭai mutupāḻk
		kayiṟupiṇik kuḻici ōlai koṇmār
		poṟikaṇ ṭaḻikkum āvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ
		uyirtiṟam peyara nallamark kaṭanta
	10.	taṟuka ṇāḷar kuṭar tarīit teṟuvarac
		ceñcevi eruvai yañcuvara ikukkuṅ
		kallatark kavalai pōkiṉ cīṟūrp
		pullarai yittip pukarpaṭu nīḻal
		elvaḷi yalaikkum iruḷkūr mālai
	15.	vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ vaṇaṅkuviṟ ṟaṭakkai
		āṉā naṟaviṉ vaṇmakiḻp piṭṭaṉ
		poruntā maṉṉar aruñcamat tuyartta
		tiruntilai yeḵkam pōla
		aruntuyar tarumivaḷ paṉivār kaṇṇē.
- maruta ṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 4. neñcē vāḻiya.;
1-4. naṉṉutal pacappavum - nam talaiviyiṉ oḷḷiya neṟṟi pacantiṭavum, āḷviṉai tarīiyar - muyaṟciyāl poruḷ īṭṭutaṟku, tuṉ aruṅ kāṉam - aṭaitaṟkariya kāṭṭu neṟiyil, tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉa - cellutal naṉṟeṉa eṇṇi, piṉṉiṉṟu - eṉ piṉṉē niṉṟu, cūḻntaṉaiyāyiṉ - eṇṇutiyāyiṉ, naṉṟu iṉṉā cūḻnti ciṉ - mikavum koṭiyavaṟṟai āyntukoṇṭaṉaiyāvāy; (etaṉāleṉiṉ,)
4-6. veytuuṟa iṭi umiḻ vāṉam - iṭikaḷai umiḻum mēkam vemmai mika, nīṅki - maḻai peyyātu nīṅkutalāl, yāṅkaṇum kuṭi patip peyarnta - evviṭattum kuṭikaḷ tattam patikaḷiṉiṉṟum peyarntu pōkaṟ kētuvāya, cuṭṭu uṭai mutu pāḻ - palarum cuṭṭik kūṟum mikka pāḻiṭamākiya pālaiyil
7-8. kayiṟu piṇikkuḻici - kayiṟṟāṟ piṇittalaiyuṟṟa kuṭatti luḷḷa, ōlai koṇmār - ōlaiyai eṭuttuk kōṭaṟku, poṟi kaṇṭu aḻikkum - ak kuṭattiṉmē liṭṭa ilacciṉaiyai āyntu nīkkum, āvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ - avvōlaiyait tērum mākkaḷ avaiyiṟṟai veḷiyē īrtteṭuttal pōla;
9-11. uyir tiṟam peyara - tam uyir vēṟoru naṟpataviyiṟpuka, teṟuvara nal amark kaṭanta - māṟṟā rañca naṟpōrai veṉṟu paṭṭa, taṟukaṇāḷar kuṭar - añcā vīrariṉ kuṭarai, ceñcevi eruvai - civanta ceviyiṉaiyuṭaiya paruntu, añcuvara - kaṇṭār añcumāṟu, tarīi ikukkum - vāṅkip pōkaṭum;
12-14. kal atark kavalai pōkiṉ - kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya kavartta neṟiyiṟ pōyiṉ, cīṟūr pul arai itti pukar paṭu nīḻal - āṅkuḷḷa cīṟūrkaḷiṉ pāṅkarp pulliya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ittiyiṉ puḷḷipaṭṭa niḻalilē, el vaḷi alaikkum - peruṅ kāṟṟu alaikkum, iruḷ kūrmālai - iruḷ mikka mālaip poḻutil;
15-19. vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ - cēraṉ paṭaittalaivaṉākiya, vaṇaṅku vil taṭakkai - vaḷainta villaip periya kaiyiṟ koṇṭa, āṉā naṟaviṉ vaṇ makiḻp piṭṭaṉ - amaiyāta kaḷḷiṉatu mikka makiḻcciyaiyuṭaiya piṭṭaṉ eṉpāṉ, poruntā maṉṉar arum camattu uyartta - pakai maṉṉaratu ariya pōril eṭutta, tiruntu ilai eḵkam pōla - tiruntiya ilait toḻilai yuṭaiya vēl pōla, ivaḷ paṉivār kaṇ - nam talaiviyatu nīr oḻukum kaṇkaḷ (tōṉṟi), arun tuyar tarum - ariya tuyariṉait tarum ātalāl eṉka.
(muṭipu) neñcē vāḻiya! nī āḷviṉai tarīiyar kāṉam tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉac cūḻntaṉaiyāyiṉ, iṉṉāc cūḻnticiṉ; (etaṉāleṉiṉ,) mutu pāḻākiya kavalaiyaik kaṭantu celluṅkāl mālaiyil, ivaḷ paṉivārkaṇ tōṉṟip, piṭṭaṉ poruntā maṉṉar camattu uyartta eḵkam pōla aruntuyar tarum ātalāl eṉka.
kuḻici ōlai koṇmār poṟi kaṇṭaḻikkum āvaṇamākkaḷiṉ, eruvai taṟukaṇāḷar kuṭar tarīi ikukkum kavalai eṉka.
(vi-rai) tarīiyar - poruḷtarīiya reṉa viritturaikka. cūḻtalōṭu taṉṉait tūṇṭutaluṅ karutip piṉṉiṉṟu cūḻntaṉaiyāyiṉ eṉṟāṉ. piṉṉiṉṟu - uṭaṉ niṉṟu eṉṟumām. naṉṟeṉak kūṟip pōntu ataṉālām ētattaip piṉṉiṉṟu ārāyvāyāyiṉ eṉṟuraittalumām. nīṅki - nīṅkalāl eṉat tirikka. cuṭṭuṭai mutupāḻ - palarālum cuṭṭappaṭutaluṭaiya pāḻ eṉka. pāḻākiya kallatark kavalai eṉak kūṭṭuka. `kayiṟu piṇik kuḻici  yōlai koṇmār, poṟikaṇ ṭaḻikku māvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ' eṉṟatu, ūrāṇmai nāṭṭāṇmaik kaḻakaṅkaṭku uṟuppiṉarait tērnteṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu, uṭaṉpāṭu terivikkum takutiyuṭaiyār palarum eḻutik kuṭattiṉkaṭ pōkaṭṭa ōlaikaḷai, āvaṇa mākkaḷ palar muṉ kuṭattiṉ mēliṭṭa ilacciṉaiyaik kaṇṭu, nīkki, uḷḷirukkum ōlaikaḷai eṭuttu eṇṇit tērappaṭṭār ivareṉṉa muṭipu ceyvatōr vaḻakkattiṉaik kuṟippatu. itu kuṭavōlai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; paḻaiya kalveṭṭukkaḷil immuṟai virivākac collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āvaṇamākkaḷ - piramāṇam vāṅkukiṟa mākkaḷ eṉpārum uḷar. ōlai koṇmār aḻikkum mākkaḷ eṉpataṉai, aḻittu ōlai koḷḷum mākkaḷ eṉa māṟuka. el vaḷi - tōṟṟamuṭaiya vaḷiyumām.
78. kuṟiñci
(kaḷavuk kālattup pirintuvanta talaimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		naṉantalaik kāṉat tāḷi yañci
		iṉantalait tarūu meṟuḻkiḷar muṉpiṉ
		variñimi ṟārkkum vāypuku kaṭāattup
		poṟinutaṟ polinta vayakkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal
	5.	irumpiṇart taṭakkaiyiṉ ēmuṟat taḻuva
		kaṭuñcūṉ maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkuñ cāral
		tēmpiḻi naṟaviṉ kuṟavar muṉṟil
		muntū ḻāymalar utirak kāntaḷ
		nīṭitaḻ neṭuntuṭup pociyat taṇṇeṉa
	10.	vāṭai tūkkum varupaṉi yaṟciram
		nammil pulampiṟ ṟammūrt tamiyar
		eṉṉā kuvarkol aḷiyar tāmeṉa
		emviṭ ṭakaṉṟa ciṉṉāṭ ciṟitum
		uḷḷiyum aṟitirō ōṅkumalai nāṭa
	15.	ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palarpukaḻ nallicai
		vāymoḻik kapilaṉ cūḻac cēyniṉṟu
		ceḻuñceyn nelliṉ viḷaikatir koṇṭu
		taṭantāḷ āmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi
		yāṇṭupala kaḻiya vēṇṭuvayiṟ piḻaiyā
	20.	tāḷiṭūuk kaṭantu vāḷamar uḻakki
		ēntukōṭ ṭiyāṉai vēntar ōṭṭiya
		kaṭumparip puravik kaivaṇ pāri
		tīmperum paiñcuṉaip pūtta
		tēṅkamaḻ putumalar nāṟumivaḷ nutalē.	
- maturai nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14. ōṅku malaināṭa - ōṅkiya malai poruntiya nāṭṭaiyuṭaiyavaṉē;
1. naṉantalai kāṉattu āḷi añci - akaṉṟa iṭattaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭiṉiṭattē āḷiyiṉai yañci;
2-5. iṉam talait tarūum - taṉ iṉattait taṉṉiṭattē kūṭṭik koḷḷum, eṟuḻ kiḷar muṉpiṉ - viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum mikka valiyiṉaiyum, vari ñimiṟu ārkkum vāypuku kaṭāattu - variyiṉaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum vāyiṟpukum matattiṉaiyum uṭaiya, poṟi nutal polinta - puḷḷikaḷ poruntiya neṟṟiyāṟ polivuṟṟa, vayakkaḷiṟṟu oruttal - vali poruntiya āṇ yāṉaikaḷiṉ talaivaṉ, irum piṇart taṭakkaiyiṉ - kariya caruccaraiyuṭaiya periyakaiyāl, ēm uṟat taḻuva - pātukāval tōṉṟat taḻuvavum;
6. kaṭum cūl maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum cāral - mutaṟ cūlaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya piṭi naṭuṅkum pakka malaiyiluḷḷa;
7. tēmpiḻi naṟaviṉ kuṟavar muṉṟil - iṉimaiyuṟap piḻinta kaḷḷiṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟavarkaḷatu muṟṟattil;
8-10. muntūḻ āy malar utira - mūṅkiliṉ aḻakiya malarkaḷ utiravum, kāntaḷ nīṭuitaḻ neṭun tuṭuppu ociya - kāntaḷatu nīṇṭa itaḻaiyuṭaiya periya malar muṟiyavum, taṇeṉa vāṭai tūkkum - taṇṇeṉṟu vāṭaikkāṟṟu vīcum, varupaṉi aṟciram - tōṉṟu
kiṉṟa paṉiyaiyuṭaiya muṉpaṉik kālattil;
11-13. emviṭṭu akaṉṟa ciṉṉāḷ - emmaiviṭ ṭakaṉṟirunta cila nāṭkaḷuḷḷē, nam il pulampil - nammaip pirintirukkum taṉimaiyāl, tammūr - tammūriṉ kaṇṇē, tamiyar aḷiyar tām - taṉittirukkum iraṅkaṟkuriya ivar, eṉ ākuvar kol eṉa - ennilai yaṭaivarō eṉa eṇṇi iraṅki;
15-24. ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palar pukaḻ nallicai - ulakelām parakkum palarum pukaḻum nalla pukaḻiṉaiyum, vāymoḻi - meymmoḻiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, kapilaṉ cūḻa - kapilaṉ āyntu viṉaiceya, cēy niṉṟu ceḻum cey nelliṉ viḷai katirkoṇṭu - neṭun tolaiviṉiṉṟu vaḷamporuntiya vayalkaḷil viḷainta neṟ katirkaḷaik koṇṭuvantu, taṭantāḷ āmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi - avaṟṟaip periya tāḷiṉaiyuṭaiya āmpal malarālāya aviyaloṭu aṭṭu uṇpittu, yāṇṭu pala kaḻiya vēṇṭu vayiṉ piḻaiyā - pala yāṇṭukaḷ kaḻiyavum pōrai virumpic ceyyum iṭattiṉiṉṟum peyarāmal, tāḷ iṭūuk kaṭantu vāḷ amar uḻakki - pakaivar vāṭ pōriṉaik kalakki muyaṟciyāl veṉṟu, ēntu kōṭṭu yāṉai vēntar ōṭṭiya - nimirnta kompukaḷ poruntiya kaḷiṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya mūvēntarai yum piṟakkiṭac ceyta, kaṭum parip puravik kaivaṇ pāri - mikka viraiviṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraiyaiyum kaivaṇmaiyaiyu muṭaiya pāriyiṉ, tīm perum paiñcuṉai pūtta - iṉiya periya paciya cuṉaikkaṭ pūtta, tēm kamaḻ putumalar nāṟum ivaḷnutal - tēṉ maṇakkum putiya malareṉa maṇakkum ivaḷatu neṟṟiyiṉai;
13-14. ciṟitum uḷḷiyum aṟitirō - ciṟitaḷavēṉum niṉait taṟintīrō.
(muṭipu) malai nāṭa! āḷi yañci, oruttal taḻuvavum maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum cāraliṭattuk kuṟavar muṉṟilil vāṭai tūkkum aṟciram, nammil pulampil aḷiyar eṉ ākuvar kol eṉa, pāriyiṉ cuṉaipūtta putumalar nāṟum ivaḷ nutalaic ciṟitum uḷḷiyum aṟitirō.
(vi-rai) iṉam talai : iṅkut talai acaiyumām. eṟuḻ muṉpu : oruporu ḷiru col. miñiṟu eṉpatu ñimiṟu eṉṟāyiṟṟu. kaṭuñcūl - mutaṟ karu. `niṉṉayan tuṟaivi kaṭuñcūṟ ciṟuvaṉ'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. tēmpiḻi naṟavu - tēṉāṟ piḻinta naṟavumām. tuṭuppu - tuṭuppup pōṟaliṉ pūviṟku ākupeyar. nam mil - nām illāta eṉka. `vāymoḻik kapilaṉ cūḻac cēy niṉṟu, ceḻuñcey nelliṉ viḷaikatir koṇṭu, taṭantā ḷāmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi' eṉṟatu, tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarum pāriyiṉ paṟamparaṇai muṟṟiyiruppa, araṇiluḷḷār uṇaviṉṟi varuntāvāṟu, kapilar kiḷikaḷai vaḷarttuk katir koṇṭuvara viṭṭa varalāṟṟai uṇarttiyavāṟu. `pāri paṟampiṉ, niraipaṟaik kurīiyiṉam kālaip pōki, muṭaṅku
puṟac cenneṟ ṟarīiya vōrāṅ, kiraitēr koṭpiṉavākip poḻutupaṭa, paṭarkoḷ mālai paṭartan tāṅku'1 eṉa iv varalāṟu oḷavaiyārāluṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. malaroṭu kūṭṭi eṉpa taṉpiṉ, aṭṭu uṇpittu eṉa varuvitturaikka. piḻaiyā, īṟu keṭṭa etirmaṟai viṉaiyeccam. vēntar ōṭṭiya eṉa iraṇṭaṉurupu uyartiṇai maruṅkiṟ ṟokku niṉṟatu uṭampoṭu puṇarttalāṟ koḷka.
(u-ṟai) `naṉantalai..... maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum' eṉṟatu, `yāṉai kākkavum piṭi naṭuṅkiṉāṟ pōla,' nīyir ivaḷaip pātukākka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttuṭaiyarā yirukkavum, piriviṟku añcā
niṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
79. pālai
(poruḷvayiṟ pirinta talaimakaṉ iṭaiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tōṭpataṉ amaitta karuṅkai āṭavar
		kaṉaipoṟi piṟappa nūṟi viṉaippaṭarntu
		kalluṟut tiyaṟṟiya valluvarp paṭuvil
		pāruṭai maruṅkiṉ ūṟal maṇṭiya
	5.	vaṉpulam tumiyap pōkik koṅkar
		paṭumaṇi yāyam nīrkkunimirntu cellum
		cētā eṭutta cennilak kurūuttukaḷ
		akaliru vicumpiṉ ūṉṟit tōṉṟum
		naṉantalai aḻuvam nammoṭu tuṇaippa
	10.	vallāṅku varutu meṉṉā talkuvara
		varuntiṉai vāḻiyeṉ neñcē iruñciṟai
		vaḷaivāyp paruntiṉ vāṉkaṭ pēṭai
		āṭutoṟu kaṉaiyum avvāyk kaṭuntuṭik
		koṭuvil eyiṉar kōṭcuram paṭara
	15.	neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum nirampā nīḷiṭai
		kalpiṟan tattam pōki
		nillāp poruṭpiṇip pirinta nīyē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. vāḻi eṉ neñcē-;
11-17. āṭutoṟu kaṉaiyum av vāyk kaṭuntuṭi - āṭuntōṟum olikkum aḻakiya vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭiya tuṭiyiṉaiyum, koṭuvil eyiṉar kōḷ curam paṭara - vaḷainta villiṉaiyumuṭaiya maṟavar (pakaivarai vaḷaittuk) koḷḷum curattiṉkaṭ cella, irum ciṟai vaḷaivāyp paruntiṉ vāṉkaṇ pēṭai - periya ciṟaiyiṉaiyum vaḷainta vāyiṉaiyumuṭaiya paruntiṉ veḷḷiya kaṇṇiṉai yuṭaiya pēṭai, neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum nirampā nīḷiṭai (taṉ tuṇaiyiṉai nōkki) neṭuṅ kūppīṭu ceyyum cellat tolaiyāta nīṇṭa iṭamāya, kal piṟaṅku attam pōki - kaṟkaḷ viḷaṅkum kāṭṭil naṭantu, nillāp poruṭ piṇip pirinta nī - nilai nillāta poruḷiṉ mēluḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl piritaluṟṟa nī;
1-9. tōṭpataṉ amaitta karuṅ kai āṭavar - tōḷilē toṅkaviṭum cōṟṟu muṭiyaik kōtta valiya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya āṭavarkaḷ, viṉaippaṭarntu - (kiṇaṟu veṭṭum) toḻiliṟ pukku, kaṉaipoṟi piṟappa nūṟi - mikka tīp poṟi yuṇṭākap pāṟaikaḷai veṭṭi, kalluṟuttu iyaṟṟiya - kallutal ceytu amaitta, val uvarp paṭuvil - mikka uvaraiyuṭaiya kiṇaṟṟil, pāruṭai maruṅkiṉ ūṟal maṇṭiya - pāriṉai uṭaitta pakkattē ūṟiya nīrai uṇṇa vēṇṭi, nīrkku nimirntu cellum - nīr parukak kalittuc cellum, koṅkar paṭu maṇi āyam cētā - koṅkaratu olikkum maṇi pūṇṭa āyamākiya cevviya ākkaḷ, vaṉpulam tumiyap pōki - vaṉṉilaṅkaḷ tuṇipaṭac ceṉṟu, eṭutta cennilak kurūuttukaḷ - kiḷampiya cem maṇṇākiya niṟam poruntiya puḻuti, akal iru vicumpiṉ ūṉṟit tōṉṟum - akaṉṟa periya vāṉiṉkaṇ mikkut tōṉṟum, naṉantalai aḻuvam - iṭamakaṉṟa kāṭṭil ;
9-11. nammoṭu tuṇaippa - nam talaivi nammoṭu tuṇaiyāka, vallāṅku varutum eṉṉātu - vallapaṭi celvōm eṉṉātu, alkuvara varuntiṉai - iṅkē vantu taṅkiya aḷavil varuntukiṉṟaṉai; iḵteṉṉai!
(muṭipu) neñcē! kalpiṟaṅku attam pōki nillāp poruṭpiṇi pirinta nī, naṉantalai aḻuvam, nammoṭu (avaḷ) tuṇaippa, vallāṅku varutum eṉṉātu alkuvara, varuntiṉai.
(vi-rai) cōṟum uṭaṉ koṇṭupōyk kiṇaṟu veṭṭutaliṉ, tōḷpataṉ amaitta eṉṟār. kalluṟuttal - kallutal. val uvar - mikka uvar. paṭu - kiṇaṟu, pār - vaṉṉilam. nīrkku nimirntu cellum cētā maṇṭiya pōki eṭutta tukaḷ eṉka. varutum - pōvōm eṉṉum poruṭṭu. neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum eṉṟatu eyiṉar pōrkkuc cellutal aṟintu pēṭai tuṇaiyai aḻaikkum eṉṟapaṭi.

80. neytal
(iravukkuṟi vanta talaimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		koṭuntāḷ mutalaiyoṭu kōṭṭumīṉ vaḻaṅkum
		iruṅkaḻi yiṭṭuccuram nīnti yiraviṉ
		vantōy maṉṟa taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa
		niṉakkevaṉ ariyamō yāmē entai
	5.	puṇartiraip parappakam tuḻaiit tanta
		paṉmīṉ uṇaṅkaṟ paṭupuḷ ōpputum
		muṇṭakam kalitta mutunīr aṭaikarai
		oṇpal malara kavaṭṭilai aṭumpiṉ
		ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi āḻi aṟuppa
	10.	iṉamaṇip puravi neṭuntēr kaṭaii
		miṉṉilaip polinta viḷaṅkiṇar aviḻpoṉ
		taṇnaṟum paintā tuṟaikkum
		puṉṉaiyaṅ kāṉal pakalvan tīmē.
- 1maruṅkūr kiḻār peruṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3. taṇ kaṭaṟ cērppa - kuḷirnta kaṭaṟkaraiyaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē! nī;
 1-3. koṭun tāḷ mutalaiyoṭu kōṭṭumīṉ vaḻaṅkum - vaḷainta kāliṉaiyuṭaiya mutalaiyoṭu cuṟāmīṉ iyaṅkum, iruṅkaḻi iṭṭuc curam nīnti - kariya uppaṅ kaḻiyāya neruṅkiya ariya vaḻiyiṉaik kaṭantu, iravil vantōy - iraviṉkaṇ vantuḷāy;
7-13. muṇṭakam kalitta mutunīr aṭaikarai - nīrmuḷḷi taḻaitta kaṭalaiyaṭainta karaiyiluḷḷa, oṇ pal malara kavaṭṭilai aṭumpiṉ - oḷḷiya palavāya malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya kavaṭupaṭṭa ilaikaḷaiyuṭaiya aṭampiṉatu, ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi āḻi aṟuppa - civanta niṟamuṭaiya melliya koṭikaḷai (niṉ) tēruruḷ aṟuttuvara, iṉamaṇip puravi neṭuntēr kaṭaii - maṇikaḷaip pūṇṭa ōriṉamākiya kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya neṭiya tēriṉaic celutti, miṉ ilaip polinta viḷaṅku iṇar - oḷiyaiyuṭaiya ilaiyoṭu polinta viḷaṅkum pūṅkottukkaḷ, aviḻ poṉ taṇ naṟum paintātu uṟaikkum - malarnta poṉpōlum taṇṇiya naṟiya cevvit tātukkaḷaic coriyum, puṉṉai am kāṉal pakal vantīmē - puṉṉai maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya kaṭaṟkaraic cōlaiyil pakalil varuvāyāka;
4-6. entai - em tantai, puṇartiraip parappakam tuḻai it tanta - poruntum alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭalakattē tuḻavik koṇarnta, palmīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōpputum - palavakai mīṉkaḷiṉ vaṟṟaliṟ poruntum puṭkaḷai ōṭṭiyiruppōm; ākaliṉ;
4. yām niṉakku evaṉ ariyam - yāṅkaḷ niṉakku eṅṅaṉam ariyamāvēm?
(muṭipu) cērppa, iruṅkaḻi iṭṭuc curam nīnti iravil vantōy, neṭuntēr kaṭaii, puṉṉaiyaṅ kāṉal pakal vantīmē; mīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōpputum; yām niṉakku evaṉ ariyam?
(vi-rai) kōṭṭu mīṉ - kompaiyuṭaiya mīṉ; cuṟāmīṉ. iṭṭuc curam - vaḻi ciṟitākiya curam. neṟiyarumai kūṟiyavāṟu.
malara aṭumpu eṉak kūṭṭuka.
81. pālai
(pirivuṇarttiya talaimakaṟkut tōḻi talaimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintu vantu colliyatu.)
		nāḷulā eḻunta kōḷval uḷiyam
		ōṅkuciṉai iruppait tīmpaḻam muṉaiyiṉ
		pullaḷaip puṟṟiṉ palkiḷaic citalai
		oruṅkumuyaṉ ṟeṭutta naṉaivāy neṭuṅkō
	5.	ṭirumpūtu kurukiṉ iṭantirai tērum
		maṇpaka vaṟanta āṅkaṭ kaṇporak
		katirteṟak kaviḻnta ulaṟutalai nōṉciṉai
		neṟiyayal marāam ēṟip pulampukoḷa
		eṟiparun tuyavum eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭai
	10.	vemmuṉai aruñcuram nīntic ciṟanta
		cemmal uḷḷam turattaliṉ kaṟuttōr
		oḷiṟuvēl aḻuvam kaḷiṟupaṭak kaṭakkum
		māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ viḷaṅkil aṉṉaem
		maiyeḻil uṇkaṇ kaluḻa
	15.	aiya cēṟirō akaṉṟucey poruṭkē.
- ālampēri cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. aiya-, akaṉṟucey poruṭku - emmaip pirintu ceṉṟu tēṭum poruṭku;
10-11. ciṟanta cemmal uḷḷam turattaliṉ - ciṟanta talaimai vāynta num uḷḷam tūṇṭutaliṉ;
11-14. kaṟuttōr - vekuṇṭeḻunta pakaivaratu, oḷiṟu vēl aḻuvam - oḷirkiṉṟa vēliṉaiyuṭaiya pōrkkaḷattai, kaḷiṟu paṭak kaṭakkum - yāṉaikaḷ maṭiya vellum, māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ viḷaṅkil aṉṉa - mikka vaṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya kaṭalaṉ eṉpāṉatu viḷaṅkil eṉum patiyai yotta, em eḻil mai uṇkaṇ kaluḻa - ematu aḻakiya maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaḷāya talaivi aḻa ;
1-6. nāḷ ulā eḻunta kōḷ val uḷiyam - viṭiyaṟ kālattē eḻuntu ulāviya taṉ iraiyaik koḷḷutalil valla karaṭi, ōṅkuciṉai iruppait tīm paḻam muṉaiyiṉ - uyarnta kiḷai
kaḷaiyuṭaiya iruppai marattiṉ iṉiya paḻattiṉai veṟuppiṉ, palkiḷaic citalai - pala kiḷaikaḷāya kaṟaiyāṉ, naṉaivāy oruṅku muyaṉṟu eṭutta - naṉainta vāyāl oruṅkukūṭi varuntik kaṭṭiya, pulaḷaip puṟṟiṉ - pulliya vaḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya puṟṟiṉatu, neṭuṅkōṭu - neṭiya ucciyiṉai, irumpu ūtu kurukiṉ iṭantu - iruppu ulaiyil ūtum turuttiyaip pōṉṟu (uyirttup) peyarttu, irai tērum - iraiyiṉai āynteṭuttuṇṇum, maṇpaka vaṟanta āṅkaṇ - maṇ piḷavupaṭa vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa pālai nilaimāya avviṭattu;
6-10. kaṇ porak katir teṟa - veyyil kaṇkaḷaip pārkka voṇṇātavāṟu pora ñāyiṟu kāytaliṉ, neṟiayal marāam - neṟiyiṉ ayalatākiya veṇkaṭampukaḷiṉ, kaviḻnta ulaṟutalai nōṉciṉai ēṟi - (taḻaikaḷ vāṭik) kaviḻntuḷa kāynta ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya valiya kiḷaikaḷil ēṟi, pulampukoḷa ēṟi paruntu uyavum - iraiyiṉaip pāynteṭukkum paruntu taṉimai koḷa varuntum, eṉṟūḻ nīḷ iṭai - veppam mikka nīṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷāya, vemmuṉai aruñcuram nīnti - vevviya muṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya ariya curattiṉait tāṇṭi;
15. cēṟirō - celluvīrō.
(muṭipu) aiya! poruṭku uḷḷam turattaliṉ, uṇ kaṇ kaluḻa, aruñcuram nīntic cēṟirō.
uḷiyam tīmpaḻam muṉaiyiṉ neṭuṅkōṭiṭantu irai tērum āṅkaṇ paruntu uyavum nīḷiṭai aruñcuram eṉka.
(vi-rai) `atta viruppai yārkaḻal putuppūt, tuytta vāya tuṇikalam  parakka,.... vaṉkai yeṇkiṉ vayanirai parakkum'1 eṉak karaṭi iruppaippū uṇṭalum, `īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta, kurumpi valcip peruṅkai yēṟṟai'2 eṉap puṟṟām paḻañcōṟu uṇṭalum muṉṉar vantuḷḷamai kāṇka.
karaṭi akaḻkiṉṟa kālattu uyirttaliṉ, kurukiṉ iṭantu eṉṟār. muṉai - eyiṉar iruppu. aḻuvam - pōrkaḷap parappu. emai eḻil eṉap pirittu viyakkattakka aḻaku eṉṟuraittalumām. uṇkaṇ : ākupeyar; talaiviyai uḷappaṭutti em kaṇ kaluḻa eṉṟuraittāḷumām. itu celavaḻuṅkuvittatu.
82. kuṟiñci
(tōḻikkut talaivi aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.)
		āṭamaik kuyiṉṟa avirtuḷai maruṅkil
		kōṭai yavvaḷi kuḻalicai yākap
		pāṭiṉ aruvip paṉinīr iṉṉicait
		tōṭamai muḻaviṉ tutaikura lākak
	5.	kaṇakkalai ikukkum kaṭuṅkural tūmpoṭu
		malaippūñ cāral vaṇṭiyā ḻāka
		iṉpal imiḻicai kēṭṭuk kaliciṟantu
		manti nallavai maruḷvaṉa nōkkak
		kaḻaivaḷar aṭukkat tiyaliyā ṭummayil
	10.	3naṉavuppuku viṟaliyil tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ
		uruva valvil paṟṟi amputerintu
		ceruccey yāṉai ceṉṉeṟi viṉāay
		pularkural ēṉaṟ puḻaiyuṭai oruciṟai
		malartār mārpaṉ niṉṟōṟ kaṇṭōr
	15.	palartil vāḻi tōḻi avaruḷ
		āriruṭ kaṅkul aṇaiyoṭu porunti
		ōriyā ṉākuva tevaṉkol
		nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu nekiḻtō ḷēṉē.
- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi vāḻi-;
1-15. āṭu amaik kuyiṉṟa avir tuḷai maruṅkil - acaiyum mūṅkilil tuḷaikkappeṟṟa viḷaṅkum tuḷaiyiṭattē, kōṭai avvaḷi kuḻal icaiyāka - aḻakiya mēl kāṟṟiṉā leḻum oli kuḻaliṉ icaiyākavum, pāṭu iṉ aruvip paṉi nīr iṉṉicai - oli iṉiya aruviyiṉ kuḷirnta nīriṉ iṉiya ōcai, tōṭu amai muḻaviṉ tutai kuralāka - tokutiyākiya muḻaviṉ neruṅkiya icaiyākavum, kaṇakkalai ikukkum kaṭuṅkural tūmpoṭu - kūṭṭamāya kalaimāṉkaḷ tāḻa olikkum kaṭiya kural peru vaṅkiyattiṉ icaiyākavum (ataṉoṭu), malaip pūñcāral vaṇṭu yāḻāka - malaiccāraliṭattē pūkkaḷi luḷḷa vaṇṭiṉ oli yāḻiṉ icaiyākavum āka, iṉpal imiḻ icai kēṭṭu - iṅṅaṉam iṉiya palavāya olikkum icaikaḷaik kēṭṭu, kali ciṟantu - āravāram  mikku, manti nallavai maruḷvaṉa nōkka - mantikaḷāya nalla tiraḷ viyappuṟṟukkāṇa, kaḻai vaḷar aṭukkattu - mūṅkil vaḷarum pakka malaiyil, iyali āṭum mayil - ulāvi āṭum mayilkaḷ, naṉavup pukum viṟaliyil tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ - kaḷattiṟ pukuntāṭum viṟali pōlat tōṉṟum nāṭṭaiyuṭai yavaṉākiya, malar tār mārpaṉ- akaṉṟa tāriṉai yaṇinta mārpaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, uruva val vil paṟṟi - aḻakiya valiya villiṉaik kaiyiṟ paṟṟi, ampu terintu - ciṟanta ampiṉai āyntu koṇṭu, ceruc cey yāṉai cel neṟi viṉāay - taṉṉāl ampu eyyappeṟṟa yāṉai ceṉṟa neṟiyiṉai viṉavi, pular kural ēṉal puḻaiyuṭai oruciṟai - mutirnta katiriṉaiyuṭaiya tiṉaip puṉattiṉ vāyiliṉ oru pakkattē, niṉṟōṉ kaṇṭōr - niṉṟavaṉaik kaṇṭōr, palar - palarāvar;
15-18. avaruḷ - avar tammuḷ, ār iruḷ kaṅkul - ariya iruḷ ceṟinta iravil, aṇaiyoṭu porunti - aṇaiyiṉkaṇ taṅki, nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu - nīr coriyum kaṇṇiṉoṭu, nekiḻ tōḷēṉ - melinta tōḷaiyuṭaiyēṉāka, ōr yāṉ ākuvatu evaṉ kol - yāṉoruttiyumē āyatu eṉṉaiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi! nāṭaṉ, mārpaṉ niṉṟōṉ kaṇṭōr palar; avaruḷ aṇaiyoṭu porunti nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu, nekiḻ tōḷēṉ ōr yāṉ ākuvatu evaṉ kol?
(vi-rai) mūṅkilil vaṇṭu tuḷaitta tuḷaiyiṉ vaḻiyē mēl kāṟṟuc cellutalāl eḻum ōcai, kuḻalicai pōlum eṉṉuṅ karuttu, `āṭamait tumpi kuyiṉṟa, akalā vantuḷai kōṭai mukattaliṉ, nīrkkiyaṅ kiṉaniraip piṉṟai vārkol, āykkuḻaṟ pāṇiyilaituvan ticaikkum' (225) eṉap piṉṉar varutaluṅ kāṇka. ivvuruvakam, `parutiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉaik, kiruḷ vaḷaippuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻiṟ, kuḻalicai tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa, maḻalai vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya, veyiṉuḻai paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻai potumpar, mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ manti kāṇ paṉakāṇ' 1 eṉa maṇimēkalaiyil amaintiruttal aṟintu iṉpuṟuttakkatu. uruva - eṉṟataṉai uriccol īṟu tirinta teṉak koṇṭu uṭkup poruntiya eṉṟalumām. `uruvak kutirai maḻavar ōṭṭiya'1 eṉṟataṉ urai kāṇka. ceruc cey yāṉai - pōr ceytu ampu eyyap peṟṟa yāṉai. malar mārpu eṉa iyaiyum. malarnta tār eṉac ciṉaikkēṟṟa aṭaiyākkalu mām.  til : acai.
`āṭamai kuyiṉṟa....... nāṭaṉ' eṉṟatu iṅṅaṉam piṟark kellām iṉpañ ceyyum nāṭaṉaik kaṇṭār palaruḷḷum namakkē tuyarāvāṉ eṉṉeṉṟavāṟu. ic ceyyuḷ putumai poruḷākat tōṉṟiya viyappu eṉṉum meyppāṭu.
(mē-ḷ) `varaiviṭai vaitta kālattu varuntiṉum, varaiyā nāḷiṭai vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum, uraiyeṉat tōḻik kuraittaṟ kaṇṇum tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamu muḷavē'2 eṉṉum cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, `avaṉai āyattār palaruṅ kaṇṭāreṉa vantōṉ muṭṭiyavāṟum, avaruḷ nekiḻ tōḷēṉ yāṉē yeṉat tāṉē kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka' eṉṟuraittaṉar, nac.
`putumai perumai'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `manti nallavai.... tōṉṟu nāṭaṉ' eṉṟavaḻi paṇṭu orukālum kaṇṭaṟiyātapaṭi āṭiṟṟu mayil eṉṟamaiyiṉ, piṟa poruṭkaṭ ṭōṉṟiya putumaiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum, `malartār mārpaṉ.... nekiḻ tōḷēṉē' eṉṟatu taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya putumai paṟṟi viyappup piṟantatu. eṉṉai? taṉ karuttu veḷippaṭātu taṉ meykkaṭṭōṉṟiya putumaiyait talaivi viyantāḷ pōlat tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṉṟamaiyiṉ eṉavum, `iṉpattai veṟuttal'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `kaṇṭuyil maṟutta leṉpatu - iravum pakalun tuñcāmai'; atu `pularkura lēṉal.... nekiḻtō ḷēṉē' eṉa varum eṉavum uraittār, pērā.
83. pālai
(talaimakaṉ iṭaic curattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		valañcuri marāattuc curaṅkamaḻ putuvīc
		curiyā ruḷaittalai poliyac cūṭik
		kaṟaiyaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉat talaṟak
		kaḷiṟṟukkaṉ ṟoḻitta uvakaiyar kaliciṟantu
	10	karuṅkāl marāattuk koḻuṅkompu piḷantu
		perumpoḷi veṇṇār aḻuntupaṭap pūṭṭi
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyama mūtūr
		naṟavunoṭai nallil putavumutaṟ piṇikkuṅ
		kallā iḷaiyar perumakaṉ pulli
	15.	viyaṉṟalai naṉṉāṭṭu vēṅkaṭaṅ kaḻiyiṉum
		cēyar eṉṉā taṉpumikak kaṭaii
		eytavan taṉavāl tāmē neytal
		kūmpuviṭu nikarmalar aṉṉa
		ēnteḻil maḻaikkaṇem kātali kuṇaṉē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē!;
1-10. valam curi marāattu curam kamaḻ putuvī - valamākac curitta veṇkaṭampiṉatu curamellām kamaḻum putiya pūkkaḷai, curi ār uḷait talai poliyac cūṭi - curuḷ koṇṭa uḷaipōṉṟa mayiriṉaiyuṭaiya talaiyil viḷaṅkumpaṭi aṇintu, kaṟaiaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉattu alaṟa - uralpōṉṟa aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya peṇ yāṉai kāṭṭiṟ (pirintu) alaṟa, kaḷiṟṟu kaṉṟu oḻitta uvakaiyar - yāṉaik kaṉṟiṉaip pirittukkoṇṭa makiḻcciyai yuṭaiyarāy, kali ciṟantu - cerukku mikku, karuṅkāl marāattuk koḻuṅ kompu piḷantu - valiya aṭimarattiṉai yuṭaiya veṇ kaṭampiṉ vaḷaviya kompiṉaip piḷantu, perum poḷi veṇnār - peritāka uritta veḷḷiya nārk kayiṟṟāl, aḻuntupaṭap pūṭṭi - ak kaṉṟiṉai aḻuttampeṟak kaṭṭik koṇarntu, neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyamam mūtūr - nīṇṭa koṭikaḷ acaiyum aṅkāṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya paḻaimaiyāṉa ūril, naṟavu noṭai nallil putavu mutal piṇikkum - kaḷviṟkum nalla illiṉ vāyiliṭattē piṇittiṭum, kallā iḷaiyar - tam vēṭṭuvat toḻilaṉṟip piṟitu toḻil kallāta vēṭarkaṭku, perumakaṉ - talaivaṉāṉa, pulli - pulli eṉpāṉatu, viyaṉtalai nal nāṭṭu vēṅkaṭam kaḻiyiṉum - virinta iṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya nalla nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa vēṅkaṭa malaiyiṉaik kaṭantu celliṉum;
11-14. neytal kūmpu viṭu nikar malar aṉṉa - neytaliṉatu mukai yaviḻnta oḷi poruntiya malarai yotta, ēntu eḻil maḻaik kaṇ emkātali kuṇaṉ - aḻakiṉai ēntiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉaḷāya nam kātaliyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷ, cēyar eṉṉātu - cēymaikkaṇ ceṉṟavar eṉṉātu, aṉpu mikak kaṭaii - aṉpu mikacceluttiṭa, eytavantaṉa tām - tām nammāl aṇuka vantaṉa.
(muṭipu) neñcē! (nām) pulli vēṅkaṭam kaḻiyiṉum, em kātali kuṇaṉ tām cēyar eṉṉātu aṉpu mikak kaṭaii eyta vantaṉa.
(vi-rai) uḷai - talaiyāṭṭam. oḻitta uvakaiyar : oḻitta : peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭāka vantatu.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, tarutalum eṉṟa mikaiyāl, niraiyallāta kōṭalum at tuṟaippāṟpaṭum eṉṟuraittu, `kaṟaiyaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉat talaṟak, kaḷiṟṟuk kaṉṟoḻitta vuvakaiyar kaliciṟantu,..... kallā viḷaiyar perumakaṉ pulli' eṉṟa aṭikaḷaik kāṭṭi,...... yāṉaik kaṉṟaik kavarntavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟaṉar, nac.
84. mullai
(talaimakaṉ pācaṟaiyiruntu colliyatu.)
		malaimicaik kulaiiya urukeḻu tiruvil
		paṇaimuḻaṅ keḻili pauvam vāṅkit
		2tāḻpeyaṟ perunīr valaṉērpu vaḷaii
		mātiram putaippap poḻitaliṉ kāṇvara
	5.	irunilaṅ kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kālai
		neruppiṉ aṉṉa ciṟukaṭ paṉṟi
		ayirkkaṇ paṭāart tuñcupuṟam putaiya
		naṟuvī mullai nāṇmalar utirum
		puṟavaṭain tirunta arumuṉai iyavil
	10.	cīṟū rōḷē 3oṇṇutal yāmē
		eripurai paṉmalar piṟaḻa vāṅki
		ariñar yātta alaṅkutalaip peruñcūṭu
		kaḷḷār viṉaiñar kaḷantoṟum maṟukum
		taṇṇaṭai taḻīiya koṭinuṭaṅ kāreyil
	15.	aruntiṟai koṭuppavuṅ koḷḷāṉ ciṉañciṟantu
		viṉaivayiṟ peyarkkun tāṉaip
		puṉaitār vēntaṉ pācaṟai yēmē.
- maturai eḻuttāḷaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. malaimicaik kulaiiya urukeḻu tiruvil - malaimītu vaḷainta accan tarum aḻakiya villiṉaiyuṭaiya, paṇai muḻaṅku eḻili - muracameṉa muḻaṅkum mēkam, pauvam vāṅki - kaṭal nīrai mukantukoṇṭu, valaṉ ērpu vaḷaii - ulakiṉai valaṉāka eḻuntu vaḷaittu, tāḻ peyal perunīr - iṟaṅkip peyyum mikka  maḻaiyai, mātiram putaippap poḻitaliṉ - ticai yellām maṟaiyap poḻitalāl, kāṇvara irunilam kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kālai - kāṭciyuṟap periya nilam aḻakupeṟṟa iṉpam eytiya ikkālattē;
10. oṇṇutal - oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nam talaivi;
6-10. ayirkkaṇ paṭāart tuñcu - nuṇ maṇaliṉ
kaṇṇuḷḷa ciṟu tūṟiṉiṭattē tūṅkum, neruppiṉ aṉṉa ciṟukaṇ paṉṟi - neruppiṉai yotta ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉ, puṟam putaiya - mutuku maṟaiya, naṟuvī mullai nāḷ malar utirum - naṟiya pūviṉatākiya mullaiyiṉ putiya pūkkaḷ utirum, puṟavu aṭaintirunta arumuṉai iyavil - kāṭu cārntirukkum ariya muṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya neṟiyiluḷḷa, cīṟūrōḷ - ciṟiya ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷāḷ āvaḷ;
10-17. yāmē - yāmō, eri purai paṉmalar piṟaḻa vāṅki - neruppup pōṉṟa pala malarkaḷai māṟupaṭa vaittu valittu, ariñar yātta alaṅkutalaip peruñcūṭu - nellariñar kaṭṭiya acaiyum pakkaṅkaḷuṭaiya periya neṟkaṭṭiṉaik koṇṭu, kaḷār viṉaiñar kaḷantoṟum maṟukum - kaḷḷiṉai uṇṭa kaḷamar kaḷantōṟum koṭu pōkum, taṇṇaṭai taḻīiya koṭi nuṭaṅku ār eyil - marutanilam cūḻnta koṭikaḷ acaiyum ivvariya eyilai, aruntiṟai koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉ - pakaivar vaṇaṅki ariya tiṟaiyākak koṭuppavum ēṟṟuk koḷḷāṉāki, ciṉañ ciṟantu - ciṉam mikku, viṉaivayiṉ peyarkkum tāṉai - mēṉmēlum pōriṉkaṭ celuttum cēṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, puṉaitār vēntaṉ pācaṟaiyēm - mālaiyai aṇinta aracaṉatu pācaṟaiyiṭattē yuḷḷēm.
(muṭipu) eḻili poḻitaliṉ irunilam kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kālai, oṇṇutal cīṟūrōḷ; yāmē. āreyil tiṟai koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉ viṉai vayiṟ peyarkkum tāṉaip puṉaitār vēntaṉ pācaṟaiyēm.
(vi-rai) ēm - ēmam; pātukāvalumām. eripurai malar - tāmarai, ceṅkaḻunīr. piṟaḻa eṉṟatu pūvō ṭarinta nellaik kaṭṭuṅ kālattu ariyōṭu māṟa vaittuk kaṭṭap peṟutalāl avai tam miṉ māṟupaṭal. eyil - araṇ, ūr. tūtuviṭak karutiya talaivaṉ cīṟūrōḷ oṇṇutal eṉat taṉ varuttan tōṉṟak kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. itaṉait tūtu kaṇṭu kūṟiya teṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
(mē-ḷ) `ēvaṉ marapiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, itu tūtu kaṇṭu varuntik kūṟiyatu eṉavum, itaṉ kaṇ taṉṉūrum arumuṉaiyiyaviṟ cīṟūr eṉṟaliṉ, tāṉ kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām eṉṟum, `tāṉē cēṟalum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `itu, kuṟunila maṉṉar pōlvār ceṉṟamai tōṉṟak kūṟiyatu' eṉṟum, `oṉṟāt tamariṉum' eṉṉum cūttirattu itu, `ākit tōṉṟum pāṅkōr pāṅkiṉum'3 eṉṟataṟku utāraṇamāku
meṉṟum `kūtir vēṉil'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `viṉaivayiṟ........ pācaṟai yēmē' eṉat talaiviyai niṉaivaṉa vākaikku vaḻuvām; akattiṟku vaḻuvaṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
85. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriya vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		naṉṉutal pacappavum peruntōḷ nekiḻavum
		uṇṇā uyakkamō ṭuyircelac cāay
		iṉṉam ākavum iṅkunat tuṟantōr
		aṟavar allar avareṉap palapulantu
	5.	āḻal vāḻi tōḻi cāral
		īṉṟunāḷ ulanta meṉṉaṭai maṭappiṭi
		kaṉṟupaci kaḷaiiya paiṅkaṇ yāṉai
		muṟṟā mūṅkil muḷaitaru pūṭṭum
		veṉvēl tiraiyaṉ vēṅkaṭa neṭuvarai
	10.	naṉṉāṭ pūtta nākiḷa vēṅkai
		naṟuvī yāṭiya poṟivari maññai
		naṉaippacuṅ kuruntiṉ nāṟuciṉai yiruntu
		tuṇaippayirn takavun5 tuṉaitaru taṇkār
		varutum yāmeṉat tēṟṟiya
	15.	paruvaṅ kāṇatu pāyiṉṟāl maḻaiyē.
- kāṭṭūr kiḻār makaṉār kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi vāḻi-; naṉṉutal pacappavum - aḻakiya neṟṟi pacantiṭavum, perun tōḷ nekiḻavum - periya tōḷ melintiṭavum, uṇṇā uyakkamoṭu uyir celac cāay - uṇṇāmaiyā lāya varuttattāl uyir nīṅka melintu, iṉṉam ākavum - nām in nilaiyiṉamākavum, iṅku nam tuṟantōr - iṅku nammai viṭṭup pirintār nam talaivar, aṟavar allar avar - eṉavē avar aṟattiṉarallar, eṉap pala pulantu āḻal - eṉṟivvāṟu palavuṅ kūṟi veṟuttut tuyaril aḻuntātē,
5-15. cāral - malaic cāraṟkaṇṇē, īṉṟu nāḷ ulanta melnaṭai maṭappiṭi kaṉṟu paci kaḷaiiya - īṉṟu aṇimai kaḻinta melliya naṭai vāynta iḷaiya piṭiyiṉatum ataṉ kaṉṟiṉatum paciyaip pōkkaṟku, paiṅkaṇ yāṉai - paciya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya kaḷiṟu, muṟṟā mūṅkil muḷai tarupu ūṭṭum - mūṅkiliṉ muṟṟāta muḷaiyiṉaik koṇarntu uṇpikkum, veṉvēl tiraiyaṉ vēṅkaṭa neṭuvarai - veṟṟi poruntiya vēliṉaiyuṭaiya tiraiyaṉatu vēṅkaṭameṉum nīṇṭa malaiyil, naṉṉāḷ pūtta nāku iḷavēṅkai - nalla nāṭkālaiyiṟ  pūtta mika iḷaiya vēṅkai marattiṉ, naṟuvī āṭiya poṟi vari maññai - naṟiya pūkkaḷiṉ tukaḷai aḷainta poṟikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya varikaḷaiyuṭaiya mayil, naṉaip pacuṅ kuruntiṉ nāṟu ciṉai yiruntu - tēṉaiyuṭaiya paciya kurunta marattiṉ naṟumaṇam vīcum kiḷaiyiliruntu, tuṇaip payirntu akavum tuṉaitaru taṇkār - taṉ tuṇaiyai aḻaittuk kūppiṭum viraintu varum kuḷirnta kārp paruvamē, yām varutum eṉat tēṟṟiya paruvam - nam talaivar tām varuvōm eṉat teḷivitta paruvamākum, maḻai pāyiṉṟu - ataṟku maḻaiyum paravā niṉṟatu, atukāṇ - ataṉaik kāṇpāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi! nām iṉṉam āka, nam talaivar nammait tuṟantār, avar aṟavarallar, eṉap pulantu āḻal; taṇ kār, yām varutum eṉat tēṟṟiya paruvam, maḻai pāyiṉṟu; atu kāṇ.
yāṉai muḷai tarupu ūṭṭum vēṅkaṭa neṭuvarai vēṅkai naṟuvīyāṭiya maññai kuruntiṉ ciṉaiyiruntu tuṇaip payirntakavum kār eṉka.
(vi-rai) nam tuṟantōr eṉpatu nattuṟantōr eṉa vikāra
māyiṟṟu. nāḷ ulanta - nāḷ muṭinta; īṉṟaṇimai kaḻinta eṉṟapaṭi. piṭi kaṉṟu ivaṟṟiṉ paciyeṉa virittuk koḷka. vēṅkai pūkkuṅ kālam maṇanāḷ ākaliṉ, naṉṉāṭ pūtta eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām. kār viraivil vantuviṭum eṉpāḷ tuṉaitaru taṇkār eṉavum, maḻai pāyiṉṟu eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷ. eṉavē itu vēṉiṟ kālattu iṟutiyāyiṟṟu. maññai tuṇaip payirntu akavum eṉṟatu, kār kālattil pirintiruttal arumaiyāl kūṭutaṟku aḻaikkum eṉṟapaṭi.
86. marutam
(vāyil maṟutta tōḻikkut talaimakaṉ colliyatu. talaimakaḷaik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟirunta talaimakaṉ paṇṭu nikaḻntatu coṟṟu iṉpuṟṟiruntatūumām.)
		uḻuntutalaip peyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavai
		peruñcōṟ ṟamalai niṟpa niraikāl
		taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
		maṉaiviḷak kuṟuttu mālai toṭarik
	5.	kaṉaiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālaik
		kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ
		kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāṭalai vanteṉa
		uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭaiyar
		potucey kampalai mutucem peṇṭir
	10.	muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟaimuṟai tarattarap
		putalvaṟ payanta titalaiyav vayiṟṟu
		vāliḻai makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
		kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip
		peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇaiyai yākeṉa
	15.	nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari
		palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
		vatuvai naṉmaṇam kaḻinta piṉṟaik
		kalleṉ cummaiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
		pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
	20.	ōriṟ kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul
		koṭumpuṟam vaḷaiik kōṭik kaliṅkat
		toṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta ōrpuṟan taḻīi
		muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputai tiṟappa
		añciṉaḷ uyirtta kālai yāḻaniṉ
	25.	neñcam paṭarnta teñcā turaiyeṉa
		iṉṉakai irukkaip piṉyāṉ viṉavaliṉ
		ceñcūṭ ṭoṇkuḻai vaṇkātu tuyalvara
		akamali uvakaiyaḷ āki mukaṉikut
		toyyeṉa iṟaiñci yōḷē māviṉ
	30.	maṭaṅkoḷ mataiiya nōkkiṉ
		oṭuṅkīr ōti māa yōḷē.
- nallāvūr kiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. uḻuntu  talaippeyta koḻuṅ kaḷimitavai - uḻuttam paruppuṭaṉ kūṭṭic camaitta cevviya kuḻaitalai yuṭaiya poṅkaloṭu, peruñ cōṟṟu amalai niṟpa - periya cōṟṟut tiraḷaiyai uṇṭal iṭaiyaṟātu nikaḻa, nirai kāl taṇ perum pantar - varicaiyākiya kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya kuḷirnta periya pantaril, tarumaṇal ñemiri - koṇarntiṭṭa maṇalaip parappi, maṉai viḷakku uṟuttu - maṉaiyiṉkaṇ viḷakkiṉai ēṟṟi vaittu, mālai toṭari - mālaikaḷait toṅkaviṭṭu;
5-10. kōḷ kāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ tiṅkaḷ - tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkappeṟṟa vaḷainta veḷḷiya tiṅkaḷai, kēṭuil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷ talai vanteṉa - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya urōkaṇi eṉum nāḷ aṭaintatāka, kaṉaiiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉ peṟu kālai - annāḷilē mikka iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟ kālaiyil, uccik kuṭattar putu akal maṇṭaiyar - ucciyil kuṭattiṉai yuṭaiyarum kaiyiṉiṟ putiya akaṉṟa maṇṭai eṉum kalattiṉaiyuṭaiyarum ākiya, potucey kampalai mutucem peṇṭir - maṇattiṉaic ceytu vaikkum āravāramuṭaiya mutiya maṅkala makaḷir, muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟai muṟai tarat tara - muṉṉē taruvaṉavum piṉṉē taruvaṉavum muṟaimuṟaiyākat tantiṭa;
11-16. putalvaṉ payanta titalai av vayiṟṟu - makaṉaip peṟṟa tēmaluṭaiya aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya, vāl iḻai makaḷir nālvar kūṭi - tūya aṇikaḷaiyuṭaiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭi niṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavi - kaṟpiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātu naṉṟāya pala pēṟukaḷaiyum tantu, peṟṟōṉ peṭkum piṇaiyai ākeṉa - niṉṉai eytiya kaṇavaṉai virumpip pēṇum viruppattai yuṭaiyai āka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīroṭu corinta īr itaḻ alari - nīroṭu kūṭṭip peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūkkaḷ, pal iruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka - mikka kariya kūntalil neṟkaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅka;
17. vatuvai naṉmaṇam kaḻinta piṉṟai - iṅṅaṉam naṉṟākiya vatuvaik kaliyāṇam muṭinta piṉpu;
18-20. tamar kal eṉ cummaiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu - cuṟṟattār kalleṉṟa oliyiṉarāy viraintu vantu, pēr iṟkiḻatti āka eṉat tara - periya maṉaik kiḻatti āvāy eṉṟu kūṟik kūṭṭa, ōr il kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul - ōr aṟaiyil uṭaṉkūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil;
21-26. koṭumpuṟam vaḷaii - mutukiṉai vaḷaittu, kōṭik kaliṅkattu - kōṭip puṭaivaikkuḷ, oṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta puṟam taḻīi - oṭuṅkikkiṭanta iṭattiṉaic cārntu, muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukam putai tiṟappa - aṇaiyum viruppuṭaṉ mukattiṉai mūṭiyirunta āṭaiyiṉai nīkka, añciṉaḷ uyirtta kālai - añci uyirtta poḻutu, niṉ neñcam paṭarntatu eñcātu uraiyeṉa - niṉ uḷḷam niṉaintataṉai maṟaiyātu urai eṉṟu, piṉ yāṉ viṉavaliṉ - yāṉ piṉpu viṉavutaliṉ, iṉ nakai irukkai - iṉiya makiḻcciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya irukkaiyiṉkaṇ;
27-31. māviṉ maṭaṅ koḷ mataiiya nōkkiṉ - māṉiṉ maṭattiṉaik koṇṭatum cerukkiṉai yuṭaiyatumāṉa nōkkiṉaiyum, oṭuṅku īr ōti - oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntaliṉaiyum uṭaiya, māayōḷ - māmai niṟattiṉai yuṭaiyāḷ;
ceñ cūṭṭu oṇ kuḻai vaṇkātu tuyalvara - civanta maṇikaḷ patitta oḷḷiya kuḻai vaḷaviya kātiṉkaṇ acaiya, akam mali uvakaiyaḷ āki - uḷḷam niṟainta makiḻcciyiṉaḷ āki, mukaṉ ikuttu - mukattiṉait tāḻttu, oyyeṉa iṟaiñciyōḷ - viraintu talaivaṇaṅkiṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) vatuvai naṉ maṇam kaḻinta piṉṟai. tamartara ōriṟkūṭiya kaṅkul, kōṭik kaliṅkattu oṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta puṟam taḻīi, mukam putai tiṟappa, añciṉaḷ uyirttakālai, niṉ neñcam paṭarntatu eñcātu uraiyeṉa yāṉ viṉavaliṉ, māyōḷ mukaṉ ikuttu oyyeṉa iṟaiñciyōḷ.
nāḷ talaivanteṉa, amalai niṟpa, pantar viḷakkuṟuttu, mālai toṭari, mutu cem peṇṭir tarat tara, makaḷir nālvar kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇaiyai ākeṉa, nīroṭu corinta alari katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka vatuvai naṉ maṇam kaḻinta piṉṟai eṉka.
(vi-rai) mitavai - poṅkal; kummāyamumām. uṇpār iṭaiyaṟāmaipaṟṟi amalai niṟpa eṉṟāṉ. ñemiri - parappi eṉap piṟaviṉaip poruṭṭu. viḷakkuṟuttu - tūymai ceytu eṉṟumām. kaṉaiyiru ḷakaṉṟakālai yeṉṟamaiyāl, muṟpakkam (pūrva pakkam) eṉpatu peṟṟām. kōḷ eṉṟatu īṇṭut tīya kōḷkaḷai. urōkaṇi eṉpataṉai varuvittu, viḻuppukaḻ nāḷāvatu urōkaṇi tiṅkaḷai aṭainta nāḷ eṉṉalumām. urōkaṇiyum tiṅkaḷum kūṭiya nāḷ kaliyāṇattiṟkuc ciṟantateṉpatu, `tiṅkaṭ cakaṭam vēṇṭiya tukaṭīr kūṭṭattu ...... vatuvai maṇṇiya makaḷir'1 eṉap piṉṉar in nūluḷḷum, `vāṉūr matiyam cakaṭaṉaiya vāṉattuc, cāli yorumīṉ takaiyāḷaik kōvalaṉ, māmutu pārppāṉ maṟaivaḻi kāṭṭiṭat tīvalam ceyvatu'1 eṉac cilappatikārat tuḷḷum varutalāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. kaliyāṇam ellārum pukutaṟku uriyatākaliṉ, `potu' eṉappaṭṭatu. piṇai - viruppam; `piṇaiyum pēṇum peṭpiṉ poruḷa'2 eṉpavākaliṉ. nelloṭu tayaṅka eṉṟamaiyāl, nellum corintamai peṟṟām. vatuvai maṇamāvatu - kuḷippāṭṭal. kōṭik kaliṅkam- putup puṭaivai. ōrpuṟan taḻīi : ōr, acai: yāḻa : acai talaimakaṉ tōḻikku vāyiṉ maṟuttavaḻik kūṟiyatāyiṉ talaivi ekkālattum eṉpāl iṉṉatōr aṉpuṭaiyaḷ; avaḷ karuttaṟiyātu kūṟukiṉṟaṉai eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷaik kāṭṭi, `itaṉuḷ vatuvaikku ēṟṟa karaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻnta vāṟum, tamar koṭuttavāṟuṅ kāṇka. cuṟṟañ cūḻntu niṟṟalāṉum tamar aṟiya maṇavaṟaic cēṟalāṉum kaḷavāṟ curuṅki niṉṟa nāṇ ciṟantamaiyaip piṉṉart talaimakaṉ viṉava, avaḷ maṟumoḻi koṭātu niṉṟamaiyait talaivaṉ tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. itaṉāṉē itu kaḷaviṉvaḻi nikaḻnta kaṟpāyiṟṟu eṉṟum,' `karaṇattiṉ amaintu muṭinta kālai'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `mukaṉikut toyyeṉa viṟaiñciyōḷē' eṉpatu karaṇattiṉ amaintu muṭintatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
`pukumukam purital'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, akamali yuvakaiyaḷāki mukaṉikut, toyyeṉa viṟaiñci yōḷē' eṉṟatu citaivu piṟarkkiṉmai eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi, itu talaimakaṉ aṟiya meyppaṭṭateṉpatu eṉṟum, `maṟai veḷip paṭutal tamariṟ peṟutal'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `tamartara, ōriṟ kūṭi yuṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul' eṉpatu,  tamariṟ peṟutal eṉṟum uraittaṉar, pērā.
87. pālai
(7viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷun talaimakaṉ iṭaiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tīntayir kaṭainta tiraḷkāl mattam
		kaṉṟuvāy cuvaippa muṉṟil tūṅkum
		paṭalaip pantarp pulvēy kurampai
		nalkūr cīṟūr ellit taṅkik
	5.	kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭumarac cēval
		talaikkural viṭiyal pōki muṉāatu
		kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar kalkeḻu kuṟumpiṉ
		eḻunta taṇṇumai iṭaṅkaṭ pāṇi
		aruñcuram celvōr neñcam tuṇṇeṉak
	10.	kuṉṟucēr kavalai icaikkum attam
		naṉinī ṭuḻantaṉai maṉṉē ataṉāl
		uvaviṉi vāḻiya neñcē maiyaṟa
		vaikucuṭar viḷaṅkum vāṉtōy viyaṉakarc
		cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamulai nalampā rāṭṭit
	15.	tāḻiruṅ kūntaṉaṅ kātali
		nīḷamai vaṉappiṉ tōḷumā raṇaintē.
- maturaip pērālavāyār.
(co-ḷ) 12. vāḻiya neñcē-;
1-4. tīm tayir kaṭainta tiraḷ kāl mattam - iṉiya tayiraik kaṭainta tiraṇṭa taṇṭiṉaiyuṭaiya mattu, kaṉṟu vāy cuvaippa muṉṟil tūṅkum - kaṉṟu taṉ vāyāṟ cuvaittiṭa muṟṟattē toṅkum, paṭalaip pantarp pulvēy kurampai - mara niḻalākiya pantaraiyum pullāl vēyappeṟṟa kuṭilkaḷaiyumuṭaiya, nalkūr cīṟūr - vaṟumaippaṭṭa ciṟiya ūriṉkaṇ, ellit taṅki - iraviṟṟaṅki;
5-6. kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭumarac cēval - ceñcūṭu poruntiya neṟṟiyaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa marattiluḷḷa cēvaliṉ, talaikkural viṭiyaṟ pōki - mutaṟ kural eḻunta viṭiyaṟkālaiyiṟ puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu;
6-11. muṉāatu - paḻamai yuṭaiyatākiya, kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar kalkeḻu kuṟumpiṉ eḻunta - vaṉkaṇmaiyuṭaiya maṟavaratu kaṟkaḷ poruntiya kāṭṭaraṇkaḷil eḻunta, taṇṇumai iṭaṅkaṇ pāṇi - taṇṇumaip paṟaiyiṉ akaṉṟa kaṇṇiṉiṉ ṟeḻum oli, aruñcuram celvōr neñcam tuṇṇeṉa icaikkum - ariya cura neṟiyē celvōr neñcu naṭukkuṟa olikkum, kuṉṟu cērka valai attam - kuṉṟiṉaic cārnta kavartta neṟikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭil, naṉi nīṭu uḻantaṉaimaṉ - nī mikavum neṭitu varuntiṉai;
11-16. ataṉāl - ātalāl, mai aṟa vaiku cuṭar viḷaṅkum vāṉṟōy viyaṉ nakar - iruḷ nīṅka viṭiviḷakku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vāṉ aḷāviya nam periya māḷikaiyiṉkaṇ, tāḻiruṅ kūntal nam kātali cuṇaṅku aṇi vaṉamulai nalam pārāṭṭi - tāḻnta kariya kūntalaiyuṭaiya nam kātaliyiṉ cuṇaṅku aṇinta aḻakiya mulaiyiṉ nalliṉpattaip pārāṭṭi, nīḷ amai vaṉappiṉ tōḷum aṇaintu- nīṇṭa mūṅkil pōṉṟa aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya tōḷiṉaiyum aṇaintu, uva iṉi - ippoḻutu makiḻvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē vāḻiya! nī cīṟūr ellit taṅki viṭiyaṟ pōki attam nīṭu uḻantaṉai maṉṉē; ataṉāl viyaṉakar, nam kātali mulai nalam pārāṭṭi, tōḷum aṇaintu iṉi uva.
(vi-rai) paṭalai - taḻaipparappu. paṭalaip pantar - mara niḻalākiya pantar. viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷkiṉṟāṉākaliṉ ippoḻutu uva eṉṟāṉ. mai - iruḷ. vaiku cuṭar - viṭiyumaḷavum eriyum viḷakku; viṭiviḷakku eṉappaṭum. tōḷumār : ār, acai.
88. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talaimakaṉ ciṟaippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colliyatu.)
		mutaiccuval kalitta mūric centiṉai
		ōṅkuvaṇarp peruṅkural uṇīiya pāṅkarp
		pakuvāyp pallip pāṭōrttuk kuṟukum
		puruvaip paṉṟi varutiṟam nōkkik
	5.	kaṭuṅkaik kāṉavaṉ kaḻutumicaik koḷīiya
		neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkam nōkki vantunam
		naṭuṅkutuyar kaḷainta naṉṉa rāḷaṉ
		ceṉṟaṉaṉ kollō tāṉē kuṉṟat
		tirumpuli tolaitta peruṅkai yāṉaik
	10.	kavuḷmali piḻitarum kāmar kaṭāam
		iruñciṟait toḻuti yārppa yāḻcet
		tiruṅkal viṭaraḷai acuṇam ōrkkum
		kāmpamal iṟumpil pāmpupaṭat tuvaṉṟik
		koṭuviral uḷiyam keṇṭum
	15.	vaṭuvāḻ puṟṟiṉa vaḻakkaru neṟiyē.
- 1īḻattup pūtaṉtēvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. mutaic cuval kalitta - paḻaṅkollaiyākiya mēṭṭu nilattil taḻaitta, mūric centiṉai - koḻutta centiṉaiyiṉ, ōṅku vaṇarp peruṅ kural uṇīiya - uyarnta vaḷainta periya katirai uṇṇa, pakuvāyp palli pāṅkar pāṭu ōrttu - piḷanta vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya palli naṟpakkattē ceyta oliyāya nimittam uṇarntu, kuṟukum - aṇuka varum, puruvaip paṉṟi varutiṟam nōkki - iḷaimai poruntiya paṉṟiyiṉ varum vakaiyiṉai nōkki, kaṭuṅkaik kāṉavaṉ - valiya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tiṉaippuṉaṅ kāppōṉ, kaḻutu micaik koḷīiya - paraṇmītu koḷutti vaitta, neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkam nōkki - nīṇṭa cuṭariṉ oḷiyiṉai nōkki, vantu nam naṭuṅku tuyar kaḷainta naṉṉarāḷaṉ - vantu namatu naṭukkattait tarum tuyariṉai oḻitta naṉmai yāḷaṉākiya nam talaivaṉ;
8-15. kuṉṟattu irumpuli tolaitta peruṅkai yāṉai - kuṉṟiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa periya puliyaik koṉṟa periya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ, kavuḷ malivu iḻitarum kāmar kaṭāam - kaṉṉattiliruntu peruki iḻiyum aḻakiya matanīril, iruñciṟait toḻuti ārppa - kariya ciṟakiṉaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam olikka, yāḻ cettu - ataṉai yāḻicaiyeṉak karuti, iruṅkal viṭar aḷai acuṇam ōrkkum - periya malaiyiṉ piḷappāya kukaiyiluḷḷa acuṇaṅkaḷ uṟṟuk kēṭkum, kāmpu amal iṟumpil - mūṅkil niṟainta ciṟu kāṭṭil, pāmpu paṭa tuvaṉṟi - pāmpu iṟantupaṭa neruṅki, koṭu viral uḷiyam keṇṭum - vaḷainta viraliṉaiyuṭaiya karaṭi tōṇṭum, vaṭu vāḻ puṟṟiṉa - vaṭukkaḷ poruntiya puṟṟukkaḷai yuṭaiyaṉavākiya, vaḻakku aruneṟi - vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyaneṟiyiṉkaṇ, ceṉṟaṉaṉ kol - ceṉṟāṉ kollō!
(muṭipu) paṉṟi varutiṟa ṉōkkik kāṉavaṉ kaḻutumicaik koḷīiya viḷakkam nōkki vantu, nam tuyarkaḷainta naṉṉarāḷaṉ, vaḻakkaru neṟi ceṉṟaṉaṉ kollō!
(vi-rai) pāṭu - ōcai. puruvai - iḷamai. kaḻutu - paraṇ. puliyaik koṉṟa yāṉaiyum, acuṇamum, pāmpiṉ puṟṟum, karaṭiyum uṭaiya koṭu neṟiyiṟ ceṉṟāṉō eṉṟu varuntiṉaḷ eṉka. it taṉmaiyāṉa vaḻiyilē vantu pōkiṉṟārōveṉa iraṅkik kūṟak kēṭṭu varaivāṉāvatu payaṉāka iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ. paṉṟi palli nimittam pārttuc cellutaluṇṭeṉṉuṅ karuttu, "eymmuḷ ḷaṉṉa parūumayi reruttiṟ, ceymmēvaṟ ciṟukaṭ paṉṟi, ōṅkumalai viyaṉpuṉam paṭīiyar vīṅkupoṟi, nūḻai nuḻaiyum poḻutiṟ ṟāḻātu, pāṅkarp pakkattup palli paṭṭeṉa, mella mellap piṟakkē peyarntutaṉ, kallaḷaip paḷḷi vatiyum nāṭa"1 eṉa naṟṟiṇaiyilum vantiruttal aṟintu makiḻaṟkuriyatu.
(u-rai) tiṉai nukartaṟkup paṉṟi nimittam pārttu varukaiyaic ceytēyum, kāṉavaṉ atu varutiṟamaṟintu cuṭar koḷuttiṉāṟ  pōla, tāmum pēṇi vantārē yāyiṉum varavu veḷippaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
89. pālai
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		teṟukatir ñāyiṟu naṭuniṉṟu kāytaliṉ
		uṟupeyal vaṟanta ōṭutēr naṉantalai
		urutteḻu kurala 1kuṭiñaic cēval
		pulcāy viṭarakam pulampa varaiya
	5.	kalleṟi icaiyiṉ iraṭṭum āṅkaṇ
		ciḷvīṭu kaṟaṅkum ciṟiyilai vēlat
		tūḻuṟu viḷaineṟ ṟutirak kāḻiyar
		kavvaip parappiṉ vevvuvarp poḻiyak
		kaḷari paranta kalneṭu maruṅkiṉ
	10.	viḷarūṉ tiṉṟa vīṅkucilai maṟavar
		maipaṭu2 tiṇṭōḷ malira vāṭṭip
		poṟaimali kaḻutai neṭuniṟai taḻīiya
		tiruntuvāḷ vayavar aruntalai tumitta
		paṭupulāk kamaḻum ñāṭpil tuṭiyikut
	15.	taruṅkalan teṟutta perumpukal valattār
		vilkeḻu kuṟumpiṟ kōḷmuṟai pakukkuṅ
		kollai yirumpuṉam neṭiya eṉṉātu
		melleṉ cēvaṭi meliya ēka
		vallunaḷ kollō tāṉē tēmpey
	20.	taḷavuṟu tīmpāl alaippavum uṇṇāḷ
		iṭumaṇaṟ 3pantaruḷ iyalum
		neṭumeṉ paṇaittōḷ māa yōḷē.
-maturaik kāñcip pulavar.
(co-ḷ) 19-22. tēm peytu aḷavuṟu tīmpāl - tēṉaic corintu kalanta iṉiya pālai, alaippavum uṇṇāḷ - alait tūṭṭavum uṇṇāḷāy, iṭu maṇal pantaruḷ iyalum - maṇal parappiya pantaruḷ aṅkum iṅkum ōṭum, neṭumeṉ paṇaittōḷ māayōḷ - nīṇṭa melliya mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉaiyuṭaiya māmai niṟattiṉaḷākiya uṉ makaḷ;
1-2. teṟu katir ñāyiṟu - yāvaṟṟaiyum aḻikkum katirkaḷai yuṭaiya ñāyiṟu, naṭu niṉṟu kāytaliṉ - mutuvēṉiṟ kālattu iṭai nāṭkaḷil niṉṟu erittaliṉ, uṟupeyal vaṟanta - mikka maḻaiyālāya nīr vaṟaṇṭa, ōṭu tēr naṉantalai - pēyttēr ōṭum akaṉṟa iṭattiṉaiyum;
3-5. uruttu eḻu kurala kuṭiñaic cēval - vekuṇṭu eḻukiṉṟa kuraliṉaiyuṭaiya pērāntaic cēval, pul cāy viṭar akam pulampa - puṟkaḷ oḻinta veṭippiṭaṅkaḷ taṉittiṭac ceṉṟu, varaiya kal eṟi icaiyiṉ iraṭṭum - malaiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷ uruṇṭu viḻum pōtu eḻum ōcaipōla māṟi olikkum (iṭattiṉaiyum);
6-9. ciṟi yilai vēlattu - ciṟiya ilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vēla marattiṉ, ūḻ uṟu viḷai neṟṟu utira - muṟaiyāka muṟṟi viḷainta neṟṟu utira, ciḷvīṭu kaṟaṅkum - ciḷvī ṭeṉṉum vaṇṭu olikkum, kavvaip parappiṉ - āravāram poruntiya parappiṉaiyuṭaiyatum, kāḻiyar vevvuvarppu oḻiya - vaṇṇārkaḷ eṭukkum vevviya uvar maṇ oḻiya, kaḷari paranta - kaḷar maṇ parantatumākiya, kalneṭu maruṅkiṉ - kaṟkaḷai iṭaiyē koṇṭa nīṇṭa iṭattiṉaiyum uṭaiyatum;
10-16. viḷar ūṉ tiṉṟa vīṅku cilai maṟavar - koḻuppiṉai yuṭaiya ūṉaittiṉṟa vicaikoṇṭa cilaiyiṉarāya maṟavarkaḷ, maipaṭu tiṇ tōḷ malira - karumai poruntiya valiya tōḷkaḷ pūrikka, poṟai mali kaḻutai neṭunirai taḻīiya - pārammikka kaḻutaikaḷiṉ nīṇṭa niraikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi varum, tiruntu vāḷvayavar vāṭṭi aruntalai tumitta - ceppamuṭaiya vāḷiṉaik koṇṭa vīrarkaḷāya vaṇikarkaḷai vāṭṭi avarkaḷatu ariya talaiyait tuṇitta, paṭu pulā kamaḻum ñāṭpil - mikka pulavu nāṟum pōrkkaḷattē, tuṭi ikuttu - tuṭiyiṉait tāḻak koṭṭi, aruṅkalam teṟutta perum pukal valattar - ariya aṇikalaṉ kaḷait tiṟaiyākap peṟṟuk kuvitta periya pōr viruppiṉaiyuṭaiya veṉṟiyiṉar, vilkeḻu kuṟumpil kōḷ muṟai pakukkum - viṟkaḷ poruntiya araṇiṭattē avaravar koḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟaiyē pirittuk koṭuppatumākiya;
17-19. kollai irumpuṉam neṭiya eṉṉātu - kollaiyiṉai yuṭaiya periya kāṭukaḷai nīṇṭa tūrattaṉa eṉṟeṇṇātu, melleṉ cēvaṭi meliya ēka vallunaḷ kollō - metteṉṟirukkum civanta aṭi varuntac cellaṟku vaṉmaiyuṭaiyaḷ āvaḷō?
(muṭipu) tīmpāl alaippavum uṇṇāḷ pantaruḷ iyalum mā ayōḷ, kollai yirum puṉam neṭiya veṉṉātu cēvaṭi meliya ēka vallunaḷ kollō?
ōṭutēr naṉantalaiyiṉaiyum kalleṟi yicaiyiṉ iraṭṭum iṭattiṉaiyum kaṉṉeṭu maruṅkiṉaiyu muṭaiya kollai irumpuṉam eṉavum, kōḷ muṟai pakukkum kollai irumpuṉam eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-rai) naṭuniṉṟu kāytaliṉ : naṭu - mutuvēṉiṟ kālattiṉ naṭukkūṟākiya nāṭkaḷ. tēr - pēyttēr. uṟupeyal `paranta' eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, peyal (vaṟantu) ōṭu tērāyp paraviya eṉka. ciṟiya ilai - ciṟiyilai eṉa vikārappaṭṭatu, vēlattu : attu, cāriyai. kaṟaṅkum kavvai eṉavum, vāṭṭit tuṇitta eṉavum kūṭṭuka. malira āṭṭi eṉap pirittu, āṭṭi eṉaṟku alaittu eṉṟuraittalu mām. vāḷ vayavar eṉṟataṉāl vāṇikac cāttarum vīrarāyiruppar eṉpatu peṟṟām. avaruṭaṉ pōr purintu koṉṟār eṉpatu tōṉṟavē ñāṭpil eṉṟār. tēṉ kalanta pāl cuvai vīriyaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟakkum eṉpatu `pāloṭu tēṉ kalantaṟṟē'1 eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. tēṉ kalanta pālait tāṉum accuṟutti ūṭṭavum uṇṇātu ōṭupavaḷ eṉa avaḷatu celvac ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟu. celvac ciṟārum ciṟumiyarum uṇavuṇṇa maṟukkuṅkāl, uṇpikkum tāyar avarkaṭku iṉiya kataikaḷ kūṟiyum accuṟuttiyum uṇpittal iyalpu eṉka.
90. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vantu kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkun talaimakaṉait tōḻi 
etirppaṭṭu niṉṟu iṟceṟip paṟivuṟīiyatu.)
		mūttō raṉṉa veṇṭalaip puṇari
		iḷaiyō rāṭum varimaṉai citaikkum
		taḷaiyaviḻ tāḻaik kāṉalam peruntuṟaic
		cilcevit tākiya puṇarcci alareḻa
	5.	ilvayiṉ ceṟittamai aṟiyāy paṉṉāḷ
		varumulai varuttā ampakaṭṭu mārpil
		terumaral uḷḷamoṭu varuntum niṉvayiṉ
		nīṅku keṉṟiyāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō
		aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuḷ cellūrk kuṇāatu
	10.	peruṅkaṭal muḻakkiṟ ṟāki yāṇar
		irumpiṭam paṭutta vaṭuvuṭai mukattar
		2kaṭuṅkaṇ kōcar niyamam āyiṉum
		uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar
		naṟunutal arivai pāciḻai vilaiyē.
- maturai marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-8. talaiva-, mūttōr aṉṉa veṇtalaip puṇari - mutiyōrai otta veḷḷiya talaiyaiyuṭaiya kaṭal, iḷaiyōr āṭum varimaṉai citaikkum - iḷaiya makaḷir viḷaiyāṭum vaṇṭal maṉaiyiṉai aḻikkum, taḷai aviḻ tāḻaik kāṉalam peruntuṟai - kaṭṭaviḻnta maṭalkaḷ poruntiya tāḻaikaḷai yuṭaiya cōlai poruntiya periya kaṭaṟṟuṟaikkaṇṇē, cil cevittākiya puṇarcci - cilar cevippaṭṭa māttiraiyākiya kūṭṭam, alareḻa - alarāki eṅkum parava, ilvayiṉ ceṟittamai aṟiyāy - tāy talaiviyai illiṭattē ceṟittamaiyai aṟiyāyāki, palnāḷ - palanāḷum, am pakaṭṭu mārpiṉ - niṉatu aḻakiya perumaiyaiyuṭaiya mārpiṉāl, varumulai varuttā - vaḷarum mulaiyiṉai yuṭaiyāḷai varutti, terumaral uḷḷamoṭu varuntum - kalaṅkum uḷḷattōṭu varuntā niṟkum, niṉvayiṉ - niṉṉiṭattē, nīṅkuka eṉṟu yāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō - ik kaḷavoḻukkat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvāyāka eṉṟu yāṉ eṅṅaṉam moḻivēṉ;
9-14. naṟu nutal arivai pāciḻai vilai - (ivaḷ tantaimār) naṟiya nutaliṉaiyuṭaiya arivaiyāya ivaḷatu paciya aṇikaṭku vilaiyāka, arun tiṟal kaṭavuḷ cellūrk kuṇāatu - ariya valikoṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya cellūriṉ kīḻppāliṉatāy, peruṅ kaṭal muḻakkiṟṟāki - periya kaṭalpōlum āravārattiṉai yuṭaiyatākiya, irumpu iṭampaṭutta vaṭu uṭai mukattar - paṭaikkalam iṭampaṭac ceytiṭṭa vaṭukkaḷaiyuṭaiya mukattiṉarāya, kaṭuṅkaṭ kōcar - añcāmaiyaiyuṭaiya kōcarkaḷ (vāḻum), yāṇar niyamam āyiṉum - putu varuvāyaiyuṭaiya niyamam eṉṉum ūriṉaik koṭuppiṉum, uṟum eṉak koḷḷunar allar - amaiyum eṉak koḷvār allar.
(muṭipu) talaiva! kāṉalam peruntuṟai cil cevittākiya puṇarcci alareḻa iṟ ceṟittamai aṟiyāy, paṉṉāḷ ivaḷai varutti varuntum niṉvayiṉ, (ik kaḷavoḻukkam) nīṅkuka eṉṟu yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō! arivai pāciḻai vilai kōcar niyamamāyiṉum uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar.
(vi-rai) `mūttōraṉṉa citaikkum' eṉṟatu puṇari uruviṉāl mutiyōrai yottum ciṟiyariṉ ceykai yuṭaiya tāyiṟṟeṉa nayampaṭak kūṟiyavāṟu. varumulai, irumpu eṉpaṉa ākupeyar. mārpu kāraṇamāka varuntutaliṉ mārpiṉ varuttā eṉṟāḷ. em varutta maṟintu nīyē varaitaṟku muyalutal muṟaimai yeṉpāḷ, yāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō eṉṟāḷ. āki eṉṟataṉai ākiya eṉat tirikka. yāṇar niyamam eṉak kūṭṭuka. talaivaṉ talaiviyaip poṉṉaṇintu varaital vaḻakkātaliṉ arivai pāciḻai vilai eṉṟāḷ. uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar eṉṟamaiyāl, poruḷ mikak koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉat talaiviyai arumai pārāṭṭiya vāṟu. aṉṟi, vilai niyamam koṭuppiṉum koḷḷārātalāl avarkaḷ aṉpiṟkum uriyaiyākuka eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām.
(u-ṟai) mūttōr iḷaiyōrai varuttāraṉṟē, atu ceyyātu puṇari iḷaiyōr maṉai citaittāṟpōla, periya aṟivuṭaiya nīr ciṟiyēmākiya eṅkaḷai varuttāniṉṟīr eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, `poruḷ mikak koṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉṟatu' eṉṟaṉar, nac.
91. pālai
(piriviṭai melinta talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		viḷaṅkupakal utaviya palkatir ñāyiṟu
		vaḷaṅkeḻu māmalai payaṅkeṭat teṟutaliṉ
		aruvi yāṉṟa peruvarai maruṅkil
		cūrccuṉai tuḻaii nīrppayaṅ kāṇātu
	5.	pāci tiṉṟa paiṅkaṇ yāṉai
		ōypacip piṭiyō ṭorutiṟaṉ oṭuṅka
		vēykaṇ ṇuṭainta veyilavir naṉantalai
		arumporuḷ vēṭkaiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum
		perumpē raṉpiṉar tōḻi iruṅkēḻ
	10.	iralai cēkkum paraluyar patukkaik
		kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar kaḷavuḻa veḻunta
		neṭuṅkāl āciṉi oṭuṅkāṭ ṭumpar
		vicipiṇi muḻaviṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ kāppap
		paciyeṉa vaṟiyāp paṇaipayil irukkait
	15.	taṭamarup perumai tāmarai muṉaiyiṉ
		muṭamutir palaviṉ koḻuniḻal vatiyum
		kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum taviralar
		maṭamāṉ nōkkiniṉ māṇalam maṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, viḷaṅku pakal utaviya pal katir ñāyiṟu- ulakam viḷaṅkaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya pakaliṉait tanta pala katir
kaḷaiyuṭaiya ñāyiṟu, vaḷam keḻu māmalai payam keṭat teṟutaliṉ- vaḷam vāynta periya malaiyiṉ payaṉ keṭṭoḻiyak kāytaliṉ, aruvi āṉṟa peruvarai maruṅkiṉ - aruvi illaiyāṉa periya pakkamalaiyiṉiṭattu, cūrccuṉai tuḻaii nīrppayam kāṇātu, teyvattiṉaiyuṭaiya cuṉaiyaittuḻāvi nīrākiya payaṉaik kāṇātu, pāci tiṉṟa paiṅkaṇ yāṉai - pāciyiṉait tiṉṟa paciya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya āṇ yāṉai, ōy pacip piṭiyoṭu oru tiṟaṉ oṭuṅka - ayarviṉait tarum paciṉaiyuṭaiya peṇ yāṉaiyiṉuṭaṉ  oru pakkattē oṭuṅkik kiṭakka, vēy kaṇ uṭainta veyil avir naṉantalai - mūṅkilkaḷ kaṇukkaḷ piḷanta veyil viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa pālai nilattē, arum poruḷ vēṭkaiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉar āyiṉum - ariya poruḷ viruppāl nam talaivar nammaip pirintu ceṉṟā rāyiṉum, perum pēraṉpiṉar - mikap periya aṉpiṉarākaliṉ;
9-18. iru kēḻ iralai cēkkum paral uyar patukkai - kariya niṟamuṭaiya āṇmāṉkaḷ taṅkum paralaiyuṭaiya uyarnta kaṟkuviyalil, kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar kaḷavu uḻavu eḻunta - vaṉkaṇmaiyiṉai yuṭaiya maḻavarkaḷ kaḷavākiya uḻaviṟku eḻum iṭamākiya, neṭuṅkāl āciṉi oṭuṅkāṭṭu umpar - nīṇṭa aṭiyai yuṭaiya īrappalā maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya oṭuṅkāṭu eṉṉum ūrkku appāl, vicipiṇi muḻaviṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ  kāppa - iṟukap piṇitta muḻaviṉai yuṭaiya kuṭṭuvaṉ eṉpāṉ purattalāl, paci eṉa aṟiyā paṇai payil irukkai - paci eṉalai aṟiyāta marutavaḷam mikka ūrkaḷai yuṭaiya, taṭa maruppu erumai tāmarai muṉaiyiṉ - vaḷainta kompiṉaiyuṭaiya erumai (mēynta) tāmaraiyai veṟukku māyiṉ, muṭam mutir palaviṉ koḻu niḻal vatiyum - vaḷaivu mikka palāviṉatu koḻuviya niḻaṟkaṇṇē taṅkum, kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum - kuṭanāṭṭiṉaiyē peṟuvarāyiṉum, maṭamāṉ nōkki - iḷamai vāynta māṉ pōlum pārvaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyāy, niṉ māṇ nalam maṟantu taviralar - niṉatu māṇpuṟṟa nalattiṉai maṟantu āṅkut taṅkuvārallar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! māṉōkki! nam talaivar veyilavir naṉantalai arum poruḷ vēṭkaiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉarāyiṉum perum pēraṉpiṉarākaliṉ kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum niṉ māṇalam maṟantu āṅkut taviralar.
(vi-rai) mārikkaṇ uṇṭa nīraik kōṭaiyil umiḻum iyalpiṉatākiya malaiyiṉ payaṉ keṭat teṟum eṉa vēṉiliṉ vemmai mikuti kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. āṉṟa - illaiyāṉa; akaṉṟa eṉpataṉ marūu. cuṉaikkaṇ cūr uṟaiyum eṉpataṉai, `cuṉaiyuṟaiyum, cūrmakaḷ mātō eṉṉumeṉ neñcē'1 eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. cūr, accamumām. yāṉai pāciyait tiṉṟamai nīrppacai karuti yeṉka. oṭu - oruvakai maram eṉpatum, ataṉmuṉ valleḻuttuvariṉ, melimikum eṉpatum, `utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē'2 `oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē' eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. īṇṭu oṭu maraṅkaḷ cūḻntamaiyiṉ oṭuṅkāṭu eṉappaṭṭa teṉka. irukkaiyaiyuṭaiya kuṭanāṭu eṉka. erumai tāmarai  muṉaiyiṉ palaviṉ niḻalil vatiyum eṉṟatu; marutattiṟkum kuṟiñcikkum aṇimai kūṟiyapaṭiyām. erumai, tāmarai yeṉṉum marutak karupporuḷkaḷ pālaikkaṇ vantaṉa; eṉṉai? `ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum, annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum, vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum'1 eṉpavākaliṉ eṉka.
92. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ceṉṟu talaimakaḷaik kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkun talaimakaṉaip 
pakaṟkuṟi nērnta vāyppāṭṭāl tōḻi varaivukaṭāyatu.)
		neṭumalai yaṭukkam kaṇkeṭa miṉṉip
		paṭumaḻai poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		kuñcaram  naṭuṅkat tākkik koṭuvaric
		ceṅkaṇ irumpuli kuḻumuñ cāral
	5.	vāral vāḻiyar aiya nēriṟai
		neṭumeṉ paṇaittōḷ ivaḷum yāṉum
		kāval kaṇṇiṉam tiṉaiyē nāḷai
		mantiyum aṟiyā marampayil iṟumpiṉ
		oṇceṅ kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ
	10.	taṇpal aruvit tāḻnīr oruciṟai
		urumuccivan teṟinta uraṉaḻi pāmpiṉ
		tirumaṇi viḷakkiṟ peṟukuvai
		iruṇmeṉ kūntal ēmuṟu tuyilē.
- maturaip pālāciriyar naṟṟāmaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. aiya-, vāḻiyar-, neṭumalai aṭukkam kaṇkeṭa miṉṉi - uyarnta malaiyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil kaṇṇoḷi keṭa miṉṉi, paṭu maḻai poḻinta pālnāḷ kaṅkul - mikka maḻai corinta pāti yiravākiya iruḷil, kuñcaram naṭuṅkat tākki - yāṉaiyai naṭuṅkumpaṭi tākki, koṭuvari ceṅkaṇ irumpuli kuḻumum cāral- vaḷainta kōṭukaḷaiyum civanta kaṇṇiṉaiyumuṭaiya periya puli muḻaṅkum malaiccāralilē, vāral - nī vārātiruppāyāka;
5-7. nēr iṟai neṭu meṉ paṇaittōḷ ivaḷum yāṉum - melliya muṉ kaiyiṉaiyum nīṇṭa melliya mūṅkilai yotta tōḷiṉaiyumuṭaiya ittalaiviyum yāṉum, kāval kaṇṇiṉam tiṉai - tiṉaippuṉaṅ kāttalaik karutiyuḷḷēm;
7-13. nāḷai - nāḷaip pakaṟkaṇ, mantiyum aṟiyā maram payil iṟumpiṉ - mantikaḷum ēṟi aṟiyamāṭṭāta maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭṭil, oṇ ceṅkāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ - oḷḷiya ceṅkāttaḷ malarnta avviṭattē, taṇ pal aruvi tāḻ nīr oru ciṟai - palavāya taṇṇiya aruvikaḷ vīḻum cuṉaiyiṉ orupuṟam, urumu civantu eṟinta uraṉ aḻi pāmpiṉ tirumaṇi viḷakkil - iṭi ciṉantu tākkaliṉ vali yaḻinta pāmpiṉatu aḻakiya talaimaṇiyākiya viḷakkilē, iruḷ mel kūntal ēm uṟu tuyil - talaiviyiṉ iruṇṭa melliya kūntalakattu iṉpam uṟum tuyilai, peṟu kuvai - nī aṭaivāy.
(muṭipu) aiya! vāḻiyar! maḻai poḻinta pāṉāṭkaṅkul kuñcaram tākkip puli kuḻumum cāral vāral; ivaḷum yāṉum tiṉai kāval kaṇṇiṉam; nāḷai iṟumpil kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ tāḻ nīr oru ciṟai pāmpiṉ tirumaṇi viḷakkil kūntal ēmuṟu tuyil peṟukuvai.
(vi-rai) nāḷai kāval kaṇṇiṉam eṉalumām. kōṭu vāḻ kuraṅku eṉappaṭuvatum, maramēṟun toḻiliṟ ciṟantatumākiya mantiyum ēṟi aṟiyāta eṉa marattiṉ uyarcci kūṟiyavāṟu. ummai ciṟappummai. `kuraṅkaṟi vārā marampayi liṟumpil'1 eṉap piṉṉar innūliṉum, `mantiyu maṟiyā marampayi loruciṟai,'2 `mantiyu maṟiyā maraṉpayi laṭukkattu'3 eṉap piṟanūlkaḷilum varutalum kāṇka. iṉi manti, eṉpataṟku ñāyiṟu eṉap poruḷ koṇṭu, parutiyiṉ katir nuḻaiyalākātavāṟu maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭu eṉṉalumām. pakaṟkuṟi nērntāḷpōṟ kūṟiṉum, kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ aruvit tāḻnīr oruciṟai eṉṟamaiyiṉ, pūkkoyya varuvārālum aruviyāṭa varuvārālum pakaṟkuṟiyum ariteṉpatu pulappaṭutti varaivu kaṭāyiṉā ḷāyiṟṟu.
93. pālai
(viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷaluṟun talaimakaṉ iṭaiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum kiḷaiñar āravum
		kēḷal kēḷir keḻīiyiṉar oḻukavum
		āḷviṉaik ketiriya ūkkamoṭu pukalciṟan
		tāraṅ kaṇṇi aṭupōrc cōḻar
	5.	aṟaṅkeḻu nallavai uṟantai aṉṉa
		peṟalaru naṉkalaṉ eyti nāṭum
		ceyalaruñ ceyviṉai muṟṟiṉa māyiṉ
		araṇpala kaṭanta muraṇkoḷ tāṉai
		vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti kūṭal
	10.	nāḷaṅ kāṭi nāṟum naṟunutal
		nīḷiruṅ kūntal māa yōḷoṭu
		varaikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar
		nuraimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkkai
		nivanta paḷḷi neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkattu
	15.	nalaṅkēḻ ākam pūṇvaṭup poṟippa
		muyaṅkukañ ceṉmō neñcē varinutal
		vayantikaḻ pimiḻtarum vāypuku kaṭāttu
		mīḷi moympoṭu nilaṉeṟiyāk kuṟuki
		āḷkōṭ piḻaiyā añcuvaru taṭakkaik
	20.	kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉai neṭuntērk kōtai
		tirumā viyaṉakark karuvūr muṉṟuṟait
		teṇṇīr uyarkaraik kavaiiya
		taṇṇāṉ porunai maṇaliṉum palavē.
- kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 16. neñcē-;
1-3. kēḷ kēṭu ūṉṟavum - uṟaviṉaraik kēṭṭiṉai nīkkit tāṅkavum, kiḷaiñar āravum - kiḷaikaḷāyuḷḷār uṇṇavum, kēḷal kēḷir keḻiiyiṉar oḻukavum - notumalāḷar keḻutakaiyiṉarāki oḻukavum vēṇṭi, āḷviṉaikku etiriya ūk kamoṭu - poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟcikku ēṟṟatāya ūkkaṅkoṇṭu, pukal ciṟantu - viruppam mikku;
4-7. āram kaṇṇi aṭupōrc cōḻar - āttimālai yaṇinta aṭum pōriṉaiyuṭaiya cōḻaratu, aṟamkeḻu nallavai uṟantai aṉṉa - aṟam poruntiya nalla avaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya uṟaiyūrai yotta, peṟal arum naṉkalaṉ eyti - peṟutaṟku ariya nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷai eyti, nāṭum ceyal arum ceyviṉai muṟṟiṉam āyiṉ - yāvarum virumpum ceytaṟkariya poruḷcey viṉaiyiṉai nām muṭittaṉam ātaliṉ;
8-11. araṇ pala kaṭanta muraṇ koḷ tāṉai - pakaivar araṇkaḷ palavaṟṟai veṉṟa māṟupāṭu koṇṭa tāṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti - vāṭāta vēppa mālaiyiṉait taritta pāṇṭiyaṉatu, kūṭal nāḷ aṅkāṭi nāṟum - maturaiyiṉ kālaik kaṭai vītiyeṉa maṇakkum, naṟunutal nīḷiruḷ kūntal māayōḷoṭu - naṟiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyum nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliṉaiyu muṭaiya māmai niṟattāḷāya nam talaiviyuṭaṉ;
12-15. varai kuyiṉṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar - malaiyaik kuṭain tiyaṟṟiyatai yotta vāṉai aḷāviya nīṇṭa maṉaiyiṉ kaṇṇē, nurai mukantaṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkkai - nuraiyai mukantu vaittāṟ pōṉṟa melliya pūkkaḷālāya paṭukkaiyaiyuṭaiya, nivanta paḷḷi neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkattu - ōṅkiya kaṭṭiliṉiṭattu neṭiya viḷakkiṉ oḷiyilē, nalamkēḻ ākam pūṇ vaṭu poṟippa - naṉmai poruntiya nam mārpilē talaiviyiṉ mārpakattup pūṇkaḷ vaṭukkaḷaic ceyya;
16-23. varinutal - vari poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyum, vayam tikaḻpu imiḻtarum - vali viḷaṅkutaliṉ muḻaṅkum, vāy puku kaṭāttu - vāyiṟ pukum matattiṉaiyum, mīḷi moympoṭu - kūṟṟuvaṉai yotta valiyuṭaṉ, nilaṉ eṟiyā - nilattiṉkaṇṇē curuṭṭi eṟintu, kuṟuki āḷ kōḷ piḻaiyā añcuvaru taṭakkai - neruṅki vantu āṭkaḷaik kollutalait tappāta accam varukiṉṟa periya kaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, kaṭum pakaṭṭu yāṉai - kaṭiya periya yāṉaip paṭaiyiṉaiyum, neṭun tērk kōtai - neṭiya tērppaṭaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya cēraṉatu, tirumā viyaṉ nakark karuvūr muṉṟuṟai - celvam mikka ciṟanta akaṉṟa nakarākiya karuvūriṉ tuṟaimuṉ, teḷ nīr - teḷinta nīriṉaiyuṭaiya, taṇ āṉ porunai-  taṇṇiya āṉ porunai eṉṉum āṟṟiṉ, uyarkarai kuvaiiya - uyarnta karaik kaṇ kuvintuḷa, maṇaliṉum pala - maṇaliṉum palavāka;
16. muyaṅkukam ceṉmō - nām muyaṅkuvōm varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! ūṉṟavum, āravum, oḻukavum vēṇṭi, ūkkamoṭu pukal ciṟantu, uṟantai yaṉṉa naṉkalaṉ eytic ceyviṉai muṟṟiṉam ātalāl, māyōḷoṭu meṉpūñ cēkkai nivanta paḷḷiyil, cuṭar viḷakkattu, āṉ porunai maṇaliṉum pala ākam vaṭup poṟippa muyaṅkukam ceṉmō.
(vi-rai) kēḷ - kēḷir eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ūṉṟal - tāṅkal. vaṟumaiyuṟṟa kiḷaiñar āravum eṉka. kēḷal kēḷir - ētilār. avar aṉpuṭaiyarāy oḻukavum eṉṟapaṭi. `ārum vetirum cārum pīrum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, meypeṟa eṉṟa ilēcāl `āraṅkaṇṇi' eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. uṟaiyūr avaiyil aṟam nilai peṟṟa teṉpataṉai, `maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟantai yavaiyat, taṟaṅkeṭa vaṟiyā tāṅku'2 `maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟantai avaiyat, taṟamniṉṟu nilaiyiṟ ṟākaliṉ'1 eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāṉum aṟika. muṟṟi eytiṉam eṉṟu māṟutalumām. `eytiya' eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, eytutaṟkeṉṟu uraippāru muḷar. āyiṉ - ātalāl. mīḷi - kūṟṟuvaṉ ātalai, `mīḷi yuṭampiṭit taṭakkai'2 eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraitta uraiyāṉ uṇarka. mīḷi - maṟamumām. mataṅ koṇṭa yāṉai kaiyai nilattiṉkaṇ eṟintu varum eṉpatu, `irumpiṇart taṭakkai yirunilam cērttic, ciṉantikaḻ kaṭāañ cerukki maraṅkolpu, maiyal vēḻam taṭaṅkaliṉ etirtara'3 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. karuvūr mēṟkaṭaṟ pakkattatu eṉpārum, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ ellaiyai aṭuttuḷḷa koṅku
nāṭṭuk karuvūrē eṉpārum eṉa, irutiṟappaṭuvar varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar. ic ceyyuḷuḷ cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, cērar eṉṉum mūvēntarum, avar talainakarākiya uṟantai, kūṭal, karuvūr eṉpaṉavum vantuḷḷamai aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `mēviya ciṟappiṉ'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum...... pukal ciṟantu' eṉpatu, `vaṇikar poruḷ vayiṟ pirintavāṟu' eṉṟum, `puṟattiṇai maruṅkiṉ'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ippāṭṭu, `puṟattiṇait talaivar palarāy akattiṇaikkaṇ aḷavi vantatu' eṉṟum, `karaṇattiṉamaintu'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum kiḷaiñarāravum' eṉpatu, `avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappu' eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
`piṟappē kuṭimai' eṉṉum cūttirattu, `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum ...... pukal ciṟantu'7 eṉpuḻi iṉṉa kāraṇattiṟ pirintu pōntu viṉai muṭittaṉamāyiṉum, avaḷai `muyaṅkukam ceṉmō' eṉṟamaiyiṉ taṉ āḷviṉaikkut takka peṇmaiyāṉ avaḷ āṟṟiyiruntāḷ eṉpatūum karutiya karuttiṉāṟ kāmak kuṟippup piṟantamaiyiṉ, aḵtu āṇmaiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum, `maṟai veḷippaṭuttalum,'8 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `nalaṅkē ḻākam.... ceṉmō neñcē' eṉpatu malital eṉavum kūṟuvar, pērā.
94. mullai
(viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷun talaimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu. talaimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuc coṟṟatūu mām.)
		tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya
		kuṟaiyilai mucuṇṭai veṇpūk kuḻaiya
		9vāṉeṉap pūtta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		maṟitturūut tokutta paṟippuṟa iṭaiyaṉ
	5.	taṇkamaḻ mullai tōṉṟiyoṭu viraii
		vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ
		aitupaṭu koḷḷi aṅkai kāyak
		kuṟunari yuḷampuṅ kūriruḷ neṭuviḷi
		ciṟukaṭ paṉṟip perunirai kaṭiya
	10.	mutaippuṉaṅ kāvalar niṉaintirun tūtum
		karuṅkōṭ ṭōcaiyo ṭoruṅkuvan ticaikkum
		vaṉpulak kāṭṭunāṭ ṭatuvē aṉpukalan
		tārvañ ciṟanta cāyal
		irumpal kūntal tiruntiḻai yūrē.
- naṉpalūrc ciṟumētāviyār.
(co-ḷ) 12-14. aṉpu kalantu ārvam ciṟanta - aṉpāl uḷḷam kalantu viruppam mikka, cāyal - meṉmait taṉmaiyaiyum, irum pal kūntal - kariya palavākiya kūntaliṉaiyum uṭaiya, tiruntu iḻai - tiruntiya iḻaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya talaiviyiṉatu, ūr - ūrāṉatu;
1-8. tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya kuvaiilai mucuṇṭai veṇpū - tēṉ aṭai poruntiya uccimalaiyiṉ pakkaliṟ ceṟinta kuvinta ilaikaḷaiyuṭaiya mucuṇṭaiyiṉ veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ, vāṉ eṉak kuḻaiyap pūtta pāl nāḷ kaṅkul - vāṉamāṉatu mīṉaippūttatu pōlak  kuḻaintiṭap pūtta naṭuiraviṉ iruḷilē, maṟit turūu tokutta paṟippuṟa iṭaiyaṉ - āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷaic cērat tokuttu vaittuḷa ōlaippāyai mutukiṟ koṇṭa iṭaiyaṉ, taṇkamaḻ mullai tōṉṟiyoṭu viraii - taṇṇiya kamaḻum mullaip pūviṉait tōṉṟip pūvuṭaṉ iṇaittu, vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ - vaṇṭukaḷ moykkat toṭutta nīr oḻukukiṉṟa kaṇṇiyaṉāy, aitu paṭu koḷḷi aṅkai kāya - meṉmai vāynta koḷḷiyiṉ tīyil akaṅkai kāyntiṭa, kūr iruḷ kuṟu nari uḷampum neṭuviḷi - mikka iruḷil kuṟunari
kaḷai alaittōṭṭum nīṇṭa oli;
9-12. ciṟu kaṇ paṉṟip perunirai kaṭiya - ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉai yuṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉ peruṅkūṭṭattai ōṭṭaṟku, mutaippuṉaṅ kāvalar - muṟṟiya tiṉaippuṉam kāttiruppōr, niṉaittiruntu ūtum karuṅkōṭṭu ōcaiyōṭu - avai varuṅkālattai eṇṇiyiruntu ūtum periya kompiṉ ōcaiyoṭu, oruṅkuvan ticaikkum - oru cēra vantolikkum, vaṉpulak kāṭṭu nāṭṭatuvē - vaṉpulamākiya kāṭṭiṉ akattatākiya nāṭṭiṉiṭattatākum;(pāka! viraintu tēriṉaic celuttuvāyāka.)
(muṭipu) (pāka!) tiruntiḻaiyūr, kaṅkulil iṭaiyaṉ kuṟu nari uḷampum neṭuviḷi puṉaṅ kāvalar ūtum kōṭṭōcai yoṭu oruṅkuvan ticaikkum kāṭṭu nāṭṭatuvē. (ākaliṉ tērai viraintu celuttuvāyāka.)
(vi-rai) tēm - tēṉ; tēṉiṟāl. kuḻaital - taḻaittal. vāṉ eṉap pūtta - vāṉ mīṉaip pūttāṟpōlat tōṉṟumāṟu pūtta eṉṟu viritturaikka. turūu - cemmaṟiyāṭu. maṟi - kuṭṭi. paṟi - maḻaiyait taṭuppataṟku olaippāyāl vaḷaittu iyaṟṟiya kuṭalai. tokutta iṭaiyaṉ eṉa iyaiyum. koḷḷiyil kaiyaik kāyttik koṇṭu nariyai ōṭṭuvaṉ eṉka. kuṟu nari - āṭṭu maṟiyai vauvutaṟku varuvatu. kuṟu nari, neṭu viḷi, ciṟu kaṇ, perunirai eṉpaṉa muraṇ eṉṉum aṇi. vaṉpulam - kuṟiñciyum mullaiyum. aṉpu - oruvarai yoruvar iṉṟiyamaiyāmai. ārvam - viruppam.
talaimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuc coṟṟatu eṉpatu tuṟaiyāyiṉ kaḷavuk kālattup pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattut talaivaṉ pāṅkaṟkut talaiviyiṉ iṭaṅ kūṟutalām. itu mullaiyiṟ kaḷavu nikaḻnta
tātaliṉ, tiṇai mayakkamām, `tiṇaimayak kuṟutalum kaṭinilai yilavē'1 eṉpavākaliṉ.
(mē-ḷ) `puṇartal pirital'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, `iruttal nimittamām; ikkālam varuntuṇaiyum āṟṟiṉāḷ eṉat tāṉ varuntutaliṉ' eṉṟār, nac.
95. pālai
(pōkkuṭaṉ paṭṭa talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		paipayap 3pacantaṉṟu nutaluñ cāay
		aitā kiṉṟeṉ taḷirpurai mēṉiyum
		palarum aṟiyat tikaḻtarum avalamum
		uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allatai niṉaiyiṉ
	5.	evaṉō vāḻi tōḻi porikāl
		pokuṭṭarai yiruppaik kuvikulaik kaḻaṉṟa
		āli yoppiṉ tūmpuṭait tiraḷvī
		āṟucel vampalar nīḷiṭai yaḻuṅka
		īṉal eṇkiṉ iruṅkiḷai kavarum
	10.	4curampala kaṭantōrk kiraṅkupa eṉṉār
		kauvai mēvala rāki ivvūr
		niraiyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟupa
		puraiya allaeṉ makaṭkeṉap paraii
		nammuṇarn tāṟiya koḷkai
	15.	aṉṉai muṉṉaryām eṉṉitaṟ paṭalē.
- ōrōṭōkattuk kantarattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi vāḻi-, pai payap pacantaṉṟu nutalum - eṉ neṟṟiyum mella mellap pacantatu, cāay aitu ākiṉṟu eṉ taḷir purai mēṉiyum - taḷirai yotta eṉ mēṉiyum nuṇuki mellitākiṉṟatu, palarum aṟiyat tikaḻtarum avalamum - eṉ tuyaramum palarum aṟiyumpaṭi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum, uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allatai - ivai eṉ uyiraik koṇṭu pōvatallatu, niṉaiyiṉ evaṉō - āyumiṭattu vēṟu eṉ ceyvaṉa;
5-10. pori kāl pokuṭṭu arai iruppai - porinta aṭiyiṉaiyum koṭṭaikaḷaiyuṭaiya araiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya iruppaiyiṉatu, kuvi kulaik kaḻaṉṟa - kuvinta kulaiyiṉiṉṟum kaḻaṉṟa,  āli oppiṉ tūmpu uṭait tiraḷ vī - paṉikkaṭṭi pōlum uṭṭuḷai yiṉaiyuṭaiya tiraṇṭa pūkkaḷai, āṟu cel vampalar nīḷiṭai aḻuṅka - vaḻic cellum putiyar annīṇṭa neṟiyiṭattē añcip pōkkiṉait tavira, īṉal eṇkiṉ iruṅkiḷai kavarum - īṉṟa karaṭi kaḷiṉ peruṅ kūṭṭam kavarntuṇṇukiṉṟa, curam pala kaṭantōrkku iraṅkupa eṉṉār - pala curaṅkaḷaiyum kaṭantu ceṉṟa tam talaivar poruṭṭut talaiviyar iraṅkuvarātal iyalpeṉṟu niṉaiyārāy;
11-12. kauvai mēvalar āki - alar tūṟṟalē virumpuvārāki, ivvūr niraiyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟuva - ivvūriluḷḷa iḻinta iyalpuṭaiya peṇṭir kūṟuvaṉavāya iṉṉāta coṟkaḷ;
13-15. puraiya alla eṉ makaṭku eṉa - eṉ makaḷukkup poruntuvaṉa alla eṉṟu, paraii - teyvattaip paravi, nam uṇarntu - namatu kaḷavoḻukkattiṉai uṇarntu vaittu, āṟiya koḷkai aṉṉai muṉṉar - amaitiyuṟṟirukkum koḷkai yiṉaiyuṭaiya nam aṉṉai muṉ, yām eṉitaṟ paṭal - yām ik kaḷavoḻukkattiṟ paṭṭu oḻukal eṅṅaṉam iyalum?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! eṉ nutalum pacantaṉṟu; mēṉiyum aitākiṉṟu; avalamum tikaḻtarum; (ivai) uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allatai niṉaiyiṉ evaṉō? curam pala kaṭantōrkku iraṅkupa eṉṉār, kauvai mēvalarāki niraiyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟupa, eṉ makaṭkup puraiya allaveṉa, paraii āṟiya koḷkai aṉṉai muṉṉar yām itaṟpaṭal eṉ?
(vi-rai) pacanta eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku, pacanta nutalum aitākiṉṟa mēṉiyum tikaḻtarum avalamum eṉap peyareccat toṭarkaḷākak koḷka. itaṟku ākiṉṟu eṉpatu vikāram. allatai - allatu : īṟu tirintatu. pacappu mutaliyaṉa uyir koṇṭē kaḻiyum eṉpatu karuttu. vīyai eṇkiṉ kiḷai kavarum eṉka. īṉṟa karaṭikaḷ cīṟum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ vampalar aḻuṅkuvar eṉka. puraiya alla - uyarvāvaṉa alla eṉalumām. paraii - teyvattaip parāvi. aṉṉai, taṉ makaḷ paḻiyoṭu paṭāta iṉpa vāḻvu peṟal vēṇṭumeṉat teyvattaip paraviṉaḷ. `namatu āṟṟā nilaimai yuṇarntu nammaic ciṉavātu amaintuḷḷa aṉṉaiyiṉ muṉpu nām mēlum ivvāṟu oḻukutal poruntātu eṉṟāḷ.'
(mē-ḷ) `eñci yōrkkum eñcuta lilavē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu pōkkuṭaṉ paṭṭamai talaivi tōḻik kuraittatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
96. marutam
(tōḻi vāyiṉ maṟuttatu)
		naṟavuṇ maṇṭai nuṭakkaliṉ iṟavukalittup
		pūṭṭaṟu villil kūṭṭumutal teṟikkum
		paḻaṉap poykai aṭaikaraip pirampiṉ
		aravāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅkoṭi
	5.	aruvi āmpal akalaṭai tuṭakki
		acaivaral vāṭai tūkkaliṉ ūtulai
		vicaivāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻuṅ
		kaḻaṉiyam paṭappaik kāñci yūra
		oṇtoṭi yāyat tuḷḷunī nayantu
	10.	koṇṭaṉai yeṉpavōr kuṟumakaḷ atuvē
		cempoṟ cilampiṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ
		aṅkaluḻ māmai2 yaḵtai tantai
		aṇṇal yāṉai aṭupōrc cōḻar
		veṇṇel vaippiṟ paruvūrp paṟantalai
	15.	iruperu vēntarum 3porutukaḷat toḻiya
		oḷiṟuvāḷ nallamark kaṭanta ñāṉṟaik
		kaḷiṟukavar kampalai pōla
		alarā kiṉṟatu palarvāyp paṭṭē.
-marutam pāṭiya iḷaṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-8. naṟavu uṇ maṇṭai nuṭakkaliṉ iṟavu kalittu - kaḷḷuṇṭa kalam kaḻuvap paṭutaliṉ (annīraiyuṇṭa) iṟā mīṉ cerukki, pūṭṭu aṟu villiṉ kūṭṭu mutal teṟikkum - pūṭṭiya nāṇ aṟṟa vil teṟippatupōla neṟ kūṭukaḷiṉ aṭikaḷiṟ ṟuḷḷi viḻum iṭamākiya, paḻaṉap poykai aṭaikarai - marutanilattup poykaiyiṉ aṭaikaraiyiluḷḷa, pirampiṉ ara vāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅ koṭi - pirampiṉatu arattiṉ vāy pōṉṟa aḻakiya muṭkaḷaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa koṭi, aruvi āmpal akal aṭai tuṭakki - nīrkkuṟai vaṟṟa āmpalatu akaṉṟa ilaiyiṉaic cuṟṟiṭa (avvilaiyai), acai varal vāṭai tūkkaliṉ - acaintuvarum vāṭaikkāṟṟu (viṭṭu viṭṭup pukuntu) acaittaliṉ, ūtu ulai vicai vāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻum - (avvilai) ūtappeṟum kollaṉ ulaikkaḷattu vicaittu iḻuttuviṭum turuttiyaip pōlap puṭaittuc curuṅkum, kaḻaṉi am paṭappaik kāñci ūra - vayalkaḷaiyum tōṭṭaṅkaḷaiyu muṭaiya kāñci maraṅkaḷ mikka ūraiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!,
9-10. oṇtoṭi āyattuḷḷum - oḷḷiya toṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya parattaiyar kūṭṭattiṉuḷḷum, nī ōr kuṟumakaḷ nayantu koṇṭaṉai eṉpa - nī oru iḷaiya makaḷai virumpi maṇantaṉai eṉṟu ūrār kūṟuvārāyiṉar;
