10-18. atuvē - ak kūṟṟu, cempoṉ cilampiṉ - civanta poṉṉālāya cilampiṉaiyum, ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ - neruṅkiya tuṭaiyiṉaiyum, am kaluḻ māmai - aḻaku oḻukum māmai niṟattiṉaiyum uṭaiya, aḵtai tantai - aḵtai eṉpāṭkut tantaiyarākiya, aṇṇal yāṉai aṭupōrc cōḻar - perumai taṅkiya yāṉaiyaiyum vellum pōriṉaiyumuṭaiya cōḻar, veṇṇel vaippil paruvūrp paṟantalai - veṇṇel viḷaiyum iṭaṅkaḷai yuṭaiya paruvūrp pōrk kaḷattē, iru peru vēntarum porutu kaḷattu oḻiya - tammoṭu poruta ēṉaic cērar pāṇṭiyarāya iruperu vēntarkaḷum pōr ceytu kaḷattiṉiṭattē paṭṭoḻiya, oḷiṟu vāḷ nal amar kaṭanta ñāṉṟai - viḷaṅkum vāḷiṉāṟ ceyyum nalla pōriṉai veṉṟa poḻutu, kaḷiṟu kavar kampalai pōla - ap pakai vēntarkaḷatu kaḷiṟukaḷaik kavarntu koṇṭakālai eḻunta āravāram pōla, palar vāyppaṭṭu alar ākiṉṟatu - palar vāyilum porunti yeḻuntu alar ākā niṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) ūra! nī ōr kuṟumakaḷ koṇṭaṉai yeṉpa. atu, cōḻar paruvūrp paṟantalaiyil iruperu vēntarum porutu oḻiya, vāḷamark kaṭanta ñāṉṟaik kaḷiṟu kavar kampalai pōla alar ākiṉṟatu.
(vi-rai) maṇṭai ikkālattu montai eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭu
kiṉṟatu. nuṭakkal - kaḻuval. annīrai yuṇṭa eṉa varuvitturaikka. naṟavu kalanta nīrātaliṉ uṇṭu kaḷippatāyiṟṟu. kūṭṭu mutaṟ ṟeṟikkum eṉṟatu vaḷamikuti kūṟiyavāṟām. teṟikkum ñekiḻum eṉṉum peyareccaṅkaḷ iṭappeyar koṇṭaṉa. pirampiṉ neṭuṅkoṭi eṉka. kavar kampalai peyareccam kāriyap peyar koṇṭatu. kiṉṟu: iṭainilai; piṟkāla vaḻakkākaliṉ, alarā kiṉṟu eṉpatu pāṭamātal vēṇṭum; aṉṟi, atu pakutip poruḷ vikuti eṉalumām.
(u-ṟai) `naṟavuṇ maṇṭai...... kāñciyūra' eṉṟatu, parattaiyar cēriyaic cūḻntu tiriyum pāṇaṉ kāmañ cālā iḷamaiyō ḷākiya parattaiyait talaimakaṉōṭu kūṭṭat talaimakaṉ avaḷai muyaṅkiya viṭattu avaḷ kaḷippuṟṟu, nīṅkiyavaḻi melikiṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi. itaṉkaṇ poykaiyaip parattaiyar cēriyākavum, muḷḷuṭaip pirampiṉ koṭiyai ataṉaic cūḻntu tiriyum neñcu valiya pāṇaṉākavum, āmpal ilaiyaik kāmañcālāk kuṟumakaḷākavum, ataṉaik koṭi tuṭakkiyataṉaip pāṇaṉ avaḷait talaimakaṉōṭu kūṭṭiyatākavum, vāṭaiyait talai makaṉākavum, atu tūkkuntōṟum avvilai vīṅkit tūkkātavaḻi nekiḻntataṉai, talaimakaṉ avaḷai muyaṅkiyavaḻi avaḷ kaḷip puṟṟu, nīṅkiya vaḻi melinta taṉmaiyākavum koḷka. maṟṟum, itil `naṟavuṇ maṇṭai...... kūṭṭu mutaṟ ṟeṟikkum' eṉṟatu naṟavu kalanta iḻinta nīraiyuṇṭa iṟāl ac cerukkiṉāl kūṭṭumutaṟ ṟeṟittatu pōlap parattaiyariṉ iḻinta iṉpattai nukarnta talaivaṉum cerukkuṟṟut taṉakkup puṟampākiya vēṟiṭattē taṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟa taṉmaiyākavuṅ koḷka.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `oṇṭoṭi yāyat tuḷḷunī nayantu, koṇṭaṉai yeṉpavōr kuṟumakaḷ eṉak kāmañ cālā iḷamaiyōḷaik kūṟiṟṟu' eṉṟaṉar, nac.
`teyva mañcal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kaḷiṟu kavar kampalai pōla, alarākiṉṟatu palar vāyp paṭṭē' eṉpatu puṟañcol māṇāk kiḷavikku utāraṇamākum eṉṟaṉar, pērā.
`orumai cuṭṭiya'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `paṉmaik kāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē' eṉpatu, orumaic col paṉmaic collōṭu toṭar taṟkup poruntu miṭamu muṇṭeṉpatūum paṭa niṉṟamaiyāl, `aḵtai tantai ...... cōḻar' eṉa orumaic col paṉmaic collōṭu toṭartaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. īṇṭu orumaic col paṉmaic collōṭu mayaṅkuta luṭaimaiyāṉ, `oruvaraik kūṟum paṉmaik kiḷavi' eṉpuḻi aṭaṅkāmai yaṟika eṉṟaṉar cēṉāvaraiyar. ac cūttirattu, `aḵtai tantai........ cōḻar' eṉpuḻic cōḻarellārum aḵtaikkut tantaiyām muṟaiyarāy niṟṟaliṉ, `tantai' eṉṉum orumai, cōḻar eṉṉum paṉmaiyōṭu taṉittaṉi ceṉṟu kūṭutaliṉ vaḻuviṉṟēṉum īṟṟup paṉmaipaṟṟi vaḻuvamaittār eṉṟaṉar, nac.
97. pālai
(vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkalai
		vaṟaṉuṟal aṅkō ṭutira valaṅkaṭantu
		pulavuppuli tuṟanta kalavukkaḻi kaṭumuṭai
		iravukkuṟum palaṟa nūṟi niraipakut
	5.	tiruṅkal muṭakkart tiṟṟi keṇṭum
		kolaivil āṭavar pōlap 1palavuṭaṉ
		peruntalai yeruvaiyoṭu paruntuvan tirukkum
		aruñcuram iṟanta koṭiyōrk kalkalum
		iruṅkaḻai iṟumpiṉ āyntukoṇ ṭaṟutta
	10.	nuṇaṅkukaṇ ciṟukōl vaṇaṅkiṟai makaḷiro
		ṭakavunarp puranta aṉpiṉ kaḻaltoṭi
		naṟavumakiḻ irukkai naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ
		vayalai vēli viyalūr aṉṉaniṉ
		alarmulai yākam pulampap palaniṉain
	15.	tāḻēl eṉṟi tōḻi yāḻaveṉ
		kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō kuravumalarn
		taṟcira nīṅkiya 2arumpata vēṉil
		aṟalavir vārmaṇal akaliyāṟ ṟaṭaikarait
		tuṟaiyaṇi 3marutu tokalkoḷa vōṅkik
	20.	kaliḻtaḷir aṇinta iruñciṉai māat
		tiṇartatai putuppū niraitta poṅkarp
		pukaipurai ammañ cūra
		nukarkuyil akavum kuralkēṭ pōrkkē.
- 4māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-;
1-3. kaḷḷi am kāṭṭa puḷḷi am poṟik kalai - kaḷḷik kāṭṭilēyuḷḷa puḷḷikaḷāya poṟikaḷaiyuṭaiya kalaimāṉai, vaṟaṉ uṟal am kōṭu utira valaṅ kaṭantu - ataṉ vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa aḻakiya kompu citarntoḻiya ataṉ ōṭṭattiṉai veṉṟu paṟṟittiṉṟa, pulavup puli tuṟanta kalavuk kaḻi kaṭumuṭai - pulāl viruppuṭaiya puli kaiviṭṭup pōṉa mūṭṭu vāy oḻinta kaṭiya muṭaināṟṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya tacai kiṭakku miṭattē;
4-6. iravu kuṟumpu alaṟa nūṟi - iravilē kāṭṭaraṇ kaḷiluḷḷār alaṟa avarkaḷaik koṉṟu, nirai pakuttu - tām koṇṭa niraikaḷaip pakuttuk koṇṭu, iruṅ kal muṭukkar - periya kaṟpāṟaiyiṉ muṭukkilē, tiṟṟi keṇṭum - tacaiyiṉai aṟuttut tiṉṉum, kolai vil āṭavarpōla - kolait
toḻil valla villiṉaiyuṭaiya veṭci vīrar pōla;
6-8. peruntalai eruvaiyoṭu paruntu pala uṭaṉ vantirukkum - periya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kaḻukukaḷōṭu paruntukaḷ palavum oruṅkē vantu cūḻntirukkum, aruñ curam iṟanta koṭiyōrkku - ariya curaneṟiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa koṭiyōrāya nam talaivariṉ piriviṟkāka, alkalum - nāṭōṟum;
9-15. iruṅ kaḻai iṟumpiṉ - periya mūṅkilkaḷaiyuṭaiya ciṟu kāṭṭil, āyntu koṇṭu aṟutta - ārāyntu koṇṭu aṟuttiṭṭa, nuṇaṅku kaṇ ciṟu kōl - ciṟiya kaṇukkaḷaiyuṭaiya nuṇṇiya kōliṉaik koṇṭa, vaṇaṅku iṟai makaḷiroṭu - vaḷainta muṉ kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya viṟaliyaroṭu, akavunar puranta aṉpiṉ - pāṇarkaḷaip purantiṭum aṉpiṉaiyum, kaḻaltoṭi naṟavu makiḻ irukkai - kaḻalum toṭiyiṉaiyum kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻum irukkaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ - naṉṉaṉ vēṇ
māṉatu, vayalai vēli viyalūr aṉṉa - vayalaikkoṭi paṭarnta vēlikaḷaiyuṭaiya viyalūriṉai yotta, niṉ alar mulai ākam pulampa - niṉatu paranta mulaiyaiyuṭaiya mārpakam taṉittu varunta, pala niṉaintu āḻēl eṉṟi - palavum niṉaintu tuṉpattil āḻntiṭātē eṉṉāniṉṟāy; eṉ - iḵteṉṉai!,
16-23. kuravu malarntu aṟciram nīṅkiya arumpatavēṉil - kuravam malarntu muṉpaṉikkālam nīṅkap peṟṟa ariya cevviyaiyuṭaiya iḷavēṉiṟ kālattē, aṟal avir vār maṇal akal yāṟṟu aṭaikarai - aṟal viḷaṅkum nīṇṭa maṇalaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟa āṟṟiṉ karaiyiṭattē, tuṟai aṇi marutu tokalkoḷa ōṅki- tuṟaiyai aḻaku ceyyum maruta maraṅkaḷoṭu tokutiyāka uyarntu, kaliḻtaḷir aṇinta irumciṉai māattu - aḻaku oḻukum taḷiraik koṇṭa periya kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya mā marattiṉ, iṇartatai putup pū niraitta poṅkar - kottukkaḷāka neruṅkiya putiya pūkkaḷ ceṟinta cōlaikaḷilē, pukai purai am mañcu ūra - pukaiyiṉai yotta aḻakiya veṇ mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻa, nukar kuyil akavum kural kēṭpōrkku - (avviṉpattai) nukarum kuyilkaḷ pāṭum  kuraliṉaik kēṭpōrukku, kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō - kaṇṇiṉ nīrai niṟuttutal eḷitākuṅkol?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! aruñcuram iṟanta koṭiyōrkku viyalūraṉṉa niṉ alarmulai yākam pulampa āḻēl eṉṟi; eṉ? vēṉilil yāṟṟiṉ aṭaikaraip poṅkaril mañcu ūrak kuyil akavum kural kēṭpōrkkuk kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō?
nirai pakuttut tiṟṟi keṇṭum āṭavar pōla, puli kalaiyiṉ valaṅ kaṭantu tuṟanta muṭaikkaṇ eruvaiyoṭu paruntu vantirukkum aruñcuram eṉa iyaiyum.
(vi-rai) kaḷḷiyam.... poṟikkalai; am: acaikaḷ: valam - veṉṟi, ōṭum veṉṟi, kaṭantu eṉpataṉpiṉ paṟṟik koṉṟu tiṉṟa eṉpataṉai viritturaikka. muṭai - muṭai nāṟṟa muṭaiya tacaikku ākupeyar. muṭaiyiṭattē eruvaiyoṭu paruntu vantirukkum eṉka. iṟumpu - pakaivar eyiṟpuṟattuk kāṭumām. makaḷir - viṟaliyar. ivar kaiyilē ciṟiya mūṅkiṟ kōlaiyuṭaiya rāvareṉpatu, `veṇkaṭaic ciṟu kōl akavaṉ makaḷir'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pāṇar, kūttarākiya akavunarum kaiyiṟ kōluṭaiya reṉpatu piṟāṇṭu varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. yāḻa : acai. eṉ - evvāṟu iyalum eṉṟapaṭi. marutu - marutoṭu eṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu viritturaikka. kuyil akavum kural - kuyil pēṭṭiṉai aḻaikkum kural eṉṟalumām. kēṭpōrkku eṉat taṉṉaip piṟarpōl vaittuk kūṟiṉāḷ. iṉi, kēṭṭu vāḷāvirukkum talaivar poruṭṭāka, eṉ kaṇ paṉi niṟuttal eḷitō eṉṟuraittalumām.
(mē-ḷ) `naṭuvu nilaittiṇaiyē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itaṉkaṇ iruvakai vēṉilum pālaik kaṇ vantaṉa eṉṟār. nac.
98. kuṟiñci
(talaimakaṉ ciṟaippuppuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talaimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyc colliyatu; tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatūumām.)
		paṉivarai nivanta payaṅkeḻu kavāaṉ
		tuṉiyil koḷkaiyō ṭavarnamak kuvanta
		iṉiya uḷḷam iṉṉā vāka
		muṉitaka niṟutta nalkal evvam
	5.	cūruṟai veṟpaṉ mārpuṟat taṇital
		aṟintaṉaḷ allaḷ aṉṉai vārkōl
		ceṟintilaṅ kelvaḷai nekiḻntamai nōkkik
		kaiyaṟu neñciṉaḷ viṉavaliṉ mutuvāyp
		poyval peṇṭir pirappuḷar pirīi
	10.	murukaṉ āraṇaṅ keṉṟaliṉ atucet
		tōvat taṉṉa viṉaipuṉai nallil
		pāvai aṉṉa palarāy māṇkaviṉ
		paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟakkaveṉ makaṭkeṉap paraiik
		kūṭukoḷ iṉṉiyam kaṟaṅkak kaḷaṉiḻait
	15.	tāṭaṇi ayarnta akaṉperum pantar
		veṇpōḻ kaṭampoṭu cūṭi iṉcīr
		aitamai pāṇi irīikkai peyarāc
		celvaṉ perumpeyar ētti vēlaṉ
		veṟiyayar viyaṉkaḷam poṟpa vallōṉ
	20.	poṟiyamai pāvaiyiṉ tūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ
		eṉṉāṅ kollō tōḻi mayaṅkiya
		maiyaṟ peṇṭirkku novva lāka
		āṭiya piṉṉum vāṭiya mēṉi
		paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟavā tāyiṉ immaṟai
	25.	alarā kāmaiyō aritē yaḵtāṉ
		ṟaṟivar uṟuviya allalkaṇ ṭaruḷi
		veṟikamaḻ neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉē
		ceṟitoṭi yuṟṟa cellalum piṟiteṉak
		kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ
	30.	yāṉuyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritē.
- veṟipāṭiya kāmakkaṇṇiyār.
(co-ḷ) 21. tōḻi-;
1-4. paṉivarai nivanta payam keḻu kavāṉ - kuḷir vāynta malaiyiṉatu uyarnta vaḷam poruntiya pakka malaiyiṭattu, avar - nam talaivar, tuṉiil koḷkaiyōṭu - veṟuppillāta koḷkaiyuṭaṉ, namakku uvanta iṉiya uḷḷam - namakkuvantaḷitta avaratu iṉiya uḷḷam, iṉṉāāka - iṉṉāvāyiṉamaiyiṉ, muṉitaka niṟutta nalkal evvam - veṟukkumāṟu nilai peṟuttiya nalkal varuttamāṉatu;
5-6. cūr uṟai veṟpaṉ - teyvam uṟaiyum malaiyiṉai yuṭaiyāṉāya at talaivaṉ, mārpuuṟa - mārpu uṟuvatoṉṟāṉē, taṇital - taṇivatātalai, aṉṉai aṟintāḷ allaḷ - nam tāy aṟiyātavaḷāki;
6-8. vārkōl ceṟintu ilaṅku elvaḷai nekiḻntamai nōkki - nīṇṭa kōṟṟoḻi lamainta neruṅki viḷaṅkum oḷi poruntiya vaḷai nekiḻnta nilaiyiṉaip pārttu, kaiyaṟu neñciṉaḷ - ceyalaṟṟa uḷḷattiṉaḷāy, viṉavaliṉ - viṉavutalālē;
8-10. mutuvāy poy val peṇṭir - mutumai vāynta poy kūṟal valla kaṭṭuvicciyarāya peṇṭir, pirappu uḷarpu irīi - pirappariciyaip parappi vaittu, murukaṉ ār aṇaṅku eṉṟaliṉ - itu murukaṉatu ceyalāṉ vanta ariya varuttam eṉṟu kūṟaliṉ, atu cettu - ataṉai vāymaiyākak karuti;
11-13. ōvattu aṉṉa viṉai puṉai nal il - ōviyattai yotta puṉainta toḻiṟ ṟiṟaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya nalla maṉaiyil, pāvai aṉṉa palar āy māṇ kaviṉ - pāvaiyaip pōṉṟa palarālum ārāyappeṟum māṇpuṟṟa aḻakāṉatu, eṉ makaṭkup paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟakka eṉa - eṉ makaṭku muṉpōṟ ciṟappuṟuka eṉṟu, paraii- teyvattaip paravi;
14-15. kūṭu koḷ iṉṉiyam kaṟaṅkak kaḷaṉ iḻaittu - iṇainta palavāya iṉiya iyaṅkaḷ ottu olikka veṟiyāṭum kaḷaṉai iyaṟṟi, āṭu aṇi ayarnta akal perum pantar - āṭutaṟ kēṟṟa aḻaku ceyta akaṉṟa periya pantalilē;
16-21. veṇ pōḻ kaṭampoṭu cūṭi - veḷḷiya paṉan tōṭṭiṉaik kaṭappamalaroṭu cūṭi, iṉ cīr aitu amai pāṇi irīi - iṉiya cīr aḻakitāka amainta tāḷattoṭu porutti, celvaṉ perum peyar ētti - murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ perum pukaḻiṉait tutittu, vēlaṉ veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷam poṟpa - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum periya kaḷam aḻakupeṟa, vallōṉ poṟi amai pāvaiyiṉ - vallōṉ āṭṭum poṟiyamainta pāvaiyaip pōla, tūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ - āṭutalai virumpiṉ, eṉām kol - eṉ ākumō?
21-25. mayaṅkiya maiyal peṇṭirkku - veṟiyāṭuṅ kaḷattuk kūṭiya mayakkam poruntiya peṇṭirkku, novvalāka āṭiya piṉṉum - tuṉpam uṇṭāka vēlaṉ āṭiya piṉṉum, vāṭiya mēṉi - eṉatu vāṭiya mēṉi, paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟavātāyiṉ - muṉpu pōlac ciṟantiṭātāyiṉ, im maṟai alar ākāmaiyō aritē - ik kaḷavoḻukkam palarum tūṟṟumāṟu veḷippaṭātiruttal aritu;
25. aḵtāṉṟu - aḵtaṉṟi;
26-30. aṟivar uṟuviya allal kaṇṭaruḷi - tam talaivar uṟuvitta ivvallalait terintaruḷutaliṉ, veṟi kamaḻ neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉ - maṇaṅkamaḻum neṭuvēḷ nam muṉṉai aḻakiṉait tantaruḷvaṉ eṉiṉ, ceṟitoṭi uṟṟa cellalum piṟiteṉa - neruṅkiya vaḷaiyalai yaṇinta nam talaivi uṟṟa tuṉpamum piṟitoṉṟāṉeytiyatu eṉa, kāṉ keḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ - kāṭu poruntiya nāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya nam talaivaṉ kēṭṭaṟiyiṉ, yāṉ uyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritu - yāṉ uyir koṇṭu vāḻtal, muṟkūṟiya ataṉiṉum aritu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi (nam) evvam, aṉṉai aṟintaṉaḷ allaḷ; viṉavaliṉ peṇṭir murukaṉ āraṇaṅku eṉṟaliṉ, atu cettu, (murukaṟ) paraii āṭaṇi ayarnta pantar, vēlaṉ pāvaiyiṟ ṟūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ eṉ ām kol? āṭiya piṉṉum vāṭiya mēṉi ciṟavātāyiṉ maṟai alarākāmai aritu; aḵtāṉṟu, neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉ, cellalum piṟiteṉak kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ, yāṉ uyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritu.
(vi-rai) uvanta - uvantu aḷiceyta. āka: kāraṇap poruṭṭāyatu. nalkal evvam - talaivaṉ aruḷāl vanta evvam eṉattāṉuṟṟa nōykkup peyar kūṟiṉāḷ. allal eṉpataṉai eccamākkuka. mutuvāy - pēci mutirnta vāy eṉṟalumām. mutuvāyp peṇṭirāvār kaṭṭuvicciyar; kaṭṭuk kūṟupavar. kaṭṭāvatu, muṟattilē nellai yiṭṭuk kuṟi collutal. pirappu - arici, tiṉai mutaliyavaṟṟaip pala pakutiyākak kūṭaiyilātal, muṟattilātal paliyāka iṭṭu vaittal. `palpirap pirīi yeṉa' (242) eṉa innūluḷḷum, `tiṉaippirap pirīi'1 eṉak kuṟuntokai yilum, `kurutiyoṭu viraiiya tūveḷ ḷarici, cilpalic ceytu palpirap pirīi'2 eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyilum varutal kāṇka. veṇ pōḻ - pōḻappaṭṭa paṉantōṭu : ākupeyar. cīr - tāḷa aṟuti. celvaṉ - murukaṉ. perum peyar - periya tirunāmamum ām. aḵtāvatu, murukaṉukkuriya āṟeḻut tarumaṟai. vēlaṉ- cevvēḷiṉ vēlai ēnti niṉṟāṭupavaṉ; pūcāri. poṟi - iyantiram. vallōṉ iyaṟṟiya poṟi eṉka. vallōṉ āṭṭum pāvai yeṉiṉum poruntum. novval - nōval. ākāmai aritu : ākum eṉṟapaṭi. aḵtāṉṟu : `iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉaiyeñciṟuti'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ' eṉṟataṉāl, aṉṟi eṉpatu aṉṟu eṉa āyiṟṟu eṉavum, `aṉṟu varukālai āvā kutalum'2 eṉṟataṉāl āṉṟu eṉa āyiṟṟu eṉavum koḷka. kaṇṭaruḷi - kaṇṭu aruḷ ceytu eṉṟurait
talumām. veṟi - murukaṉukku iyalpāya naṟumaṇam. `maṇaṅkamaḻ teyvat tiḷanalam kāṭṭi'3 eṉṟār nakkīraṉārum. iṉi veṟiyāṭum kaḷattup pōtaraliṉ annāṟṟam kamaḻum eṉṟalumām. cellalum piṟiteṉak kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ yāṉuyir vāḻtal...... aritu eṉṟatu, taṉatu uṇmaik kātalukku iḻukku uṇṭātal karuti eṉka. ic ceyyuḷ talaivi kūṟṟātaṟkē peritum poruntiyatām. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ik karuttiṉarē yeṉpatu `kaḷavala rāyiṉum'4 eṉṉuñ cūttiravuraiyāṟ peṟappaṭum. tōḻi kūṟṟāyiṉ talaivikkum taṉakkum uḷḷa oṟṟumaiyāl, yāṉ eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ eṉal vēṇṭum.
99. pālai
(uṭaṉ pōkiya talaimakaḷait talaimakaṉ maruṭṭic colliyatu.)
		vāḷvari vayamāṉ kōḷukir aṉṉa
		cemmukai yaviḻnta muṇmutir murukkiṉ
		citarār cemmal tāay matareḻil
		māṇiḻai makaḷir pūṇuṭai mulaiyiṉ
	5.	mukaipiṇi aviḻnta kōṅkamo ṭacaiinaṉai
		atiral paranta antaṇ pātiri
		utirvī añciṉai tāay etirvī
		marāa malaroṭu virāayp parāam
		aṇaṅkuṭai nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ
	10.	naṉṟē kāṉam nayavarum amma
		kaṇṭiciṉ vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ nuntai
		aṭukaḷam pāynta toṭicitai maruppiṉ
		piṭimiṭai kaḷiṟṟil tōṉṟum
		kuṟuneṭun tuṇaiya kuṉṟamum uṭaittē.
-pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 11. kuṟumakaḷ - iḷamaṭavāy - vāḻiyō - vāḻvāyāka;
1-3. vāḷ vari vaya māṉ tōḷ ukir aṉṉa - vāḷ pōlum varikaḷaiyuṭaiya valiya puliyiṉatu kollum toḻilaiyuṭaiya nakam pōṉṟa, cem mukai aviḻnta muḷ mutir murukkiṉ - civanta mukai virinta muḷ niṟainta murukka malarākiya, citar ār cemmal tāay - vaṇṭu cūḻnta vāṭiya pūkkaḷ parantu;
3-6. matar eḻil māṇ iḻai makaḷir pūṇ uṭai mulaiyiṉ - katirtta aḻakiṉaiyum māṇpuṟṟa aṇiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya makaḷiratu pūṇaṇinta mulaiyiṉaip pōṉṟa, mukai piṇi aviḻnta kōṅka
moṭu naṉai atiral acaii - mukaikaḷ alarnta kōṅkam pūkkaḷōṭu kottukkaḷāya puṉalippū kūṭik kiṭakka;
6-7. paranta am taṇ pātiri añciṉai utir vī tāay - paraviya aḻakiya kuḷirnta pātiriyiṉatu aḻakiya ciṉaiyiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūkkaḷōṭu tāvi;
7-8. etir vī marāa malaroṭu virāay - māṟupaṭṭa pūkkaḷ veṇkaṭappam pūkkaḷōṭu viravit tāvi;
8-9. parāam aṇaṅku uṭai nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ - paravuk kaṭaṉ pūṇṭa teyvamuṭaiya kōviliṉkaṇ kalantu kiṭakkum pūkkaḷaip pōla;
10-11. kāṉam naṉṟē nayavarum - ik kāṭu peritum virumpat takkatākiṉṟatu, kaṇṭiciṉ - kāṇpāka;
11-14. (maṟṟum ik kāṉam), nuntai aṭukaḷam pāynta toṭi citai maruppiṉ - uṉ tantai pakaivarai aṭum pōrkkaḷattup pāyntu pōr ceytalāl pūṇ citainta kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiyavum, piṭimiṭai - piṭikaḷ cūḻap peṟṟavumāya, kaḷiṟṟil tōṉṟum - kaḷiṟukaḷaip pōlat tōṉṟum, kuṟu neṭun tuṇaiya - ciṟiyavum periyavumāya aḷaviṉaiyuṭaiya, kuṉṟamum uṭaittu - kuṉṟaṅ kaḷaiyum uṭaiyatākiṉṟatu; (kāṇpāyāka.)
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ, vāḻiyō! murukkiṉ cemmal tāay, mukai piṇi yaviḻnta kōṅkamoṭu naṉai atiral acaii, pātiri añciṉai utir vī etir vī marāa malaroṭu tāay, virāay, aṇaṅkuṭai nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ kāṉam naṉṟu nayavarum; (atu) piṭimiṭai kaḷiṟṟiṟ ṟōṉṟum kuṟu neṭun tuṇaiya kuṉṟamum uṭaittu; amma! kaṇṭiciṉ.
(vi-rai) kōḷ ukir - iraiyaip paṟṟiya ukireṉṟumām; kurutiyāṟ civanta ukireṉpāṉ `kōḷukir' eṉṟāṉ; puliyukir vaṇṇamum vaṭivum paṟṟi murukkiṉ cemmukaikku uvamaiyāyatu. mulaipōlum mukai yeṉka. `marāa malaroṭu virāayp parāam' eṉṟatu aḷapeṭai vaṇṇam; eṉṉai? "aḷapeṭai vaṇṇa maḷapeṭai payilum"1 eṉṟārākaliṉ. amma : uraiyacai; kēṭpittaṟ poruṭṭumām. pakaivaratu matiṟkatavaip pāytaliṉ toṭi citainta teṉalumām. maruppiṟ kaḷiṟu piṭimiṭai kaḷiṟu eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `oṉṟāt tamariṉum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷaik kūṟi, `itu talaiviyai maruṭṭiyatu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nac.
100. neytal
(tōḻi varaivu kaṭāyatu.)
		araiyuṟ ṟamainta āram nīvip
		puraiyap pūṇṭa kōtai mārpiṉai
		nallakam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅki nīvan
		telliṉiṟ peyartal eṉakkumā riṉitē
	5.	peruntirai muḻakkamo ṭiyakkavin tirunta
		koṇṭal iraviṉ iruṅkaṭal maṭutta
		koḻumīṉ koḷpavar iruḷnīṅ koṇcuṭar
		ōṭāp pūṭkai vēntaṉ pācaṟai
		āṭiyal yāṉai aṇimukat tacaitta
	10.	ōṭai oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum
		pāṭunart toṭutta kaivaṇ kōmāṉ
		pariyuṭai naṟṟērp periyaṉ viriyiṇarp
		puṉṉaiyaṅ kāṉaṟ puṟantai muṉṟuṟai
		vampa nārai yiṉaṉolit taṉṉa
	15.	ampal vāytta teyya taṇpular
		vaikuṟu viṭiyal pōkiya erumai
		neytalam putumalar  3māntum
		kaitaiyam paṭappaiem maḻuṅka lūrē.
- ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. arai uṟṟu amainta āramnīvi - naṟumaṇam kūṭṭi araikkappeṟṟu muṭinta cantaṉattaip pūci, puraiyap pūṇṭa kōtai mārpiṉai - uyarvuṟa mālaiyiṉaip pūṇṭa mārpiṉaiyuṭaiyaiyāy, nī elliṉil vantu nal akam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅkip peyartal eṉakkum iṉitu - nī iraviṉil vantu niṉatu nalla mārpu vaṭuvuṇṭāka muyaṅkip pōtal eṉakkum mika iṉitākum; (āyiṉum,)
5-7. perum tirai muḻakkamoṭu iyakku avintirunta - periya alaiyiṉ oliyōṭu ataṉ alaivum ōyntirunta, koṇṭal iravil - mēkañ cūḻnta iravil, iru kaṭal maṭutta koḻumīṉ koḷpavar - koḻumīṉaip piṭikkum paratavar kariya kaṭalilē maṭutta ampiyiluḷḷa, iruḷ nīṅku oḷcuṭar - iruḷ nīṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa oḷi poruntiya viḷakkam;
8-10. ōṭāp pūṭkai vēntaṉ pācaṟai - piṟakkiṭāta mēṟ kōḷiṉaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉ pācaṟaikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa, āṭu iyal yāṉai aṇimukattu acaitta - aṭutal valla yāṉaiyiṉ aḻakiya mukattiṟ piṇitta, ōṭai oṇ cuṭar oppat tōṉṟum - ōṭaiyiṉatu oḷḷiya cuṭarpōlat tōṉṟum (iṭamākiya);
11-15. pāṭunart toṭutta kaivaṇ kōmāṉ - pāṭi varuvārai vaḷaittuk koḷḷum kaivaṇmai vāynta kōmāṉākiya, pari uṭai naltērp periyaṉ - kutiraikaḷ pūṇṭa ciṟanta tēriṉai yuṭaiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, viri iṇarp puṉṉai am kāṉal puṟantai muṉṟuṟai - malar virinta kottukkaḷaiyuṭaiya puṉṉai maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya cōlai cūḻnta puṟaiyāṟṟiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟaiyiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa, vampa nārai iṉaṉ olittaṉṉa ampal - putiya nāraiyiṉ kūṭṭam olittāṟ pōṉṟa alar;
15-18. taṇ pular vaikuṟu viṭiyal - taṇṇeṉṟu pulantiṭum iruḷ taṅkiya viṭiyaṟkālattē, pōkiya erumai neytal putumalar māntum - veḷic ceṉṟa erumai neytaliṉ putitāka alarnta malariṉait tiṉṉum, kaitai am paṭappai em aḻuṅkal ūr - tāḻai vēlikaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya tōṭṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya ematu āravāram mikka ūriṉkaṇ, vāytta - eḻuntaṉa.
(muṭipu) talaiva, nī elliṉil vantu peyartal emakkum iṉitu; āyiṉum, mīṉ koḷpavar iruṅkaṭal maṭutta oṇcuṭar vēntaṉ pācaṟai yāṉaiyiṉ ōṭai  oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum periyaṉ puṟantai muṉṟuṟai nārai iṉaṉ olittaṉṉa ampal em aḻuṅkal ūr vāyttaṉa.
(vi-rai) eṉakkumār: ummai eccap poruṭṭu; mār : acai. uvamaikkaṇ yāṉai mukat tacaitta ōṭai yeṉṟamaiyāl, poruḷilum ataṟkēṟpa, kaṭalmaṭutta ampiyiṟ piṇitta cuṭar eṉa varuvitturaikka. `pāṭunart toṭutta kaivaṇ kōmāṉ' eṉṟatu, pāṭuvār poruḷ vēṭṭup piṟariṭam cellāvāṟu mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkupavaṉ eṉṟapaṭiyām. puṉṉaiyil nārai yirukku meṉpatum, vampa nārai eṉṉum vaḻakkum, `niṉṟa puṉṉai nilantōy paṭuciṉai - vampa nārai cēkkum'1 eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuḷḷuṅ kāṇka. puṟantai - puṟaiyāṟu eṉpataṉ marūu. itu poṟaiyāṟu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. `naṟavumaki ḻirukkai naṟṟērp periyaṉ, kaṭkamaḻ poṟaiyāṟ ṟaṉṉa'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. vāytta : muṟṟuviṉai. teyya: acai. `ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum'2 eṉṟa cūttirattu oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāl, piṟa karupporuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa uḷavēṉuṅ koḷka eṉṟaliṉ, marutattu vilaṅkākiya erumaiyum, ciṟupoḻutākiya vaikaṟaiyum neytaṟkaṇ vantaṉa.
101. pālai
(piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭa kiḻatti tōḻikkuc colliyatu. 
tōḻi kiḻattikkuc colliyatūu mām.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi yimmai
		naṉṟucey maruṅkil tītil eṉṉum
		toṉṟupaṭu paḻamoḻi yiṉṟupoyt taṉṟukol
		takarmarup pēyppac cuṟṟupu curinta
	5.	cuvaṉmāy pittaic ceṅkaṇ maḻavar
		vāyppakai kaṭiyum maṇṇoṭu kaṭuntiṟal
		tīppaṭu ciṟukōl villoṭu paṟṟi
		nuraiteri mattaṅ koḷīi niṟaippuṟat
		taṭiputait toṭutōl paṟaiya vēkik
	10.	kaṭipulaṅ kavarnta kaṉṟuṭaik koḷḷaiyar
		iṉantalai peyarkkum naṉantalaip peruṅkāṭ
		ṭakaliru vicumpiṟ kōṭam pōlap
		pakaliṭai niṉṟa palkatir ñāyiṟ
		ṟuruppavir pūriya 3cuḻaṉṟuvaru kōṭaip
	15.	puṉkāl muruṅkai ūḻkaḻi paṉmalar
		taṇkār āliyil tāvaṉa utirum
		paṉipaṭu paṉmalai iṟantōrkku
		muṉitaku paṇpiyāñ ceytaṉṟō vilamē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi vāḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉaik kēṭpāyāka!,
1-3. immai naṉṟu cey maruṅkil tītu il eṉṉum - immaiyil naṉṟu ceyyumiṭattut tītu varuvatillai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum, toṉṟu paṭu paḻamoḻi - toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi, iṉṟu poyttaṉṟukol - iṉṟu poyyāyatō;
4-11. takar maruppu ēyppac cuṟṟupu curinta - cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kiṭāyiṉ kompiṉai yoppac cuṟṟik kaṭai curuṇṭa, cuvalmāy pittaic ceṅkaṇ maḻavar - piṭariyai maṟaikkum talai mayiriṉai yum civanta kaṇṇiṉaiyumuṭaiya maḻavarkaḷ, vāyp pakai kaṭiyum maṇṇoṭu - vāyiṉiṉṟeḻum irumalāya pakaiyiṉai eḻāmaṟ ṟīrkkum maruntāya puṟṟumaṇṇai vāyil aṭakkik koṇṭaṉarāy, kaṭum tiṟal tīp paṭu ciṟu kōl villoṭu paṟṟi - kaṭiya tiṟalvāynta tīyuṇṭām ciṟiya ampiṉai villōṭu kaiyiṟpaṟṟi, nurai terimattam koḷīi - veṇṇeyyai veḷippaṭuttum kaṭaiyum mattiṉaik kavarntukoṇṭu, niraip puṟattu aṭi putai toṭutōl paṟaiya ēki - āṉiraikaḷ uḷḷaviṭattut tam aṭiyai maṟaittuḷḷa ceruppukkaḷ tēyac ceṉṟu, kaṭi pulam kavarnta kaṉṟuṭaik koḷḷaiyar - pakaivar kāval iṭattilē kavarnta kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya āṉ iṉattaiyuṭaiyarāy, iṉam talai peyarkkum naṉantalaip peruṅkāṭṭu - avviṉattiṉai avviṭattiṉiṉṟu koṇṭu pōkum akaṉṟa iṭattaiyuṭaiya periya kāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē;
12-16. akal iru vicumpiṟku ōṭampōla - akaṉṟa periya vāṉākiya kaṭaṟkaṇ ōṭampōla, pakaliṭai niṉṟa pal katir ñāyiṟṟu - pakalil vāṉiṭaiyē niṉṟa pala katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya ñāyiṟṟiṉ, uruppu avirpu ūriya cuḻaṉṟu varu kōṭai - veppam viḷaṅkip parakkac cuḻaṉṟu varu mēl kāṟṟiṉāl, puṉ kāl muruṅkai ūḻ kaḻi paṉmalar - pulliya aṭimarattiṉaiyuṭaiya muruṅkaiyiṉ mutirntu kaḻiyum pala pūkkaḷ, taṇ kār āliyil tāvaṉa utirum - kuḷirnta kārkālattu ālaṅkaṭṭi pōlap parantaṉavāy utirum;
17-18. paṉipaṭu paṉmalai iṟantōrkku - naṭukkamuṇṭākum pala malaikaḷaiyum tāṇṭic ceṉṟa nam talaivarkku, muṉitaku paṇpuyām ceytaṉṟō ilam - veṟukkattakka ceyal yātum nām ceytilamē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! "immai..... paḻamoḻi" iṉṟu poyttaṉṟukol! peruṅkāṭṭilē, ñāyiṟṟiṉ uruppu avirpu ūrtaliṉ, cuḻaṉṟuvaru kōṭaiyāl muruṅkaiyiṉ paṉmalar āliyiṉ utirum paṉmalai yiṟantōrkku yām muṉitaku paṇpu ceytaṉṟō ilam.
maḻavar, maṇṇoṭu ciṟukōl villoṭu paṟṟi, koḷīi, ēki, kavarnta koḷḷaiyar iṉantalai peyarkkum peruṅkāṭu eṉka.
(vi-rai) vāyppakai - vāyiṉiṉ ṟuṇṭām pakai; irumal; avaratu varavai veḷippaṭuttaliṉ pakai eṉṟār. nurai - veṇṇey. paṟaiya - kaḻala veṉṟumām. kaṉṟuṭaik koḷḷaiyar - kaṉṟiṉai yuṭaiya āṉiṉ koḷḷaiyar eṉka. vicumpiṟku ōṭam - vicumpākiya kaṭaliṉkaṇ ōṭam: aḷakkalākā virivu paṟṟi vicumpiṟkuk kaṭalum, ataṉai ūṭaṟuttuc cellutal paṟṟi ñāyiṟṟukku ōṭamum uvamaiyāyiṉa. ūriya - ūrtalāl. kāṭṭiṉkaṇ malar utirum malaiyeṉka. kāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya paṉmalai yeṉṟalumām. malaiyiṟan tōrkku muṉitaku paṇpu yām ceytilam; aṅṅaṉamākavum avar varaiviṉai nīṭṭikkac ceytu nammai varuttutaliṉ `naṉṟu cey maruṅkil tītuil'  eṉṉum paḻamoḻi iṉṟu poyttatu pōlum eṉak kiḻatti kavaṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. `naṉṟu cey maruṅkil' eṉṟamaiyāl ivaḷ iyaṟkaip puṇarccik kālattut talaivaṟku iṉpam viḷaittā ḷeṉpatu peṟṟām. tōḻi kūṟṟāyiṉum poruḷituvē.
102. kuṟiñci
(iravuk kuṟikkaṇ ciṟaippuṟamākat tōḻikkuc colluvāḷāyt talaimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		uḷaimāṉ tuppiṉ ōṅkutiṉaip perumpuṉattuk
		kaḻutiṟ kāṉavaṉ piḻimakiḻntu vatinteṉa
		uraitta cantiṉ ūral iruṅkatup
		paituvaral acaivaḷi yāṟṟak kaipeyarā
	5.	oliyal vārmayir uḷariṉaḷ koṭicci
		peruvarai maruṅkil kuṟiñci pāṭak
		kuraluṅ koḷḷātu nilaiyiṉum peyarātu
		paṭāap paiṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟ ṟoyyeṉa
		maṟampukal maḻakaḷi ṟuṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ
	10.	āra mārpiṉ ariñimi ṟārppat
		tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ eḵkuṭai valattaṉ
		kāvalar aṟital ōmpip paiyeṉa
		vīḻāk katavam acaiyiṉaṉ pukutan
		tuyaṅkupaṭar akala muyaṅkit tōḷmaṇan
	15.	tiṉcol laḷaiip peyarntaṉaṉ tōḻi
		iṉṟevaṉ kollō kaṇṭiku maṟṟavaṉ
		nalkā maiyiṉ ampa lāki
		oruṅkuvan tuvakkum paṇpiṉ
		iruñcūḻ ōti oṇṇutal pacappē.
- 1maturai iḷampālāciriyaṉ cēntaṅkūttaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-;
1-9. uḷaimāṉ tuppiṉ - ciṅkam pōlum valiyiṉaṉāy, tiṉaip perum puṉattu ōṅku kaḻutil - periya tiṉaippuṉattil ōṅkiya paraṇiṉkaṇṇē, kāṉavaṉ piḻi makiḻntu vatinteṉa - kāṉavaṉ kaḷḷuṇṭu kaḷittu iruntaṉaṉāka, uraitta cantiṉ ūral irum katuppu - pūciya mayirc cāntiṉaiyuṭaiya paranta kariya kūntalai, aituvaral acaivaḷi āṟṟa - melleṉa acaintu varum kāṟṟup pukuntu pulartta, oliyal vār mayir kai peyarā uḷariṉaḷ - taḻaittu nīṇṭa ak kūntalait taṉ kaiyāṟ peyarttuk kōtik koṇṭu, koṭicci - avaṉ maṉaivi, peruvarai maruṅkil - ap periya varaiyiṉ pakkattē, kuṟiñci pāṭa - kuṟiñcip paṇṇaip pāṭāniṟka, kuralum koḷḷātu - tāṉ koṇṭa tiṉaik katiriṉaiyum uṭkoḷḷātu, nilaiyiṉum peyarātu - niṉṟa nilaiyiṉiṉṟum akalātu, paṭāap paiṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟṟa - tuyilvarappeṟāta aḻakiya kaṇ tuyilvarap peṟṟu, maṟam pukal maḻa kaḷiṟu oyyeṉa uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ - vīrattiṉai virumpiya iḷaiya kaḷiṟu viraintu tūṅkum nāṭṭaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya nam talaivaṉ;
10-15. āram mārpiṉ ari ñimiṟu ārppa - cantaṉam pūciya taṉatu mārpiṉkaṇṇē aḻakiya vaṇṭukaḷ olippa, tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ eḵku uṭai valattaṉ - tāraṉum kaṇṇiyaṉum vēl poruntiya valakkaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉumāki, kāvalar aṟital ōmpi - kāvalāḷar aṟitalaip parikarittu, paiyeṉa vīḻāk katavam acaiyiṉaṉ pukutantu - tāḻ vīḻttāta kataviṉarukē taṅki melleṉap pukuntu, uyaṅku paṭar akala muyaṅkit tōḷ maṇantu - varuntum tuṉpam nīṅka aṇaittut tōḷiṉaik kūṭi, iṉcol aḷaiip peyarntaṉaṉ - iṉ coṟkaḷaik kalantu pēcic ceṉṟaṉaṉ; (aṅṅaṉamākavum ippāl,)
16-19. iṉṟu avaṉ nalkāmaiyiṉ - avaṉ iṉṟu oru nāḷ vantu aruḷ ceyyāmaiyiṉ, oruṅku vantu ampal āki - oruṅkē vantu alar ākumāṟu, uvakkum paṇpiṉ - makiḻcciyai viḷaivikkum iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya, iruñ cūḻ ōti oṇnutal pacappu - kariya kūntalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa oḷḷiya neṟṟiyiṉ pacappiṉai, evaṉ kollō kaṇṭikum - nām kaṇṭatu eṉṉaiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṉavaṉ piḻi makiḻntu vatinteṉa, koṭicci kuṟiñci pāṭa, kaḷiṟu kaṇpāṭu peṟṟu uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ, muyaṅkit tōḷ maṇantu iṉ col aḷaiip peyarntaṉaṉ; iṉṟu avaṉ nalkāmaiyiṉ, ampal āka, nutaliṭattup pacappiṉai evaṉ kol kaṇṭikum?
(vi-rai) uḷaimāṉ - piṭari mayiriṉaiyuṭaiya vilaṅku; ciṅkam. piḻi - kaḷ, piḻiyappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. pāṭa - pāṭutalāl. pāṭutalāṟ koḷḷātu peyarātu uṟaṅkum eṉka. tār - mārpiṟ cūṭuvatu. kaṇṇi - talaiyiṟ cūṭuvatu. aṟital ōmpi - aṟiyātapaṭi pātukāttu. maṟṟu : viṉaimāṟṟu. ampal āki : ampal āka eṉat tirikka. uvakkum paṇpiṉ ōti eṉka; nutal eṉṟalumām. iṉi ōti eṉpataṉai āku peyarākak koṇṭu, tōḻi ōti eṉa viḷiyākkalumām. iṉṟu oru nāḷ aruḷ ceyyāmaiyāl, neṟṟiyil pacappu uṇṭāyatu eṉṉai eṉṟapaṭi.
(u-ṟai) koṭicci, kāṉavaṉukku iṉpam uṇṭākap pāṭiya kuṟiñcip paṇ, yāṉaiyait tiṉai yuṇṇāmaṟ ceytataṉṟi, paci oru puṟam naliya, ataṉai uṟaṅkavum ceytatupōla, ampaṟ peṇṭir, tāykku uṇarttiya alar, talaivaṉ talaiviyai nukarāmaṟ ceytataṉṟi, vēṭkai orupuṟam naliya, avaṉ vāḷātē puṟattē ayarntirukkavum ceytatu eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `iravukkuṟiyē...... maṉaiyakam pukāk kālaiyāṉa'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷaik kūṟi, itu maṉaiyakam pukkatu eṉṟuraittaṉar, nac.
103. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc coṟṟatu.)
		niḻalaṟu naṉantalai yeḻālēṟu kuṟitta
		katirtta ceṉṉi nuṇaṅkucen nāviṉ
		vitirtta pōlum annuṇ palpoṟik
		kāmar cēval ēmañ cēppa
	5.	muḷiyaril pulampap pōki muṉāatu
		murampaṭain tirunta mūri maṉṟat
		tatarpārt talkum ākeḻu ciṟukuṭi
		uṟaiyunar pōkiya vōṅkunilai viyaṉnakar2
		iṟainiḻal oruciṟaip pulampayā uyirkkum
	10.	vemmuṉai yaruñcuram nīntit tamvayiṉ
		īṇṭuviṉai maruṅkiṉ mīṇṭōr maṉṉeṉa
		naḷḷeṉ yāmat tuyavuttuṇai yāka
		nammoṭu pacalai nōṉṟu tammoṭu
		tāṉē3 ceṉṟa nalaṉum
	15.	nalkār kollōnām nayantici ṉōrē.
- kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuc ceṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. (tōḻi),  nām nayanticiṉōr - nammāl virumpappaṭṭa talaivar;
1-5. niḻal aṟu naṉantalai - niḻalaṟṟa akaṉṟa pālai nilattē, eḻāl ēṟu kuṟitta - pullūṟṟiṉāl eṟṟutal kuṟikkappaṭṭa, katirtta ceṉṉi - periya talaiyiṉaiyum, nuṇaṅku cen nāviṉ - nuṇukiya civanta nāviṉaiyum, vitirtta pōlum am nuṇ pal poṟi - teḷittaṉa pōlum aḻakiya ciṟiya palavāya puḷḷikaḷaiyu muṭaiya, kāmar cēval - aḻakiya kuṟumpūḻc cēval, ēmam  cēppa - pātukāvalāṉa iṭattiṟ ceṉṟu taṅka eṇṇi, muḷi aril pulampap pōki - tāṉirunta kāyntupaṭṭa ciṟu tūṟu taṉit toḻiyap pōy;
5-10. muṉāatu murampu aṭaintirunta mūri maṉṟattu - vaṉṉilattaic cārntirunta paḻaimaiyāṉa periya maṉṟilē, atar pārttu alkum - (āṟalaikka) vaḻiyaip pārttut taṅki yirukkum, ā keḻu ciṟukuṭi - pacukkaḷ poruntiya cīṟūriṉkaṇṇē, uṟaiyunar pōkiya ōṅki nilai viyaṉnakar - uṟaivōr viṭṭup pōṉa iṭamākiya uyarnta nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya periya maṉaiyil, iṟai niḻal oru ciṟai - iṟappu niḻaliṉ oru pakkattē taṅki, pulampu ayā uyirkkum - taṉimaiyāl peru mūcceṟintirukkum, vemmuṉai arum curam nīnti - koṭiya muṉaiyiruppukkaḷaiyuṭaiya ariya curattiṉaik kaṭantu;
10-15. tam vayiṉ īṇṭu viṉai maruṅkiṉ - tammiṭattu vantuṟṟa poruḷīṭṭum viṉaiyakattē, mīṇṭōr eṉa - mīṇṭum cellu taluṟṟāreṉa, naḷ eṉ yāmattu uyavut tuṇai āka - naḷḷeṉṟa naṭu yāmattum ucāvum tuṇaiyākum poruṭṭu, nammoṭu pacalai nōṉṟu - nammoṭu itukāṟum pacalaiyaip poṟuttiruntu ippoḻutu, tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟa nalaṉum - at talaivaruṭaṉ tāṉē valintu ceṉṟa nam nalaṉaiyēṉum, nalkār kollō - namakku aruḷārō?
(muṭipu) (tōḻi!) nām nayanticiṉōr, aruñcuram nīnti, viṉai maruṅkiṉ mīṇṭōr eṉa nammoṭu pacalai nōṉṟu tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟa nalaṉum nalkārkollō?
eḻālēṟu kuṟitta cēval, ēmam cēppa, pōki iṟai niḻal oru ciṟai pulampu ayāvuyirkkum aruñcuram eṉka.
(vi-rai) eḻāl - pullūṟu, ēṟu - eṟital, vavvutal; `kūrukirp paruntiṉ ēṟukuṟit torīi'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. katirtta ceṉṉi - oḷiviṭum talai eṉalumām. cēval - īṇṭuk kuṟumpūḻc cēval. mīṇṭōr - mīṇṭu ceṉṟār. ākiya eṉum pāṭattiṟkum ākum poruṭ ṭeṉpatē poruḷ. nalaṉum uyavut tuṇaiyāka nammoṭu pacalai nōṉṟiruntu, tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟatu; iṉi, nalkār kollō eṉṟu uraittalumām. itaṟkuc ceṉṟatu eṉṟatu ceṉṟa eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu eṉka.
104. mullai
(viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷun talaimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		vēntuviṉai muṭitta kālait tēmpāyn
		tiṉavaṇ ṭārkkun taṇṇaṟum puṟaviṉ
		veṉvēl iḷaiyar iṉpuṟa valavaṉ
		vaḷpuvalit tūriṉ allatu muḷḷuriṉ
	5.	munnīr maṇṭilam āti āṟṟā
		naṉṉālku pūṇṭa kaṭumpari neṭuntēr
		vāṅkuciṉai poliya ēṟip putala
		pūṅkoṭi avaraip poyyataḷ aṉṉa
		uḷḷil vayiṟṟa veḷḷai veṇmaṟi
	10.	māḻki yaṉṉa tāḻperuñ ceviya
		puṉtalaic ciṟārō ṭukaḷi maṉṟuḻaik
		kavaiyilai āriṉ aṅkuḻai kaṟikkum
		cīṟūr palapiṟak koḻiya mālai
		iṉitucey taṉaiyāl entai vāḻiya
	15.	paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ palapulan tuṟaiyum
		āytoṭi yarivai kūntal
		pōtukural aṇiya vēytan tōyē.
- maturai marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14. entai - em talaivaṉē!,
1-7. vēntu viṉai muṭitta kālai - vēntaṉ viṉaiyai muṭitta kālattē, tēm pāyntu iṉa vaṇṭu ārkkum - ticaikaḷtōṟum paṟantu kūṭṭamāya vaṇṭukaḷ āravārikkum, taṇ naṟum puṟavil - kuḷirnta naṟiya mullai nilattē, veṉvēl iḷaiyar iṉpuṟa - veṉṟi poruntiya vēliṉaiyuṭaiya vīrar iṉpam aṭaiya, valavaṉ vaḷpu valittu ūriṉ allatu - pākaṉ kaṭivāḷattiṉai iḻuttup piṭittuc celuttiṉ allatu, muḷuṟiṉ munnīr maṇṭilam āti āṟṟā - tāṟṟukkōl tīṇṭiṉ kaṭal cūḻnta ulakē āti eṉum celaviṟkup pōtāta, kaṭumpari nalnālku pūṇṭa neṭuntēr - mikka vēkamuṭaiya nalla nāṉku kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟa nīṇṭa tēril, vāṅku ciṉai poliya ēṟi - vaḷainta koṭiñci poliya ēṟi;
7-13. putala pū koṭi avaraip poy ataḷ aṉṉa - putalkaḷiṟ paṭarnta pūvaiyuṭaiya avaraik koṭiyiluḷḷa poyt tōl pōṉṟa, uḷil vayiṟṟa - uḷḷīṭillāta vayiṟṟiṉavum, māḻki yaṉṉa tāḻperum ceviya - mayaṅkik kiṭantālotta tāḻntu toṅkum periya ceviyiṉavum ākiya, veḷḷai veṇmaṟi - veḷḷāṭṭiṉ veḷḷiya kuṭṭi, puṉtalaic ciṟārōṭu ukaḷi - puṟkeṉṟa talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ciṟuvarkaḷōṭu kutittuc ceṉṟu, maṉṟuḻaik kavai ilai āriṉ am kuḻai kaṟikkum - maṉṟiṉ kaṇṇē kavaiyāya ilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya āttiyiṉ aḻakiya taḷiraik kaṭikkum, ciṟu ūr pala piṟakku oḻiya - ciṟiya ūrkaḷ pala piṉṉē oḻintiṭa vantu;
13-17. mālai - im mālaik kālattē, paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ pala pulantu uṟaiyum - nīrvaṭiyum kaṇṇiṉaḷāyp palavaṟṟaiyum eṇṇi veṟuttu uṟaiyāniṟkum, āytoṭi arivai - ārāynta vaḷaiyalaiyuṭaiya talaivi, aṇiya - aṇintukoḷḷumāṟu, kural kūntal - avaḷuṭaiya kottākiya kūntalil, pōtu vēytantōy - malaraic cūṭṭiṉai (ākavē), iṉitu ceytaṉai - iṉiya toṉṟaic ceytaṉaiyāvāy, vāḻiya - nī vāḻvāyāka.
(muṭipu) entai! vēntu viṉai muṭitta kālai, kaṭumpari neṭuntēr ēṟi, cīṟūr pala piṟakku oḻiya (vantu,) mālai, pulantu uṟaiyum arivai aṇiya kūntal pōtu vēytan
tōy, ataṉāl, iṉitu ceytaṉai, vāḻiya!
(vi-rai) tēm - ticai, `avaṉ maṟaitēem nōkki'1 eṉpatu kāṇka; tēṉumām. vaḷpu - vār, kaṭivāḷam. muḷ - tāṟṟu muḷ. āti - kutiraiyiṉ nērōṭṭam. nālku - nāṉku; peyart tiricol leṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar; `pālpurai puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi' 2 eṉpataṉ urai kāṇka. ciṉai - tēriṉ uṟuppākiya koṭiñci. poy ataḷ - uḷḷīṭiṉṟit tōl māttiramāyirukkum kāy. talaivaṉai entai yeṉṟu kūṟuvatu, `aṉṉai yeṉṉai yeṉṟalu muḷavē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ummaiyāl taḻuvappaṭum. maṟi, ciṟārō ṭukaḷik kuḻai kaṟikkum cīṟūr pala piṟakkoḻiya eṉṟatu, kārp paruvattil, talaivaṉ mīṇṭu varum vaḻiyāṉatu, avaṉukku iṉpattai viḷaittu, talaivipāl viraintēkat tūṇṭiyataik kuṟittavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, vañcik kaṇṇum potuviyal varuvaṉa uḷa eṉṟu kūṟi, atu `vēntu viṉai muṭitta' eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuḷ, cuṭṭi oruvar peyar kūṟā vañci potuviyalāy vantavāṟu kāṇka, eṉṟum, `viṉaivayiṟ pirintōṉ'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷaik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ viṉaimuṭitta kālait tēriḷaiyar celaviṟ kēṟpa ūrātu kōlūṉṟiṉ ulakiṟantaṉa celaviṟkup paṟṟāta kutirait tērēṟi iṭaiccurattil taṅkātu mālaikkālattu vantu pūccūṭṭiṉai;  iṉitu ceytaṉai; entai vāḻiya, eṉat tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟum uraittaṉar, nac.
105. pālai
(makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.)
		akalaṟai malarnta arumpumutir vēṅkai
		oḷḷilait toṭalai taii melleṉa
		nalvarai nāṭaṉ taṟpā rāṭṭa
		yāṅkuval lunaḷkol tāṉē tēmpeytu
	5.	maṇicey maṇṭait tīmpāl ēnti
		īṉāt tāyar maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ
		niḻaṟkayat taṉṉa nīḷnakar varaippiṉ
		emmuṭaic celvamum uḷḷāḷ poymmaruṇṭu
		pantupuṭaip paṉṉa pāṇip pallaṭic
	10.	cilparik kutiraip palvēl eḻiṉi
		keṭalaruri tuppiṉ viṭutoḻiṉ muṭimār
		kaṉaiyeri naṭanta kalkāy kāṉattu
		viṉaival ampiṉ viḻuttoṭai maṟavar
		tēmpiḻi naṟuṅkaḷ makiḻiṉ muṉaikaṭantu
	15.	vīṅkumeṉ curaiya ēṟṟiṉan tarūum
		mukaitalai tiṟanta vēṉil
		pakaitalai maṇanta pallatarc celavē.
- tāyaṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 4-8. maṇi cey maṇṭait tīmpāl tēm peytu ēnti - maṇikaḷiḻaitta poṟkalattil iṉiya pāloṭu tēṉ kalantu ēnti, īṉāt tāyar maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ - cevilit tāyar ūṭṭavum uṇṇāḷāya eṉmakaḷ, niḻaṟ kayattu aṉṉa nīḷ nakar varaippil - niḻaṟkaṇṇuḷḷa kuḷampōlak kuḷirnta nīṇṭa māḷikaiyiṭattēyuḷḷa, em uṭaic celvamum uḷḷāḷ - ematu periya celvattaiyum niṉaiyāḷāy;
1-3. akal aṟai malarnta arumpu mutir vēṅkai - akaṉṟa pāṟaiyil arumpu mutirntu malarnta vēṅkaiyiṉ, oḷilait toṭalai taii - oḷḷiya ilai virāviya mālaiyiṉai aṇintu, melleṉa - metteṉa, nalvarai nāṭaṉ taṉ pārāṭṭa - nalla malai nāṭaṉākiya talaivaṉ taṉṉaip pārāṭṭi vara;
8. poy maruṇṭu - ap poyyai meyyeṉa mayaṅki;
9-17. pantu puṭaippaṉṉa pāṇip pallaṭi - pantu puṭaikkap paṭṭu eḻuvatotta tāḷattuṭaṉ kūṭiya pala aṭiyīṭṭiṉaiyum, cil pari - cilavakaic celaviṉaiyumuṭaiya, kutirai - kutirai kaḷaiyum, palvēl - pala vēṟpaṭaiyiṉaiyu muṭaiya, keṭalarum tuppiṉ eḻiṉi - keṭutalillāta vali poruntiya eḻiṉi yeṉpāṉ, viṭu toḻil muṭimār - ēviviṭṭa toḻilai muṭikkum poruṭṭu, kaṉai eri naṭanta kalkāy kāṉattu - mikka eri parattaliṉ pāṟaikaḷ kāyntirukkum kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, viṉai val ampiṉ viḻuttoṭai maṟavar - pōrttiṟam vāynta ampiṉatu tappāta toṭuttalai yuṭaiya vīrarkaḷ, tēmpiḻi naṟuṅkaḷ makiḻiṉ - piḻinta tēṉāṟ camainta naṟiya kaḷḷuṇṭa makiḻcciyiṉāl, muṉai kaṭantu - pakaivar pōr muṉaiyai veṉṟa, vīṅku meṉ curaiya ēṟu iṉam  tarūum - parutta melliya maṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiyavum ēṟukaḷōṭu kūṭiyavu mākiya āṉiṉattaik koṇarntu tarum, pakai talai maṇanta - pakaivaruṭaṉ porutal kūṭiya, pal atar - palavākiya neṟikaḷil, mukaitalai tiṟanta vēṉil - malai muḻaiñcukaḷ veṭittaṟ kētuvākiya vēṉiṟ kālattē, celavu cellutalai;
4. yāṅku vallunaḷ kol - eṅṅaṉam vallaḷ āyiṉaḷē!
(muṭipu) īṉāttāyar tīmpāl tēm peytu maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ, emmuṭaic celvamum uḷḷāḷ, varai nāṭaṉ taṟ pārāṭṭa, poy maruṇṭu pakai talaimaṇanta pallataril, vēṉil celavu yāṅku vallunaḷ kol!
eḻiṉi viṭu toḻil muṭimār maṟavar, muṉai kaṭantu ēṟṟiṉam tarūum pakai talai maṇanta pallatar eṉka.
(vi-rai) melleṉa eṉṟataṉaic celavu eṉpataṉōṭu iyaittu uraittalumām. naṟṟāyiṉ vēṟu paṭukkac ceviliyai, īṉāt tāyar eṉṟār. cilpari - āti, maṇṭilam mutaliya celavu vakai. viṭutoḻil - ēviya toḻil; pakaivar āṉiṉattaik kavarntu varum toḻil; pakaivar kavarnta taṉatu niraiyai mīṭkum toḻilumām. kaḷ makiḻiṉ - kaḷḷaiyuṇṭu makiḻntāl eṉṟumām. vēṟṟu iṉam eṉap pirittu, pakaip pulattu iṉam eṉṟalumām. mukai - malaimuḻaiñcu. ēṟṟiṉam tarūum atar, pakai talaimaṇanta atar eṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka.
106. marutam
(talaimakaḷ taṉṉaip puṟaṅkūṟiṉāḷākak kēṭṭa parattai 
avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		eriyakain taṉṉa tāmaraip paḻaṉattup
		poriyakain taṉṉa poṅkupala ciṟumīṉ
		veṟikoḷ pācaṭai yuṇīiyar paippayap
		paṟaitapu mutuciral acaipuvan tirukkun
	5.	tuṟaikē ḻūraṉ peṇṭutaṉ koḻunaṉai
		nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa nāmatu
		ceyyā māyiṉum uyyā maiyiṉ
		ceṟitoṭi teḷirppa vīcic ciṟitavaṇ
		ulamantu varukam ceṉmō tōḻi
	10.	yoḷi ṟuvāṭ ṭāṉaik koṟṟac ceḻiyaṉ
		veḷiṟil kaṟpiṉ maṇṭamar aṭutoṟum
		kaḷiṟupeṟu valcip pāṇaṉ eṟiyum
		taṇṇumaik kaṇṇiṉ alaiiyartaṉ vayiṟē.
- ālaṅkuṭi vaṅkaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-;
1-6. eri akaintaṉṉa tāmaraip paḻaṉattu - tī kiḷait terintāṟ pōlum tāmaraip pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya vayalil, pori akaintaṉṉa poṅku pala ciṟu mīṉ uṇīiyar - neṟpori mutaliyaṉa teṟittāṟ pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum pala ciṟiya mīṉkaḷai uṇṇum poruṭṭu, veṟi koḷ pācaṭai - maṇaṅkoṇṭa paciya ilaiyil, paṟaitapu mutuciral - paṟattal oḻinta mutiya ciccilip paṟavai, paippaya acaipu vantirukkum - mella mella acaintu vantirukkum, tuṟaikēḻ ūraṉ peṇṭu - tuṟai poruntiya ūraṉiṉ maṉaivi, taṉ koḻunaṉai nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa - taṉ kaṇavaṉai nammoṭu kūṭṭi veṟuttup pēcukiṉṟāḷ eṉpar;
6-7. nām atu ceyyām āyiṉum uyyāmaiyiṉ - nām ataṟkētuvāya toṉṟum ceyyām āyiṉum avaḷ kūṟum paḻiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅka māṭṭāmaiyiṉ;
10-13. oḷiṟu vāḷ tāṉai koṟṟac ceḻiyaṉ - viḷaṅkum vāṭpaṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya veṟṟi poruntiya pāṇṭiyaṉ, veḷiṟu il kaṟpiṉ maṇṭu amar aṭutoṟum - kuṟṟamillāta paṭaip
payiṟciyoṭu kūṭi neruṅkiya pōril aṭuntōṟum, kaḷiṟu peṟu valcip pāṇaṉ eṟiyum - kaḷiṟāya uṇaviṉaip peṟum pāṇaṉ aṭikkum, taṇṇumaik kaṇṇiṉ - mattaḷattiṉ kaṇpōla, taṉ vayiṟu alai iyar - avaḷ taṉ vayiṟṟai alaikkumpaṭi;
8-9. ceṟi toṭi teḷirppa vīci - ceṟinta vaḷai olittiṭak kaiyai vīci, ciṟitu avaṇ ulamantu varukam - ciṟitu poḻutu avviṭattē ulāvi varuvōm, ceṉmō - varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! tuṟai kēḻ ūraṉ peṇṭu, taṉ koḻunaṉai nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa; nām atu ceyyā māyiṉum uyyāmaiyiṉ, ceḻiyaṉ amar aṭutoṟum pāṇaṉ eṟiyum taṇṇumaik kaṇṇiṉ, taṉ vayiṟu alaiiyar, ciṟitu avaṇ ulamantu varukam, ceṉmō.
(vi-rai) teḷirttal - olittal. ceṉmō - vārāy eṉṟapaṭi. mō; muṉṉilai yacai. kaṟpu - kalvi, paṭaikkalap payiṟci.
(u-ṟai) mutumaiyāl paṟakkamāṭṭāta ciral, mīṉukku aṇmaiyil pācaṭai mītiruntum ataṉaik kavaramāṭṭāmalum ēṉai yiḷañciral kavartaṟkup poṟāmalumiruttal pōl, mutumaiyāl eḻucci kuṉṟiya talaivi, taṉ maṉaiyakattē kaṇavaṉaik
koṇṭiruntum avaṉai vaḷaittukkoḷḷa māṭṭāmalum, ēṉai yiḷam paruvamuṭaiyār taḻuvutaṟkup poṟāmalum irukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu.
(mē-ḷ) `parattai vāyil'1  eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `ceṟitoṭi teḷirppa vīcic ciṟitivaṇ - ulamantu varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi' eṉṟakkāl talaivikkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac collik kūṟal vēṇṭu meṉpatu eṉṟaṉar, pērā.


107. pālai
(tōḻi talaimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintu vantu talaimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		nīcela vayarak kēṭṭoṟum palaniṉain
		taṉpiṉ neñcat tayāappoṟai melinta
		eṉṉakat tiṭumpai kaḷaimār niṉṉoṭu
		karuṅkal viyalaṟaik kiṭappi vayiṟutiṉ
	5.	ṟirumpuli tuṟanta ēṟṟumāṉ uṇaṅkal
		neṟicel vampalar uvantaṉar āṅkaṇ
		olikaḻai nelliṉ ariciyō ṭōrāṅ
		kāṉilaip paḷḷi aḷaipey taṭṭa
		vāṉiṇam urukkiya vāal veṇcōṟu
	10.	pukararait tēkkiṉ akalilai māntum
		kallā nīṇmoḻik katanāy vaṭukar
		vallāṇ arumuṉai nīnti allāntu
		ukumaṉū ṟañcum orukāṟ paṭṭat
		tiṉṉā ēṟṟat tiḻukki muṭaṅkūrn
	15.	torutaṉit toḻinta uraṉuṭai nōṉpaka
		ṭaṅkuḻai iruppai aṟaivāy vāṉpuḻal
		pulluḷaic ciṟāar villiṉ nīkki
		maraikaṭin tūṭṭum varaiyakac cīṟūr
		mālai iṉṟuṇai yākik kālaip
	20.	pacunaṉai naṟuvīp parūuppara luṟaippa
		maṇamaṉai kamaḻum kāṉam
		tuṇaiyī rōtiyeṉ tōḻiyum varumē.
- kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 22. (talaiva,) tuṇai īr ōti eṉ tōḻiyum - kaṭaiyotta kuḷirnta kūntalaiyuṭaiya eṉ tōḻiyum;
1-3. nī celavu ayarak kēṭṭoṟum - nī uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellutalai virumpa avviruppiṉaik kēṭkuntoṟum, aṉpiṉ neñcattup pala niṉaintu - aṉpiṉaiyuṭaiya neñcattu (muṉpu nikaḻnta) palavaṟṟaiyum eṇṇi (ataṉoṭu), ayāap poṟai melinta eṉ akattu iṭumpai kaḷaimār - varuttattait tāṅkuvatāl melintuḷa eṉ neñcattuḷḷa tuṉpiṉai nīkkum poruṭṭu;
4-12. irumpuli karuṅkal viyal aṟai kiṭappi - periya puliyāṉatu kariya kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟa pāṟaiyil kiṭatti, vayiṟu tiṉṟu tuṟanta ēṟṟumāṉ uṇaṅkal - vayiṟu niṟaiyat tiṉṟu viṭṭup pōṉa māṉēṟṟiṉ kāynta tacaiyai, neṟi cel vampalar- vaḻic celvōrāya putiyar, uvantaṉar āṅkaṇ - kaṇṭu makiḻn taṉarāy (ataṉai) avviṭattē, oli kaḻai nelliṉ ariciyōṭu ōrāṅku - taḻaitta mūṅkiliṉ viḷainta nelliṉ ariciyuṭaṉ oruṅkē kūṭṭi, āṉilaip paḷḷi aḷaipeytu aṭṭa - āyar cēriyiluḷḷa tayiriṉaip peytu camaitta, vāl niṇam urukkiya vāl veṇcōṟu - veḷḷiya niṇattiṉai urukacceyta mika veḷḷiya cōṟṟiṉai, pukar arait tēkkiṉ akal ilai māntum - puḷḷi poruntiya araiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tēkka marattiṉatu akaṉṟa ilaiyil vaittu uṇṇuvatum, kallā nīḷmoḻik kata nāy vaṭukar - kalviyillāta neṭumoḻi kūṟum ciṉam mikka nāyaiyuṭaiya vaṭukaratu, val āṇ arumuṉai - valiya āṇmai viḷaṅkum ariya pōr muṉaiyāvatumākiya curattiṉai, nīnti - kaṭantu ceṉṟu;
13-15. uku maṇ ūṟu añcum oru kāl paṭṭattu - iṭintu viḻum maṇṇiṉ iṭaiyūṟṟiṉai añcuvatāṉa orē tuṟaiyiṉai yuṭaiya ōṭaiyiluḷḷa, iṉṉā ēṟṟattu iḻukki muṭam kūrntu - iṉṉātatākiya ēṟṟaṅkoṇṭa neṟiyil vaḻukki viḻuntu mikka muṭampaṭṭu, oru taṉittu oḻinta uraṉ uṭai nōṉ pakaṭu - taṉṉan taṉiyē oḻintu kiṭakkum uṭalvali vāynta poṟukkun taṉmaiyuṭaiya pakaṭṭiṉai;
16-21. pul uḷaic ciṟār - pulliya kuṭumiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ciṟuvar, am kuḻai iruppai aṟai vāy vāṉ puḻal - aḻakiya taḷiraiyuṭaiya iruppaiyiṉ aṟṟa vāyiṉaiyum veḷḷiya tuḷaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya pūvai, villiṉ nīkki - villiṉāl utirttu, marai kaṭintu ūṭṭum - ataṉait tiṉṉavarum maraimāṉait  turantu uṇpikkum, varai akac cīṟūr - varaiyiṭattē yuḷḷa ciṟiya ūrkaḷil, mālai iṉtuṇai āki - mālaik kālattē niṉakku iṉiya tuṇaiyākit taṅki, kālai pacu naṉai naṟu vī - kālaip pōtil putiya tēṉaiyuṭaiya naṟiya pūkkaḷ, parūup paral uṟaippa - periya pāṟaik kaṟkaḷil utirntu kiṭattalāṉ, maṇa maṉai kamaḻuṅ kāṉam - maṇam nikaḻum maṉai pōṉṟu nāṟuṅ kāṭṭil;
3. niṉṉoṭu - niṉṉuṭaṉ;
22. varum - vārā niṟpaḷ.
(muṭipu) talaiva! nī celavu ayarak kēṭṭoṟum eṉ tōḻiyum, pala niṉaintu, eṉṉakattu iṭumpai kaḷaimār, vampalar veṇcōṟu māntuvatum vaṭukar arumuṉaiyāvatu mākiya curattiṉai nīnti varaiyakac cīṟūr mālai iṉṟuṇai yāki, kālai maṇamaṉai kamaḻum kāṉam niṉṉoṭu varum.
(vi-rai) vayiṟu tiṉṟu - vayiṟu niṟaiyat tiṉṟu. aṭṭa veṇcōṟu eṉavum, vāl niṇa murukkiya veṇcōṟu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. aṭutalāl niṇam urukiya teṉka. vampalar veṇcōṟu māntum muṉai eṉavum, vaṭukar aṭumuṉai eṉavum taṉittaṉi iyaiyum, pakaṭu - pakaṭṭiṉai. orukāṟ paṭṭam - orē tuṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ōṭai. puḻal, pūviṟku ākupeyar. mālai iṉtuṇai yāki eṉpataṟku - mālaip poḻutil nī iṉiya tuṇai yākaiyālē eṉṟuraittalumām. itaṟku āki eṉṟataṉai āka eṉat tirikka.
(u-ṟai) ōṭaiyilē nīruṇṭu atiṉiṉṟum ēṟamāṭṭāta muṭampaṭṭa uraṉuṭaip pakaṭṭiṟkuc ciṟār iruppaip pūvai marattiṉiṉṟum pirittu maraiyiṉaik kaṭintu ūṭṭiṉāṟpōla, kaḷavoḻukkamākiya iṉpattai nukarntu, ivvoḻukkat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅki varaivoṭu puka māṭṭātu varuntum niṉakkuk kuṟṟēvaṉ makaḷākiya yāṉ talaimakaḷaic cuṟṟattiṉiṉṟum pirittu notumalar varaiviṉai māṟṟi uṭaṉ pōkkiṟku oruppaṭuttā niṉṟēṉ eṉpatu.
108. kuṟiñci
(talaimakaṉ ciṟaippuṟattāṉākat talaimakaṭkuc 
colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu.)
		puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum uṇarntōrk
		kottaṉṟu maṉṉāl evaṉkol muttam
		varaimutal citaṟi yavai1pōl yāṉaip
		pukarmukam poruta putunīr āli
	5.	paḷiṅkucori vatupōl pāṟai varippak
		kārkatam paṭṭa kaṇṇakaṉ vicumpiṉ
		viṭupoṟi ñekiḻiyil koṭipaṭa miṉṉip
		paṭumaḻai poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		āruyirt tuppil kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum
	10.	iruḷiṭait tamiyaṉ varutal yāvatum
		aruḷāṉ vāḻi tōḻi alkal
		viravuppoṟi maññai verīi yaraviṉ
		aṇaṅkuṭai yaruntalai paivirip pavaipōl
		kāyā meṉciṉai tōya nīṭip
	15.	paḵṟuṭup peṭutta alaṅkukulaik kāntaḷ
		aṇimalar naṟuntā tūtun tumpi
		kaiyāṭu vaṭṭil tōṉṟum
		maiyāṭu ceṉṉiya malaikiḻa vōṉē.
- taṅkāṟ poṟkollaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi vāḻi-,
12-18. viravup poṟi maññai verīi - puḷḷikaḷ viraviya mayilaik kaṇ ṭañci, araviṉ aṇaṅkuṭai arum talai pai virippavai pōl - pāmpiṉ varuttattaic ceyyum ariya nañciṉaiyuṭaiya talaikaḷ paṭattai virippaṉa pōla, kāyā meṉciṉai tōya - kāyāviṉ melliya ciṉaikaḷ tōya, nīṭip pal tuṭuppu eṭutta alaṅku kulaik kāntaḷ aṇi malar - nīṇṭu tūkkiya pala tuṭuppukaḷ pōlum acaiyum kulaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāntaḷatu aḻakiya malariṉ, naṟum tātu ūtum tumpi - naṟiya tātiṉaik kuṭaintuṇṇum vaṇṭukaḷ, kai āṭu vaṭṭil tōṉṟum - kaiyiṉkaṇ vaittu āṭum vaṭṭup pōlat tōṉṟum, mai āṭu ceṉṉiya malai kiḻavōṉ - mēkam tavaḻum cimaiyaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya malaikku uriyaṉākiya nam talaivaṉ;
2-11. muttam varai mutal citaṟiyavai pōl - malaittalaiyil muttukkaḷ citaṟiyavai pōla viḷaṅkum, yāṉaip pukar mukam poruta putu nīr āli - yāṉaiyiṉ puḷḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mukattil mōti vīḻnta putiya ālaṅkaṭṭi, paḷiṅku corivatu pōl pāṟai varippa - paḷiṅkiṉaic corivatu pōlap pāṟaiyil vīḻntu kōlam ceyya, kār katam paṭṭa kaṇ akal vicumpil - mukilkaḷ ciṉanteḻuntaṉavāya iṭamakaṉṟa vāṉil, viṭu poṟi ñekiḻiyiṉ koṭi paṭa miṉṉi - poṟiviṭukiṉṟa koḷḷi pōl oḻuṅkupaṭa miṉṉi, paṭu maḻai poḻinta pāṉāḷ kaṅkul - mikka maḻaiyaic corinta naṭu nāḷākiya iravil, ār uyir tuppiṉ - ariya uyirp poruḷākiya uṇaviṉaiyuṭaiya, kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum iruḷiṭai - kollum toḻiluṭaiya vilaṅkukaḷ tiriyum iruḷiṭaiyē, alkal tamiyaṉ varutal yāvatum aruḷāṉ - nāṭōṟum taṉiyē varutalāṉē ciṟitum aruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉallaṉ;
1-2. puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum - tammaic cārntōratu tuṉpiṉaip pōkkiyaruḷalum, uṇarntōrkku ottaṉṟu - aṟivuṭaiyārkkup poruntiyatākumē, maṉ - nam talaivaṉ aṅṅaṉam ceytilaṉ, evaṉkol - eṉṉaiyō?
(muṭipu) malai kiḻavōṉākiya nam talaivaṉ pāṉāṭ kaṅkul kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum iruḷiṭai alkal tamiyaṉ varutalāṉē yāvatum aruḷāṉ; puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum uṇarntōrkku ottaṉṟu maṉ; evaṉ kol?
(vi-rai) puṉkaṇ aruḷalum - puṉkaṇṇai nīkki yaruḷalum eṉka; maṉ : oḻiyicai. varaiyiṭattuc citaṟiya muttam pōl yāṉai mukattiṟ poruta āli eṉpatum, maññai verīi araviṉ talai pai virippavai pōl kāyā meṉ ciṉai tōya nīṭi eṭutta kulaik kāntaḷ eṉpatum, kai yāṭu vaṭṭil pōlak kāntaḷ malarai yūtum tumpi tōṉṟum eṉpatum iraṭṭaik kiḷaviyākiya uvamaṅkaḷām. āli - nīrkkaṭṭi; nīr eṉa muṉ vantamaiyiṉ īṇṭup peyar māttiraiyāka niṉṟatu. mukil kumuṟi eḻuntataik katampaṭṭatu eṉṟār. kōṇmā; ciṅkam mutaliyaṉa; oruvakai vilaṅkumām. eṉṉai? `accap poruḷāvaṉa : vaḷḷeyiṟ ṟarimā vāḷvari vēṅkai, muḷḷeyiṟ ṟaravē muḻaṅkaḻaṟ centī, īṟṟā matamā ēka pātam, kūṟṟaṅ kōṇmā kuṉṟuṟai yacuṇam eṉṟu collap paṭṭaṉa pōlvaṉa' eṉṟār pērāciriyar ākaliṉ. varutal - varutalāl eṉa urupu virikka. kāyāviṉ meṉciṉaikku maññaiyum, tūkkiya kāntaṭ kulaikkup pāmpu paṭam virittatum uvamam. tumpikku vaṭṭu, vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, toḻil eṉṉum mūṉṟum paṟṟiya uvamaiyām.
(u-ṟai) kaṇṭār virumpattakka kāntaṭkulai añcattakka araviṉ paṭam pōlum eṉṟatu, nammāl virumpattakka talaivaṉ varukai, āṟṟatu tīmaiyāl, namakku accattai viḷaivikkiṉṟatu eṉṟu talaivaṉ uṇarumāṟu tōḻi kūṟiyapaṭiyām.
(mē-ḷ) `viraviyum varūum marapiṉa veṉpa'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `kāyā meṉciṉai....... malaikiḻa vōṉē' eṉpuḻi, āṭutaṟṟoḻil paṟṟiyum, vaṭivupaṟṟiyum, vaṇṇampaṟṟiyum (uvamai) vantatu eṉpar, pērā.
109. pālai
(iṭaiccurattut talaimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		pallitaḻ meṉmalar uṇkaṇ nalyāḻ
		narampicait taṉṉa iṉṟīṅ kiḷavi
		nalanal korutti irunta ūrē
		kōṭuḻu kaḷiṟṟiṉ toḻuti yīṇṭik
	5.	kāṭukāl yātta nīṭumarac cōlai
		viḻaiveḷil āṭuṅ kaḻaivaḷar naṉantalai
		2veṇṇuṉai yampiṉ vicaiyiṭa vīḻntōr
		eṇṇuvaram paṟiyā uvaliṭu patukkaic
		curaṅkeḻu kavalai kōṭpāṟ paṭṭeṉa
	10.	vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam iṟantōr
		kaipporuḷ illaiyāyiṉum 3meykkoṇ
		ṭiṉṉuyir cekāar viṭṭakal tappaṟkup
		peruṅkaḷiṟṟu maruppoṭu variyataḷ iṟukkum
		aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum
	15.	vaṟaṉuṟu kuṉṟam palavilaṅ kiṉavē.
- kaṭuntoṭaik kāviṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. pal itaḻ mel malar uṇ kaṇ - pala itaḻ kaḷaiyuṭaiya melliya malar pōṉṟa maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaiyum, nalyāḻ narampu icaittaṉṉa iṉ tīm kiḷavi - nalla yāḻiṉ narampu olippatu pōṉṟa mika iṉiya moḻiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, nalam nalku orutti - nalaṉellām taravalla oppaṟṟa nam kātali, irunta ūr - irukkum ūrāṉatu;
4-10. kōṭu uḻu kaḷiṟṟiṉ toḻuti īṇṭi - tam kompiṉāl pakaiyāyavaṟṟaik kutti uḻum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉaikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭa, kāṭu kāl yātta nīṭu maraccōlai - kāṭākap paranta nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya cōlaiyiluḷḷa, viḻai veḷil āṭum kaḻaivaḷar  naṉantalai - oṉṟai yoṉṟu viḻainta aṇilkaḷ kūṭi āṭum mūṅkil vaḷarnta akaṉṟaviṭattē, veḷ nuṉai ampiṉ vicai iṭa vīḻntōr - niṇam tōytalāl veḷḷiya muṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ampiṉ vēkam paṭa iṟantōrkaḷiṉ, eṇṇu varampu aṟiyā uval iṭu patukkai - eṇṇiṉ ellai aṟiya lākāta taḻaiyiṭṭu mūṭiya patukkaikaḷaiyuṭaiya, curam keḻu kavalai kōḷpāl paṭṭeṉa - curattiṟ poruntiya kavar neṟikaḷ āṟalaikkum pakutiyiṟ koḷḷap peṟṟatāka, vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam - vaḻippōvār illaiyākiya ac cura neṟiyil;
10-15. iṟantōr kaip poruḷ illai yāyiṉum - aṟiyātu vantōr kaiyiṟ poruḷ illaiyāyiṉum, mey koṇṭu iṉ uyir cekār viṭṭu akal tappaṟku - avartam meyyaip paṟṟik koṇṭu avar iṉiya uyiriṉaik kollārāy viṭṭu akaṉṟu vanta piḻaikkāka, perum kaḷiṟṟu maruppoṭu vari ataḷ iṟukkum - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ kompoṭu puliyiṉ vari poruntiya tōlait taṇṭamākat taṉ ēvalarai iṟukkac ceyyum, aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum vaṟaṉ uṟu kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa - aṟaṉillāta aracaṉ āḷum vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa kuṉṟukaḷ pala kuṟukkiṭṭuḷḷaṉavē; (eṉṟu talaivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ).
(muṭipu) nalam nalku orutti irunta ūr, aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa.
cōlaiyai yuṭaiya akaṉṟa iṭattil vīḻntōratu patukkaikaḷ poruntiya curaṅ keḻu kavalai kōṭpāṟ paṭṭeṉa, vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam eṉṟum, attam iṟantōr uyir cekāar viṭṭakal tappaṟku maruppoṭu vari ataḷ iṟukkum aṟaṉil vēntaṉ eṉṟum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-rai) īṇṭi - īṇṭa eṉat tirikka. kāl yāttal - parattal. kōṭpāl tīṅkiṉ pakuti; eṉṟatu āṟalaikkum pakuti iṟukkum - ēvalarai iṟukkac ceyyum eṉa viritturaikka. aṟaṉil vēntaṉ eṉṟatu - kāṭṭiṉai āḷum talaivaṉai. kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa vātaliṉ ittuṇaiyum pōntu iṉi vaṟitē mīḷutal ollā teṉṟāṉ eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `aṟaṉil vēnta ṉāḷum, vaṟaṉuṟu kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉavē' eṉa kāṭṭut talaivaṉai nāṭṭut talaivaṉ peyarāṟ kūṟiṉar eṉṟaṉar, nac.
110. neytal
(tōḻi cevilittāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.)
		aṉṉai aṟiyiṉum aṟika alarvāy
		ammeṉ cēri kēṭpiṉuṅ kēṭka
		piṟitoṉ ṟiṉmai aṟiyak kūṟik
		koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyvam nōkkik
	5.	kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval niṉakkē2 kāṉal
		toṭalai yāyamoṭu kaṭaluṭaṉ āṭiyuñ
		ciṟṟil iḻaittum ciṟucōṟu kuvaiiyum
		varuntiya varuttam tīra yāñciṟi
		tiruntaṉa māka eyta vantu
	10.	taṭameṉ paṇaittōḷ maṭanal līrē
		ellum elliṉ ṟacaivumika uṭaiyēṉ
		mellilaip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik
		kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō
		eṉamoḻin taṉaṉē oruvaṉ avaṟkaṇ
	15.	ṭiṟaiñciya mukattem puṟañcērpu porunti
		ivainumak kuriya valla iḻinta
		koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam iḻumeṉa
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum nāvāy tōṉṟuva
		kāṇā mōveṉak kāliṟ citaiyā
	20.	nillātu peyarnta pallō ruḷḷum
		eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu naṉṉutāl
		oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi
		yāṉpeyar keṉṉa nōkkit tāṉtaṉ
		neṭuntērk koṭiñci paṟṟi
	25.	niṉṟōṉ pōlum 3iṉṟumeṉ kaṭkē.
- pōntaip pacalaiyār.
(co-ḷ) 5-9. toṭalai āyamoṭu kaṭal uṭaṉ āṭiyum - mālaipōṉṟa āyattārōṭu kaṭalil oruṅku viḷaiyāṭiyum, kāṉal ciṟṟil iḻaittum - kaṭaṟkaraic cōlaiyil ciṟu vīṭu kaṭṭiyum, ciṟu cōṟu kuvaiiyum - ciṟu cōṟṟai aṭṭuk kuvittum, varuntiya varuttam tīra - iṅṅaṉam varuntiya varuttam nīṅka, yām ciṟitu iruntaṉamāka - yām ciṟitu iḷaippāṟi yiruntēmāka (atupōtu);
9-14. oruvaṉ eyta vantu - oru talaivaṉ emmiṭam aṇuka vantu, taṭameṉ paṇait tōḷ maṭa nallīrē - periya melliya mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉaiyuṭaiya maṭappam vāynta nallīrē, ellum elliṉṟu - pakalum oḷi iḻantatu, acaivu mika uṭaiyēṉ - taḷarcci mikavum uṭaiyēṉ, mel ilaip parappiṉ viruntu uṇṭu yāṉum - yāṉum melliya ilaip parappiṉ nīyir iṭum viruntuṇaviṉai uṇṭu, ik kal eṉ ciṟu kuṭit taṅkiṉ evaṉō - intak kalleṉṟa āravāramuṭaiya ciṟukuṭiyil taṅkiṉāl eytum kuṟai yātu? eṉa moḻintaṉaṉ - eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ;
14-17. avaṉ kaṇṭu iṟaiñciya mukattem - avaṉaik kaṇṭu kaviḻnta mukattēmāy, puṟam cērpu porunti - maṟaivāṉa iṭattē cērntiruntu, iḻum eṉa - iḻumeṉṟa melliya kuralil, ivai numakku uriya alla - ivvuṇavu numakku ēṟṟaṉa alla, iḻinta koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam - iḻivāya koḻumīṉālāya uṇavu eṉṟu kūṟiṉēm: (piṉṉar;)
18-22. neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum nāvāy tōṉṟuva - nīṇṭa koṭikaḷ acaiyāniṟkum nāvāykaḷ tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟai, kāṇāmō eṉa- yām kāṇpē mallēmō eṉṟu kūṟi, kāliṉ citaiyā - em ciṟṟil ciṟu cōṟukaḷaik kālāṟ citaittu viṭṭu, nillātu peyarnta pallōruḷḷum -aṅku nillātu pōya palaruḷḷum, eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu - avaṉ eṉṉaiyē kuṟittu nōkkiya pārvaiyoṭu, nal nutāl oḻikō yāṉ eṉa aḻitakak kūṟi - nalla neṟṟiyiṉai yuṭaiyāḷē cellukō eṉṟu eṉ neñcam aḻintiṭak kūṟa;
23-25. yāṉ peyarka eṉṉa - yāṉ celka eṉṉalum, nōkki - (peyarātu eṉṉai) nōkkiyavaṉāy, tāṉ taṉ neṭun tērk koṭiñci paṟṟi - avaṉ taṉatu nīṇṭa tēriṉ koṭiñciyiṉaip paṟṟik koṇṭu, niṉṟōṉ - niṉṟaṉaṉ, iṉṟum eṉ kaṭkup pōlum - iṉṟum eṉ kaṇ muṉ niṟpatu annilaiyē pōlum;
1-5. aṉṉai aṟiyiṉum aṟika - itaṉai aṉṉai aṟiyiṉum aṟivāḷāka, alar vāy a meṉ cēri kēṭpiṉum kēṭka - alar kūṟum vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya am melliya cēriyiṉar kēṭpiṉum kēṭṭiṭuka, piṟitu oṉṟum iṉmai aṟiyak kūṟi - vēṟoṉṟum illāmaiyai nī yaṟiyak kūṟi, koṭuñ cuḻip pukārt teyvam nōkki - vaḷainta cuḻikaḷ mēviya pukāriṭattuḷḷa teyvattai nōkki, niṉakkuk kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuvaṉ - niṉakkuk kaṭiya cūḷ ceytu taruvaṉ.
(muṭipu) kāṉal toṭalai āyamoṭu varuntiya varuttam tīra yām ciṟitu iruntaṉam; oruvaṉ, acaivu mika uṭaiyēṉ, viruntuṇṭu ic ciṟu kuṭit taṅkiṉ evaṉō eṉa moḻintaṉaṉ; iḻinta koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam; nillātu peyarnta pallōruḷḷum eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi, yāṉ peyarka eṉṉa, koṭiñci paṟṟi niṉṟōṉ pōlum iṉṟum eṉ kaṭkē; (itaṉai) aṉṉai aṟiyiṉum aṟika; cēri kēṭpiṉum kēṭka. piṟitoṉṟiṉmai aṟiyak kūṟip pukārt teyvam nōkki niṉakkuk kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuvaṉ.
(vi-rai) `aṉṉai eṉṟatu naṟṟāyai. cēri: ākupeyar. talaiviyuṟṟa nōykkuk kāraṇam piṟitoṉṟu miṉmaiyai eṉka, piṟitoṉṟum eṉṉum muṟṟummai tokkatu. maṟṟevaṉō: maṟṟu yāṉ eṉṉūrkkuc celluva toḻintu eṉap poruḷ tarutaliṉ viṉaimāṟṟu. eṉṉē - eṉṉaiyē. kūṟi, kūṟa eṉat tirikka. niṉṟōṉ pōlum : niṉṟōṉ eṉa muṟṟākki annilaiyē pōlum eṉa viritturaikka. eṉṟu meṉ makaṭkē eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavaṉē eṉṟum eṉ makaṭku uriyaṉ pōlumeṉa aṉṉai aṟiyiṉum aṟika eṉa iyaitturaikka.
kaṭuñcūḷ eṉpataṉai, kaṭi eṉṉum uriccol muṉṉēṟṟup poruḷil vantataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭi, muṉṉēṟṟu, puṟattilaṉṟit `teyva mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉiṉṟu teḷittal'1 eṉpar, cēṉā.
mēlellām āyattāraiyum talaiviyaiyum uḷappaṭuttik kūṟiya tōḻi piṉṉar `nillātu peyarnta pallē muḷḷum'2 eṉṉātu, pallōruḷḷu meṉat taṉṉai vēṟu pirittuk kūṟiyatu avarellām talaivaṉukku iraṅkātu ceṉṟamaiyum, tāṉ maṭṭum taṉittu niṉṟamaiyum tōṉṟak kūṟiyatākum eṉpar, nac.
(mē-ḷ) `māyōṉ mēya'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, neytaṉilattil nuḷaiyarkku valaivaḷam tappiṉ am makaḷir kiḷaiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cuṟavukkōṭu naṭṭup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttaliṉ āṇṭu varuṇaṉ veḷippaṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyvam nōkkik kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval eṉa varum eṉṟum `meytoṭṭup payiṟal'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `mellilaip parappiṉ viruntuṇṭi yāṉumik, kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉ maṟṟevaṉō' eṉa varuvatu `ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum.... kūṟi' eṉṟa cūttirap pakutiyil, piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka eṉṟum, ac cūttirattut `taṇṭātu irappiṉum' eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ, `ellum.... oruvaṉ eṉat `talaivaṉ iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiyataṉait tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka' eṉṟum, `eḷitta lēttal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `ellum.... oruvaṉ' itu eḷittal eṉṟum, accūttirattu, piṟitoṉṟiṉmai..... kaṭuñcū ṭarukuva ṉiṉakkē' itu talaippāṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
`kaṇṇiṉuñ ceviyiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi'3 eṉpuḻi, talaimakaṉ maṉattu nikaḻnta aḻivellām oḻikō yāṉ eṉṟa uraiyāṉē uṇarntamaiyiṉ atu ceviyuṇarvu eṉappaṭum eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, pērā.
111. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ tōḻi talaimakaḷai yāṟṟuvittatu.)
		uḷḷāṅ kuvattal cellār kaṟuttōr
		eḷḷal neñcat tēeccol nāṇi
		varuvar vāḻi tōḻi yaraca
		yāṉai koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla
	5.	alantalai ñemaiyattu valanta cilampi
		ōṭaik kuṉṟattuk kōṭaiyoṭu tuyalvara
		maḻaiyeṉa maruṇṭa mammar palavuṭaṉ
		oykaḷi ṟeṭutta nōyuṭai neṭuṅkai
		tokucoṟ kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum
	10.	attak kēḻal aṭṭa naṟkōḷ
		cennāy ēṟṟai kammeṉa īrppak
		kuruti yārum eruvaic ceñcevi
		maṇṭama raḻuvat tellik koṇṭa
		puṇtēr viḷakkiṉ tōṉṟum
	15.	viṇtōy piṟaṅkal malaiyiṟan tārē.
- pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 3. tōḻi vāḻi-;
1-2. uḷḷāṅku uvattal cellār - tamakkup poruḷ uḷḷa aḷaviṟku makiḻta lilarāy, kaṟuttōr eḷḷal neñcattu ēec col nāṇi - pakaivar ikaḻum neñcattuṭaṉ kūṟum ampu pōlum colliṟku nāṇiṉarāy;
3-6. araca yāṉai koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla - paṭṭattu yāṉai taṉmītu koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla, ōṭaik kuṉṟattu - ōṭai eṉṉum kuṉṟattuḷḷa, alantalai ñemaiyattu - kāynta talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ñemai marattiṉmītu, valanta cilampi - piṉṉiya cilampiyiṉ kūṭāṉatu, kōṭaiyoṭu tuyalvara - mēl kāṟṟāl acaiya;
7-15. maḻai eṉa maruṇṭa - ataṉai mēkam eṉa mayaṅkiya, mammar oy kaḷiṟu pala uṭaṉ - mayakkattiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaitta kaḷiṟukaḷ palavum oruṅkē, eṭutta nōyuṭai neṭuṅ kai - tūkkiya varuttattiṉaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa kaikaḷ, toku col kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum attam - pukaḻiṉait tiraṭṭik kūṟum kūttaratu tūmpiṉaip pōlat tōṉṟi olikkum kāṭṭiluḷḷa, kēḻal aṭṭa nalkōḷ cennāy ēṟṟai - āṇ paṉṟiyiṉaik koṉṟa taṉ iraiyiṉai naṉku paṟṟikkoḷḷum tiṟaṉuṭaiya cennāyiṉ ēṟṟai, kam eṉa īrppa - viraivāka ataṉai iḻuttiṭa, kuruti ārum eruvaic ceñcevi - vaḻiyelām oḻukiya kurutiyiṉaip parukum paruntiṉ civanta cevikaḷ, maṇṭu amar aḻuvattu ellik koṇṭa puṇtēr viḷakkiṉ tōṉṟum - mikka pōrkkaḷap parappil irāppoḻutil kaikkoṇṭa vīrarkaḷatu marumattup puṇkaḷai ārāyum viḷakkukaḷaip pōṉṟu tōṉṟum, viṇtōy piṟaṅkal malai iṟantōr - vāṉait tōyum viḷakkattaiyuṭaiya malaiyiṉaik kaṭantu poruḷ īṭṭac ceṉṟōrāya nam talaivar;
3. varuvar - viraiya varuvar (eṉat tōḻi talaiviyai āṟṟuvittāḷ.)
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi! uvattal cellār, ēec col nāṇi malaiyiṟantōr varuvar.
(vi-rai) uḷḷāṅku - uḷḷapaṭi, ēeccol - perumitattuṭaṉ kūṟum collumām. alantatalai alantalai yeṉa vikārappaṭṭatu. ñemaiyattu, attu : cāriyai. ōṭaik kuṉṟattiṟku aracayāṉaiyum, ñemaiyattu valanta cilampik kūṭṭiṟkut tukiṟ koṭiyum uvamam. uṇṭaṟku nīriṉṟi melinta kaḷiṟu mēluḷḷa cilampikkūṭṭai mēkameṉa maruṇṭu atil nīruṇṭaṟkuk kaiyai mēlē eṭutta teṉka. kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ oliyum yāṉaiyiṉ uyirppoliyum oru taṉmaiyaṉa eṉpatu, `kaṇṇiṭai viṭutta kaḷiṟṟuyirt tūmpiṉ'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟika. uyirkkum attam eṉavum, attattil kēḻalaṭṭa ēṟṟai īrppa ataṉ kurutiyai ārum eruvai eṉavum iyaiyum. kuruti paṭinta attam amarkkaḷam pōlāka, eruvaic ceñcevi āṇṭuḷḷa viḷakkup pōlat tōṉṟum.
kammeṉa : ticaic col.
(u-ṟai) cilampik kūṭṭiṉai mēkameṉak karutum yāṉaiyaik kāṇum talaivar, tām poruḷallataṉaip poruḷeṉak karutiyataṉai uṇarntu viraiya mīḷvar eṉṟapaṭi.
112. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talaimakaṉai yetirppaṭṭu niṉṟu 
tōḻi colli varaivu kaṭāyatu.)
		1kūṉal eṇkiṉ kuṟunaṭait toḻuti
		citalai ceyta cennilaip puṟṟiṉ
		maṇpuṉai neṭuṅkō ṭuṭaiya vāṅki
		irainacaiip parikkum araināṭ kaṅkul
	5.	īṉṟaṇi vayavuppiṇap pacitteṉa maṟappuli
		oḷiṟēntu maruppiṉ kaḷiṟaṭṭuk kuḻumum
		paṉiyiruñ cōlai yemiyam eṉṉāy
		tīṅkucey taṉaiyē yīṅkuvan tōyē
		nāḷiṭaip paṭiṉeṉ ṟōḻi vāḻāḷ
	10.	tōḷiṭai muyakkam nīyum veyyai
		2kaḻiyak kātalar āyiṉum cāṉṟōr
		paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār
		varaiyiṉ evaṉō vāṉtōy veṟpa
		kaṇakkalai ikukkum kaṟiyivar cilampiṉ
	15.	maṇapparuṅ kāmam puṇarntamai aṟiyār
		toṉṟiyal marapiṉ maṉṟal ayarap
		peṇkōḷ oḻukkam kaṇkoḷa nōkki
		notumal viruntiṉam pōlavivaḷ
		putunāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam yāmē.
- neytaṟ cāyttuytta āvūr kiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 13. vāṉtōy veṟpa - vāṉai aḷāvum malaiyai yuṭaiya talaivaṉē!,
1-4. kūṉal kuṟunaṭai eṇkiṉ toḻuti - kūṉiya mutukiṉaiyum kuṟukakkuṟuka aṭiyiṭṭu naṭakkum naṭaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya karaṭik kūṭṭam, citalai ceyta cennilaip puṟṟiṉ - kaṟaiyāṉ eṭutta civanta nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya puṟṟiṉatu, maṇpuṉai neṭuṅkōṭu uṭaiya vāṅki - maṇṇāṟ puṉainta nīṇṭa ucci uṭaintiṭap peyarttu, irai nacaiip parikkum araināḷ kaṅkul - puṟṟāñcōṟāya iraiyiṉai virumpic cūḻntu koṇṭirukkum naṭunāḷ iravil;
5-8. īṉṟa aṇi vayavu piṇa pacitteṉa - kuṭṭiyai īṉṟa aṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vēṭkaiyaiyuṭaiya peṇpuli pacittatāka, maṟappuli - taṟukaṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya āṇpuli, oḷiṟu ēntu maruppiṉ kaḷiṟu aṭṭuk kuḻumum - oḷi vīcukiṉṟa ēntiya kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya āṇ yāṉaiyaik koṉṟu muḻaṅkukiṉṟa, paṉi irumcōlai - kuḷirnta kariya cōlaiyil, emiyam eṉṉāy - yām tamiyēm eṉṟu niṉaiyāyāy, tīṅku ceytaṉaiyē īṅku vantōyē - īṅku varutaliṉ nī tīṅku ceytāy āvāy;
9-10. nāḷ iṭaippaṭiṉ eṉ tōḻi vāḻāḷ - nī vārātu oru nāḷ iṭaiyī ṭuṟiṉum eṉtōḻi uyir vāḻāḷkāṇ, tōḷiṭai muyakkam nīyum veyyai - aṅṅaṉē nāḷum eṉ tōḻiyiṉ tōḷiṉ kaṇ muyaṅkutalai nīyum virumputaluṭaiyai;
11-12. cāṉṟōr kaḻiyak kātalar āyiṉum - cāṉṟōrāvār mikak kātal koṇṭār āyiṭiṉum, paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār - paḻiyuṭaṉ kūṭi varum iṉpiṉai virumpār ākaliṉ;
13. varaiyiṉ evaṉ - nī varaintu koḷḷiṉ kuṟaiyāvatu eṉṉai? (aṅṅaṉam varaintiṭiṉ;)
14-16. kaṇam kalai ikukkum kaṟi ivar cilampil - kūṭṭa mākiya kalaimāṉkaḷ tāḻa olikkum miḷakuk koṭi paṭarnta malaiccāralil, maṇappu arum kāmam puṇarntamai aṟiyār - eytutaṟku ariya kāmattāl nīr kūṭiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉai aṟiyāta emar, toṉṟu iyal marapiṉ maṉṟal ayara - toṉṟu toṭṭu varum muṟaippaṭi vatuvai nikaḻttiṭa;
17-19. peṇkōḷ oḻukkam kaṇkoḷa nōkki - nī ivaḷai varaintukoḷḷum avvoḻukkattiṉaik kaṇṇāra nōkki, yām notumal viruntiṉam pōla - yām ayalēmākiya putiyēm pōla, ivaḷ putunāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam - ivaḷ putiya nāṇālākiya oṭukkattiṉaiyum kāṇpē maṉṟō?
(muṭipu) arai nāṭ kaṅkul puli kaḷiṟu aṭṭuk kuḻumum cōlaiyil īṅku vantōy tīṅku ceytaṉai; nāḷ iṭaippaṭiṉ eṉ tōḻi vāḻāḷ; tōḷiṭai muyakkam nīyum veyyai; cāṉṟōr paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār; varaiyiṉ evaṉ? maṉṟal ayara ap peṇkōḷoḻukkattai nōkki yām notumal viruntiṉam pōla ivaḷ putu nāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam.
(vi-rai) `kūraleṇkiṉ' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku, vaḷainta uṭampiṉaiyuṭaiya karaṭiyiṉ eṉpatē poruḷākum. aṇi - aṇimaiyuṭaiya. piṇā eṉpatu piṇa eṉṟāyatu, `kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉaikeṭa vukaram - aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, aṟiya eṉṉum ilēcāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. kaḻiyāk kātalar eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, nīṅkāta kātalaiyuṭaiyār eṉka. maṉṟal nikaḻttum tamarāvār, irumutu kuravarum cāṉṟōru mutalāyavar.
(u-ṟai) karaṭiyiṉ kūṭṭam citalai ceyta puṟṟiṉ kōṭṭiṉai yuṭaittu, atiluḷḷa puṟṟām paḻañ cōṟṟai yuṇṭum, mēlum ataṉai virumpic cūḻntāṟ pōla, nī emar kāvalaic citaittut talaiviyai nukarntum mīṭṭum ak kaḷa voḻukkattaiyē virumpip pakaliṉum iraviṉum cūḻavārāniṉṟāy eṉpatu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `āṟṟatu tīmai yaṟivuṟu kalakkamum' eṉpataṟku ippāṭṭu utāraṇa mākum eṉṟum, `poḻutu māṟuṅ kāppum'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `naṉmaiyun tīmaiyum piṟitiṉaik kūṟalum' eṉṟa pakutikku, `kaḻi peruṅ kātala rāyiṉuñ cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉap piṟarmēl vaittut talaivaṉai aṟivu koḷuttiṉamaiyiṉ vaḻuvāy amaintatu eṉṟum, `paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉavē, pukaḻoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkuva reṉak koḷḷa vaittaliṉ, naṉmaiyun tīmaiyum piṟitiṉmēl vaittuk kūṟiṟṟām eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
`kalvi taṟukaṇ'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kaḻiyāk kātala rāyiṉuñ cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉpatu pukaḻ paṟṟip piṟanta perumitam' eṉpar, pērā.
113. pālai
(talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		naṉṟal kālaiyum naṭpiṟ kōṭār
		ceṉṟu vaḻippaṭūun tiripil cūḻcciyil
		puṉṟalai maṭappiṭi akavunar perumakaṉ
		5amarvīcu vaṇmakiḻ aḵtaip pōṟṟik
	5.	kāppukkai niṟutta palvēṟ kōcar
		iḷaṅkaḷ kamaḻum neytalam ceruviṉ
		vaḷaṅkeḻu naṉṉā ṭaṉṉaveṉ tōḷmaṇan
		taḻuṅkal mūtūr alareṭut taraṟṟa
		nalkātu tuṟanta kātalar eṉṟum
	10.	kalporūu meliyāp 1pāṭiṉōṉ aṭiyaṉ
		2alku vaṉcuraip peyta valciyaṉ
		ikantaṉa vāyiṉum iṭam pārttup pakaivar
		ōmpiṉar uṟaiyum kūḻkeḻu kuṟumpil
		kuvaiyimil viṭaiya vēṟṟā oyyum
	15.	kaṉaiyiruñ curuṇaik kaṉikāḻ neṭuvēl
		viḻavayarnta taṉṉa koḻumpal tiṟṟi
		eḻāap pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
		neṟicel vampalark koṉṟa tevvar
		eṟipaṭai kaḻīiya cēyaric ciṉṉīr
	20.	aṟutuṟai yayirmaṇal paṭukaraip pōkic
		cēyar eṉṟalil ciṟumai yuṟṟaveṉ
		kaiyaṟu neñcat tevvam nīṅka
		aḻāam uṟaitalum uriyam parārai
		alaṅkal añciṉaik kuṭampai pulleṉap
	25.	pulampeyar maruṅkil puḷḷeḻun tāṅku
		meyyiva ṇoḻiyap pōkiavar
		ceyviṉai maruṅkil celīiyareṉ uyirē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. naṉṟu al kālaiyum naṭpil kōṭār - (naṭṭār ākkamiṉṟik) kēṭuṟṟa kālaiyum naṭput taṉmaiyiṟ ṟiriyārāy, ceṉṟu vaḻippaṭūum tiripu il cūḻcciyil - an naṭṭārpāṟ ceṉṟu avar kuṟippiṟ paṭum māṟupāṭillāta aṟivuṭaimaiyāl, akavunar perumakaṉ - kūttaraip purakkum talaivaṉāy, puṉtalai maṭappiṭi amar vīcu vaṇmakiḻ - (avarkaṭkup) pulliya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya piṭiyiṉai amariṉkaṇ aḷikkum vaṇmaiyālākiya makiḻvuṭaiya, aḵtai pōṟṟi  - aḵtai eṉpāṉaip pātukāttu, kāppuk kai niṟutta - avaṉaik kāval mikka iṭattē nilai niṟuttiya, palvēl kōcar - pala vēṟpaṭaiyiṉai yuṭaiya kōcareṉpavaratu;
6-7. iḷaṅ kaḷ kamaḻum neytalañ ceṟuviṉ - putiya kaḷ kamaḻum neytalañ ceṟu eṉum, vaḷam keḻu nal nāṭu aṉṉa - vaḷam poruntiya nalla nāṭṭai yotta;
7-9. eṉ tōḷ maṇantu  - eṉ tōḷiṉaik kūṭi, aḻuṅkal mūtūr alar eṭuttu araṟṟa - āravāramuṭaiya paḻaiya ūr alariṉai mikuttuk kūṟa, nalkātu tuṟanta kātalar - aruḷātu viṭṭēkiya nam kātalar;
9-11. eṉṟum kal porūu meliyāp pāṭuiṉ nōṉ aṭiyaṉ - eññāṉṟum kallaip porutu melivuṟāta ōcai iṉiya valiya aṭiyiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉum, alku vaṉ curai peyta valciyaṉ - mikka valiya mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyiṟ peyta uṇaviṉai yuṭaiyavaṉum;
12-17. ikantaṉavāyiṉum - taṉ nāṭṭellaiyaik kaṭantu cēymaikkaṇṇa vāyiṉum, iṭam yārttu - kavarum cevvi pārttu, pakaivar ōmpiṉar uṟaiyum kūḻkeḻu kuṟumpil - pakaivar āviṉaip pātukāttu uṟaiyum uṇavu mikka araṇkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, kuvai imil viṭaiya vēṟṟu ā oyyum - tiraṇṭa imilaiyuṭaiya viṭaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya pakaippulattu ākkaḷaik kavarntu celuttum, kaṉai irum curuṇai kaṉi kāḻ neṭuvēl - ceṟinta kariya pūṇaiyum ney kaṉinta taṇṭaiyumuṭaiya nīṇṭa vēliṉaiyum, viḻavu ayarntaṉṉa koḻum pal tiṟṟi - viḻāc ceytālotta koḻumai yākiya pala uṇavukaḷaiyumuṭaiya, eḻāap pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭṭu umpar - pakaivarkkup puṟakkiṭāta pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉatu nalla nāṭṭiṟku appāṟpaṭṭa;
18-22. neṟicel vampalark koṉṟa tevvar - vaḻiyiṟ pōkum putiyarkaḷaik koṉṟa āṟalaippōr, eṟi paṭai kaḻīiya cēy aric cil nīr - tāṅkaḷ eṟinta paṭaikkalattaik kaḻuviya civanta niṟamuṭaiya arittōṭum ciṉṉīraiyuṭaiya, aṟu tuṟai ayir maṇal paṭukarai pōki - makkaḷ iyakkam aṟṟa tuṟaiyākiya nuṇmaṇal poruntiya karaiyiṉait tāṇṭi, cēyar eṉṟaliṉ - cēymaikkaṇ uḷḷār eṉṟu palarum kūṟaliṉ, ciṟumaiyuṟṟa eṉ kaiyaṟu neñcattu evvam nīṅka - nōyuṟṟa eṉ ceyalaṟṟa neñcattut tuṉpam oḻiya;
23-27. paru arai alaṅkal am ciṉaik kuṭampai pul eṉa- parutta araiyiṟ kiḷaitta acaiyum aḻakiya kiḷaiyiluḷḷa taṉ kūṭupolivaṟṟoḻiya, pulam peyar maruṅkil puḷ eḻuntāṅku - tāṉ peyarntu pōka eṇṇiya pulattiṭattup paṟavai puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟāṟ pōla, mey ivaṇ oḻiya - eṉatu mey iṅkut taṉit toḻiya, eṉ uyir pōki-eṉ uyir pirintu puṟappaṭṭu, avar ceyviṉai maruṅkil celīiyar - avar viṉai ceyyumiṭattiṟ celvatāka, aḻāam uṟaitalum uriyam - ataṉāṉē nām aḻātēmāy uṟaitaṟku uriyam āvēm.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kōcar naṉṉāṭaṉṉa eṉ tōḷ maṇantu tuṟanta kātalar pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭṭumpar, paṭukarai pōkic cēya reṉṟaliṉ evvam nīṅka eṉ uyir pōki avar viṉaicey maruṅkiṟ celīiyar; aḻāam uṟaitalum uriyam.
aṭiyaṉum valciyaṉumākiya vēṟṟā oyyum pāṇaṉ eṉka.
(vi-rai) kūttar mutalāyiṉārkku yāṉai mutaliyavaṟṟai amarkkaḷattil aḷittalai, `māṟṟār uṟumuraṇ citaittaṉiṉ nōṉṟāḷ vāḻttik, kāṇku vanticiṟ kaḻaṟoṭi yaṇṇal........ maḻaiyiṉum perumpayam poḻiti yataṉāṟ, paciyuṭai yokkalai yorīiya, icaimēn tōṉṟaṉiṉ pācaṟai yāṉē'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. māvīcu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, piṭiyoṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉai vaḻaṅkum eṉka. neytalañ ceṟuvākiya naṉṉā ṭeṉka. iṉcāriyai al vaḻikkaṇ vantatu. pāṭu iṉ nōṉ aṭiyaṉ  ōcai iṉiya valiya aṭiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ; ceruppaṇintu paralil naṭattaliṉ ōcai iṉiya eṉṟār. paraṭṭiṉō ṉaṭi eṉṟa pāṭattiṟkup paraṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya valiya aṭi yeṉka. viṭaiya vēṟṟā - vēṟṟuppulattu viṭai
kaḷōṭu kūṭiya ākkaḷ. aṭiyaṉ valciyaṉ ākiya pāṇaṉ eṉavum, vēṟṟā oyyum pāṇaṉ eṉavum, vēlaiyum tiṟṟiyaiyum uṭaiya pāṇaṉ eṉavum, taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. `valimiku moympiṟ pāṇaṉ' eṉavum `vaṭāatu valvēṟ pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭu' eṉavum innūluṭ piṟāṇṭum varutaliṟ pāṇaṉeṉpāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkaṇ irunta oru kāṭṭut talaivaṉ āvaṉ eṉka. kātalar cēya reṉṟaliṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka. uyir uṭampai viṭṭup pōtaṟkup puḷ kuṭampaiyai viṭṭuccellutal uvamamātalai, `cēkkai maraṉoḻiyac cēṇīṅku puṭpōla, yākkai tamark koḻiya nīttu'2 eṉpataṉā laṟika. evvam nīṅka, aḻām uṟaitalum uriyam eṉṟatu, uyir ceṉṟa kālattu nilaiyaik karutiyatu.
114. mullai
(viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷun talaimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kēḷāy ella tōḻi vēlaṉ 
		veṟiyayar kaḷattuc ciṟupala tāaya
		viravuvī uṟaitta īrnaṟum puṟaviṉ
		uravukkatir maḻuṅkiya kalcēr ñāyiṟu
	5.	aravunuṅku matiyiṉ aiyeṉa maṟaiyum
		ciṟupuṉ mālaiyum uḷḷār avareṉa
		nampulan tuṟaiyum evvam nīṅka
		nūlaṟi valava, kaṭavumati uvakkāṇ
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum vāṉtōy puricai
	10.	yāmaṅ koḷpavar nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar
		vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum
		aruṅkaṭik kāppiṉ aruñvaru mūtūrt
		tirunakar aṭaṅkiya mācil kaṟpiṉ
		arimatar maḻaikkaṇ amaipurai paṇaittōḷ
	15.	aṇaṅkucāl arivaiyaik kāṇkuvam
		polampaṭaik kalimāp pūṇṭa tērē.
-..........................
(co-ḷ) 8. nūl aṟi valava - parinūl valla pākaṉē!,
1-7. ella tōḻi kēḷāy - ellā ēṭi tōḻiyē yāṉ kūṟuvataṉaik kēṭpāyāka; vēlaṉ veṟi ayar kaḷattu tāya - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum kaḷattil paranta, ciṟu pala viravu vī - ciṟiya palavāya kalanta pūkkaḷpōla, uṟaitta īr naṟum puṟaviṉ - pūkkaḷ utirnta kuḷirnta naṟiya mullai nilattē, uravu katir maḻuṅkiya kalcēr ñāyiṟu - valiya katiriṉ vemmai kuṟainta mēṟku malaiyaic cērnta ñāyiṟu, aravu nuṅku matiyiṉ aiyeṉa maṟaiyum - pāmpu viḻuṅkum tiṅkaḷeṉa mella maṟaikiṉṟa, ciṟu pul mālaiyum uḷḷār avar  eṉa - ciṟiya pulliya mālaikkālattum nam talaivar nammai niṉaintilarē eṉṟivvāṟu nam talaivi taṉ tōḻiyai nōkkikkūṟi, nam pulantu uṟaiyum evvam nīṅka - nammai veṟuttu uṟaiyum tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu;
8-15. neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum vāṉtōy puricai - neṭiya koṭi acaiyum vāṉait tōyum matiliṉkaṇ, yāmam koḷpavar nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar - irāppoḻutaik kāttiruppōr ēṟṟiya neruṅkiya viḷakkukkaḷ, vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum - vāṉiṭattēyuḷḷa mīṉpōla viḷakkamuṟṟuk kāṇappeṟum, arukaṭi kāppiṉ - aṇukutaṟkuriya mikka kāvaliṉaiyuṭaiya, añcu varum mūtūr - pakaivarkku accattiṉait tarum mutiya ūriluḷḷa, tirunakar aṭaṅkiya - celvamuḷḷa maṉaiyil tayaṅkiya, mācu il kaṟpiṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kaṟpiṉaiyum, ari matar maḻaikkaṇ - cevvari paṭarnta matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷaiyum, amai purai paṇaittōḷ - mūṅkilai otta parutta tōḷiṉaiyum uṭaiya, aṇaṅkucāl arivaiyaik kāṇkuvam - teyvampōṉṟa ciṟanta nam talaiviyaik kāṇpōm;
16. polampaṭaik kali mā pūṇṭa tēr - poṉṉālāya cēṇattiṉai yaṇinta cerukkiṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa tēriṉai;
8. uvakkāṇ kaṭavumati - uṅkē celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) valava! nam talaivi tōḻiyai nōkki, `ciṟupuṉ mālaiyum uḷḷār avar' eṉak kūṟi, pulantu uṟaiyum evvam nīṅka, mūtūril tirunakar aṭaṅkiya arivaiyaik kāṇkuvam! uvakkāṇ tēr kaṭavumati.
puricaiyil nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum mūtūr eṉka.
(vi-rai) ella - ellā; keḻutakaip potuccol: kal - atta malai. katir vemmai maḻuṅkiṉamaiyāl matipōl eṉṟār. ciṟitu ciṟitāka maṟaital kuṟittu aravu nuṅku mati eṉṟār. aiyeṉa - viyakkum paricu eṉṟumām. puṉ mālai - varuttattaic ceyyum mālaiyumām. nam pulantu eṉpatu vikāramāyiṟṟu. nūl - kutiraiyuḷa maṟiyum nūl.  uvakkāṇ - uṅkē; mūtūrkkaṇ eṉṟapaṭi. kaṭikkāppu : oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi. aṭaṅkiya kaṟpiṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, āṟiya kaṟpiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟuraittalumām. aṇaṅku cāl - aruntati pōlum kaṟpuṭaiya eṉṟalumām.
115. pālai
(piriviṭai vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		aḻiyā viḻaviṉ añcuvaru mūtūrp
		paḻiyilar āyiṉum palarpuṟaṅ kūṟum
		ampal oḻukkamum ākiya veñcoṟ
		cēriyam  peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka
	5.	nuṇpūṇ erumai kuṭanāṭ ṭaṉṉaeṉ
		āynalam tolaiyiṉum tolaika eṉṟum
		nōyila rākanam kātalar vāyvāḷ
		evvi vīḻnta ceruviṟ pāṇar
		kaitoḻu marapiṉmuṉ parittiṭūup paḻicciya
	10.	vaḷḷuyir vaṇarmarup paṉṉa oḷḷiṇarc
		cuṭarppūṅ koṉṟai ūḻuṟu viḷaineṟṟu
		aṟaimicait tāam attam nīḷiṭaip
		piṟaimaruḷ vāṉkōṭ ṭaṇṇal yāṉaic
		ciṉamiku muṉpiṉ 1vāmāṉ añci
	15.	iṉaṅkoṇ ṭoḷikkum añcuvaru kavalai
		naṉṉar āykaviṉ tolaiyac cēynāṭṭu
		nannīt tuṟaiyum poruṭpiṇik
		kūṭā maiyiṉ nīṭi yōrē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) (tōḻi!)
1-4. aḻiyā viḻaviṉ añcuvarum mūtūr - eṉṟum nīṅkāta viḻāviṉaiyuṭaiya pakaivarkku accam tarum im mutiya ūriṉ kaṇṇē, palar - palaraiyum, paḻiyilarāyiṉum - avar paḻi yoṉṟum ilarāyiṉum, puṟaṅkūṟum ampal oḻukkamum - puṟañcoṟ kūṟutalākiya ampal oḻukkattiṉaiyum, ākiya vem col - vevviya collaiyum  uṭaiyārākiya, cēri peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka - cērip peṇṭir nammai ikaḻiṉum ikaḻuka;
5-6. nuṇpūṇ erumai kuṭa nāṭu aṉṉa - nuṇṇiya toḻiṟ pāṭamainta pūṇiṉaiyuṭaiya erumai yeṉpāṉatu kuṭa nāṭṭiṉaiyotta, eṉ āy nalam tolaiyiṉum tolaika - eṉatu aḻakiya nalam tolaiyiṉum tolaivatāka;
7-12. vāy vāḷ evvi vīḻnta ceruvil - tappāta vāṭpaṭai yiṉaiyuṭaiya evvi eṉpāṉ vīḻnta pōrkkaḷattē, pāṇar pāṇarkaḷ, kaitoḻu marapiṉ - kaiyāṟ ṟoḻum muṟaimaiyoṭu, muṉ paḻicciya - muṉpellām parāviya (yāḻiṉ), vaḷ uyir vaṇar maruppu - vaḷam poruntiya oliyaiyuṭaiya vaḷainta kōṭṭiṉai, parittu iṭūu aṉṉa - oṭittup pōkaṭṭāl otta, oḷ iṇar cuṭar pū koṉṟai - oḷi poruntiya kottukkaḷaiyuṭaiya cuṭarum pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya koṉṟaiyiṉatu, ūḻ uṟu viḷai neṟṟu - muṟaiyāka muṟṟi viḷainta neṟṟukkaḷ, aṟaimicait tāam attam nīḷ iṭai - pāṟai mītu parantu kiṭakkum curattiṉ nīṇṭa neṟiyiluḷḷa,
13-15. piṟai maruḷ vāṉ kōṭṭu aṇṇal yāṉai - piṟaiyiṉai yotta veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya perumai taṅkiya yāṉaikaḷaiyum, ciṉam miku muṉpiṉ vām māṉ añci - ciṉam mikka valimaiyaiyum tāvum kutiraikaḷaiyumuṭaiya añci eṉpāṉ, iṉam koṇṭu oḷikkum añcu varu kavalai - pakaivaratu āṉiṉaṅkaḷaik kaippaṟṟi maṟaittiṭum accantōṉṟum kavartta neṟikaḷilē;
16-18. naṉṉar āykaviṉ tolaiya - naṉṟākiya namatu aḻaku tolaiya, nam nīttuc cēy nāṭṭu uṟaiyum poruṭpiṇi - nammaip pirintu neṭun tūrattiluḷḷa nāṭṭil vatitaṟkētuvāya poruḷīṭṭum ceyal, kūṭāmaiyiṉ nīṭiyōr - uriya kālattiṉuḷ muṟṟāmaiyiṉ kālam tāḻntiruppōr ākiya;
6-7. nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyilar āka - nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyiṉṟi iṉitiruppārāka.
(muṭipu) mūtūril veñcoṟ peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka; eṉ āy nalam tolaiyiṉum tolaika; evvi vīḻnta ceruvil pāṇar muṟittu iṭūup paḻicciya vaṇar maruppu aṉṉa koṉṟai neṟṟu tāam attam nīḷiṭai, añci iṉam koṇṭoḷikkum kavalaiyil, kaviṉ tolaiya nīttuc cēynāṭṭuṟaiyum poruṭpiṇi kūṭāmaiyiṉ nīṭiyōr ākiya nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyilarāka.
(vi-rai) palar - palarum; `palar pukaḻ ñāyiṟu' eṉpuḻip pōla muṟṟummai tokkatu. cēri am peṇṭir: am, cāriyai. pāṇar yāḻilē teyvam uṟaital paṟṟiyum, tamakku uṇavaḷikkum utaviyuṭaimai paṟṟiyum yāḻiṉait toḻutu paravuvār eṉavum, evvi iṟantapiṉ iṉit tam pāṭṭiṉaik kēṭṭup paricil aḷippār ilareṉṉum varutta mikutiyāl yāḻiṉai muṟittup pōkaṭṭār eṉavum koḷka. maruppu aṉṉavāka neṟṟu aṟaimicait tāam eṉka. itaṉāl pāṇar muṟittup pōkaṭṭa maruppum palaveṉpatu peṟṟām. vayamāṉ añci eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, ciṅkam pōlum valiyuṭaiya añci eṉṟuraikka. yāṉai ciṉa miku muṉpiṉ vayamāṉ añci iṉaṅkoṇ ṭoḷikkum eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, yāṉaiyāṉatu ciṅkattaik kaṇṭañci taṉ iṉattaik koṇṭoḷikkum kavalai eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `maṅkala moḻiyum vaiiya moḻiyum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `nōyila rākanaṅ kātalar' eṉak kūṟuvatu, nammai aṟaṉaṉṟit tuṟattaliṉ tīṅku varumeṉṟu añci vāḻttiyatu; namporuṭṭut tīṅku varumeṉa niṉaittaliṉ vaḻuvāy amaintatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
116. marutam
(tōḻi talaimakaṉai vāyil maṟuttatu.)
		eriyakain taṉṉa tāmarai yiṭaiyiṭai
		arintukāl kuvitta cennel viṉaiñar
		kaṭkoṇṭu maṟukuñ cākā ṭaḷaṟṟuṟiṉ
		āykarum paṭukkum pāypuṉal ūra
	5.	periya nāṇiṉai maṉṟa poriyeṉap
		puṉkaviḻ akaṉṟuṟaip poliya oṇṇutal
		naṟumalark kāṇvaruṅ kuṟumpal kūntal
		māḻai nōkkiṟ kāḻiyal vaṉamulai
		eḵkuṭai eḻilnalat toruttiyoṭu *1nerunai
	10.	vaikupuṉal ayarntaṉai yeṉpa atuvē
		poypuṟam 2potintiyām karappavum kaiyikantu
		alarā kiṉṟāl tāṉē malartār
		maiyaṇi yāṉai maṟappōrc ceḻiyaṉ
		poyyā viḻaviṉ kūṭaṟ paṟantalai
	15.	uṭaṉiyain teḻunta iruperu vēntar
		kaṭalmaruḷ perumpaṭai kalaṅkat tākki
		iraṅkicai muracam oḻiyap parantavar.
		ōṭupuṟam kaṇṭa ñāṉṟai
		āṭukoḷ viyaṉkaḷat tārppiṉum peritē.
-paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. eri akainta aṉṉa tāmarai iṭaiyiṭai - neruppuk kappuviṭ ṭerivatu pōṉṟa tāmaraip pūkkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē, cennel kāl arintu kuvitta viṉaiñar - cenneṟṟāḷai arintu ariariyākap pōkaṭṭa nellarivōr, kaṭ koṇṭu maṟukum cākāṭu - taṅkaṭkuk kaḷḷaik koṇṭu palakālum tiriyum vaṇṭi, aḷaṟṟu uṟiṉ - cēṟṟiṟ patintiṭiṉ, āykarumpu aṭukkum pāypuṉal ūra - ataṉaip pōkkac ciṟanta karumpukaḷai aṭukki iṭaimaṭukkum pāyum puṉal vaḷa mikka ūraṉē, periya nāṇilai maṉṟa - uṟutiyāka nī peritum nāṇillātavaṉāvāy;
5-10. pori eṉap puṉku aviḻ akal tuṟai poliya -poripōlap puṉkampū malarum akaṉṟa nīrttuṟai polivuṟa, oḷnutal - oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyum, naṟumalar kāṇvarum kuṟum pal kūntal - naṟiya malarkaḷ vēynta kāṇṭaṟkiṉiya kuṟiya palavāya kūntaliṉaiyum, māḻai nōkkiṉ - māvaṭup pōṉṟa kaṇṇiṉaiyum, kāḻ iyal vaṉa mulai - muttuvaṭam acaiyum aḻakiya mulaiyiṉaiyum, eḵku uṭai eḻil nalattu oruttiyoṭu - nuṇṇiya aḻakiṉ nalattiṉaiyumuṭaiya oru parattaiyōṭu, nerunai vaikupuṉal ayarntaṉai eṉpa - nēṟṟu iṭaiyaṟātu oḻukum puṉalil viḷaiyāṭ ṭayarntaṉai eṉap palaruṅ kūṟuvar;
10-11. atuvē - atutāṉ, poypuṟam potintu yām karappavum - ataṉaip poy yeṉṟu puṟattē mūṭi yām maṟaikkavum, kaiyi kantu - emceyaliṉaik kaṭantu;
12-19. malar tār - malarnta pūmālaiyiṉaiyum, mai aṇi yāṉai - talaiyil mai aṇinta yāṉaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, maṟappōr - maṟam poruntiya pōril valla, ceḻiyaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉatu, poyyā viḻaviṉ kūṭal paṟantalai - eṉṟum nīṅkāta viḻaviṉaiyuṭaiya kūṭaṟ pōrkkaḷattil, uṭaṉ iyaintu eḻunta iru peru vēntar - tammuḷ oruṅku iyaintu eḻunta cōḻa cēra aracarkaḷatu, kaṭalmaruḷ perum paṭai - kaṭal pōṉṟa peruntāṉaikaḷai, kalaṅkat tākki - kalaṅkum paricu tākki, iraṅku icai muracam oḻiyap parantu avar ōṭupuṟam kaṇṭa ñāṉṟai - olikkum oliyiṉaiyuṭaiya muracu oḻintu kiṭakka avar parantu ōṭum puṟakkoṭaiyaik kaṇṭa nāḷil, āṭu koḷviyaṉ kaḷattu ārppiṉum peritu - veṟṟi koṇṭa periya kaḷattiṉkaṇ eḻunta āravārattiṉum peritāka;
12. alar ākiṉṟu - alarākiṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) pāy puṉalūra! periya nāṇilai maṉṟa; eḻil nalat toruttiyoṭu nerunai vaikupuṉal ayarntaṉai eṉpa; atu yām karappavum kaiyikantu, ceḻiyaṉ kūṭaṟ paṟantalai, iruperu vēntar perumpaṭai kalaṅkat tākki avar puṟaṅkaṇṭa ñāṉṟai, kaḷattu eḻunta ārppiṉum peritu alar ākiṉṟu.
(vi-rai) āy karumpu - melliya karumpumām. karumpu aṭukkum eṉṟatu maruta nilattiṉ vaḷamikuti kūṟiyapaṭi. māḻai - iḷamai eṉṟum, aḻa keṉṟumām. eḻil nalam - eḻucciyum nalamumām. yāṉaikku neṟṟiyil añcaṉam aṇital marapātaliṉ, maiyaṇi yāṉaiyeṉṟār.
(u-ṟai) nellariyum viṉaiñar tam toḻilai viṭuttu iḻintakaḷ vaṇṭiyiṉ āḻcciyaip pōkkaṟkuc ciṟanta karumpiṉaic citaippatu pōla, nī illaṟañ ceytalāya niṉ oḻukkattiṉaik kaiviṭṭu, iḻinta parattaiyiṉ iṉpattai nukartaṟkuc ciṟanta talaiviyai varuttukiṉṟāy eṉpatu.
(mē - ḷ) `kiḻavōṉ viḷaiyāṭ ṭāṅku maṟṟē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `eḵkuṭai yeḻiṉalat toruttiyoṭu.... alarā kiṉṟāṟ ṟāṉē' eṉpatu, talaivi piṟar alar kūṟiyavaḻik kāmañ ciṟantu pulantavāṟu eṉṟum, `vāyiṟ kiḷavi veḷippaṭak kiḷattal' 1  eṉṉum cūttirattu, `eriyakain taṉṉa tāmarai yiṭai yiṭai....... yāppiṉum peritē' eṉpataṉuḷ, `nāṇilai maṉṟa' eṉat tōḻi kūṟi alarā kiṉṟāl eṉa veḷippaṭak kiḷattaliṉ vaḻuvāy amaintatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
117. pālai
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		mauvaloṭu malarnta mākkural nocciyum
		avvari alkul āyamum uḷḷāḷ
		ētilaṉ poymmoḻi nampi ērvi(ṉai)
		vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar pulampap pōki
	5.	veruvaru kavalai āṅkaṇ aruḷvarak
		karuṅkāl ōmai yēṟi veṇṭalaip
		paruntupeṭai payirum pāḻnāṭ ṭāṅkaṇ
		polantoṭi teḷirppa vīcic cēvaṭic
		cilampunaka iyalic ceṉṟaeṉ makaṭkē.
	10.	cāntuḷar vaṇarkural vāri vakaivakuttu
		yāṉpōtu tuṇaippat takaram maṇṇāḷ
		taṉṉō raṉṉa takaiveṅ kātalaṉ
		veṟikamaḻ paṉmalar puṉaiyap piṉṉuviṭac
		ciṟupuṟam putaiya neṟiputāḻn taṉakol
	15.	neṭuṅkāl māat tūḻuṟu veṇpaḻam
		koṭuntāḷ yāmai pārppoṭu kavarum
		poykai cūḻnta poyyā 2 yāṇar
		vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu
		tīnīrk kāṉyāṟṟu aviraṟal pōṉṟē.
- ..............................
(co-ḷ) 1-2. mauvaloṭu malarnta mākkural nocciyum- mullaip pūvōṭu malarnta kariya kottiṉaiyuṭaiya nocciyaiyum, avvari alkul āyamum uḷḷāḷ - aḻakiya vari poruntiya alkuliṉaiyuṭaiya āyattiṉaiyum niṉaiyāḷāyum;
10-11. cāntu uḷar vaṇar kural vāri - mayirc cāntu pūciya vaḷainta kottākiya kūntalai vāri, vakai vakuttu - vakaippaṭuttu, yāṉ pōtu tuṇaippa - yāṉ malarkaḷai iṇaikkappuka, takaramum maṇṇāḷ - ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭut takaramum pūcikkoḷḷāḷāyum;
