3-4. ētilaṉ poy moḻi nampi - piṟaṉoruvaṉ poym moḻiyiṉai virumpi, ēr viṉai vaḷamkeḻu tiru nakar pulampap pōki - aḻakiya toḻiṉalam vāynta vaḷam poruntiya celva mikka māḷikai taṉit toḻiyap puṟappaṭṭu;
5-9. veru varu kavalai āṅkaṇ - añcattakka kavartta neṟikaḷāka avviṭaṅkaḷil, veṇtalaip paruntu - veḷḷiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya paruntu, karuṅkāl ōmai ēṟi - kariya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ōmai marattiṉ mītēṟi, aruḷ vara - irakkam uṇṭāka, peṭai payirum pāḻ nāṭṭu āṅkaṇ - taṉatu peṭaiyiṉai aḻaikkum pāḻāṉa nāṭṭiṭattē, polam toṭi teḷirppa vīci - taṉatu poṟṟoṭi yolikkak kaiyiṉai vīciyum, cēvaṭic cilampu naka iyali - civanta aṭiyiṟ cilampukaḷ viḷaṅka ulāviyum, ceṉṟa eṉ makaṭku - pōy viṭṭa eṉ makaḷukku;
12-13. taṉ ōraṉṉa takai vem kātalaṉ - taṉṉaiyē otta takaimaiyaiyum viruppattaiyumuṭaiya kātalaṉākiya talaivaṉ, veṟi kamaḻ paṉmalar puṉaiyap piṉṉuviṭa - maṇam kamaḻum palavakai malarkaḷai vait taṇiceyaṟkup piṉṉutalaic ceyya (avaḷ kūntal),
15-19. neṭuṅ kāl māattu ūḻ uṟu veṇ paḻam - neṭiya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya māmarattiṉatu muṟaiyāka muṟṟiya veḷḷiya paḻattiṉai, koṭu tāḷ yāmai pārppoṭu kavarum - vaḷainta kāliṉaiyuṭaiya yāmai taṉ pārppōṭu kavarntuṇṇum, poykai cūḻnta poyyā yāṇar- poykaikaḷ cūḻntuḷḷa eṉṟum aṟāta putu varuvāykaḷaiyuṭaiya, vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu - vāṇaṉatu ciṟukuṭi eṉṉum ūrkku vaṭakkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa, tīm nīrk kāṉyāṟṟu avir aṟal pōṉṟu - iṉiya nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ viḷaṅkum aṟal pōṉṟu;
14. ciṟupuṟam putaiya neṟipu tāḻntaṉa kol - piṭar maṟaiya neṟintu toṅkukiṉṟaṉavō?
(muṭipu) nocciyiṉaiyum āyattiṉaiyum niṉaiyāḷā yum, takaram maṇṇāḷāyum, ētilaṉ poymmoḻi nampi, nakar pulampappōki, pāḻ nāṭṭāṅkaṇ, vīciyum iyaliyuñ ceṉṟa eṉ makaṭku, kātalaṉ piṉṉuviṭa, avaḷ kūntal vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu kāṉyāṟṟu aṟal pōṉṟu ciṟupuṟam putaiya neṟipu tāḻntaṉakol?
(vi-rai) aruḷ vara peṭaikku aruḷ; kaṇṭārkku aruḷ vara eṉalumām. kaiyai vīci eṉka. taṉṉō raṉṉa - aṉpu, oḻukkam mutaliyavaṟṟāl taṉṉai yotta.
118. kuṟiñci
(ceṟippaṟivuṟīi iravum pakalum vāra leṉṟu varaivu kaṭāayatu.)
		kaṟaṅkuveḷ 1aruvi piṟaṅkumalaik kavāaṉ
		tēṅkamaḻ iṇara vēṅkai cūṭit
		toṇṭakap paṟaiccīr peṇṭiroṭu viraii
		maṟukiṟ ṟūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu
	5.	2iyaṉmuru koppiṉai vayanāy piṟpaṭap
		pakalvariṟ kavvai añcutum ikalkoḷa
		irumpiṭi kaṉṟoṭu viraiiya kayavāyp
		peruṅkai 3 yāṉaik kōḷpiḻait tirīiya
		aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ
	10.	taṉiyai varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum
		eṉṉā kuvaḷkol tāṉē paṉṉāḷ
		puṇarkuṟi ceyta pularkural ēṉal
		kiḷitaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ
		aḷiyaḷ tāṉiṉ aḷiyala tilaḷē.
- kapilar
(co-ḷ) 1-6. kaṟaṅku veḷ aruvi piṟaṅku malaik kavāaṉ - olikkum veḷḷiya aruvikaḷ viḷaṅkum malaiyiṉ cāraṟ kaṇṇē, tēm kamaḻ iṇara vēṅkai cūṭi - tēṉ kamaḻum kottuk kaḷaiyuṭaiya vēṅkaip pūkkaḷaic cūṭi, toṇṭakap paṟaic cīr - toṇṭakam eṉṉum paṟaiyiṉ tāḷattiṟkicaiya, peṇṭiroṭu viraii - āṭavar peṇṭiroṭu kalantu, maṟukiltūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu - terukkaḷil āṭum ematu ciṟukuṭip pākkattiṉkaṇṇē, iyal muruku oppiṉai - iyaṅkum murukaṉai oppāyāki, vayanāy piṟpaṭap pakalvariṉ- valiya nāy piṉṉē varap pakaṟkaṇ nī variṉ, kavvai añcutum - ūrār kūṟum alarkku añcukiṟōm;
6-10. irumpiṭi kaṉṟōṭu viraiiya - kariya piṭiyōṭum kaṉṟōṭuṅ kūṭiya, kayavāy peruṅkai yāṉaik kōḷ piḻaittu- akaṉṟa vāyiṉaiyum nīṇṭa kaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya yāṉaiyaik koḷḷutal piḻaittu, ikalkoḷa irīiya - ataṉāl avaṟṟaip pakaimai porunta maṟaintirukkumpaṭi ceyta, aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ - kollum puli tiriyum ariya iruḷ cūḻnta naḷḷiravil, taṉiyai varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum - nī taṉiyaiyāki varutalai ataṉaik kāṭṭiṉum añcukiṉṟōm;
11-14. paṉṉāḷ puṇarkuṟi ceyta pularkural ēṉal - pala nāḷum puṇarum pakaṟkuṟiyākak koṇṭa muṟṟiya katiraiyuṭaiya tiṉaip puṉattilē, kiḷi kaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ - kiḷiyai ōṭṭutaṟkup pāṭum pāṭalum nīṅkiṉaḷ, eṉ ākuvaḷ kol - iṉi eṉ ākuvaḷō, aḷiyaḷ niṉ aḷi alatu ilaḷē - iraṅkat takkāḷāya eṉ tōḻi niṉ aruḷai yaṉṟip piṟitoru paṟṟuk kōṭum ilaḷ.
(muṭipu) talaiva, ciṟukuṭip pākkattu iyal muruku oppiṉai pakalvariṉ kavvai añcutum; aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ taṉiyai varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum, ivaḷ, ēṉal kiḷikaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ; eṉ ākuvaḷ kol? aḷiyaḷ niṉ aḷiyalatu ilaḷ.
(vi-rai) aruvip piṟaṅkumalai eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ aruviyaiyuṭaiya viḷaṅkum malai yeṉka. ikal muruku eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, pakaivaroṭu māṟupāṭu koṇṭa murukaṉ eṉka. mikalkoḷa eṉap pirittu, tarukku amaiya eṉṟuraittalumām. irīiya - ōṭumpaṭi ceyta eṉṟumām. yāṉai kōṭ piḻaittu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, yāṉaiyāṉatu koḷḷutal piḻaittu irīiya eṉka. vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ eṉpatu pāṭamākak koḷḷiṉ, vaḻaṅkum neṟiyiṉ kaṇ iruḷaiyuṭaiya naṭunāḷ eṉka.
pakalvariṟ kavvai yañcutum eṉṟataṉālē pakaṟkuṟi vilakkiyum, āriruḷ naṭunāḷ varutal añcutum eṉṟataṉāl iravukkuṟi vilakkiyum, ēṉalkiḷikaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ eṉṟataṉāl ceṟippu aṟivuṟuttiyum, niṉ aḷiyala tilaḷ eṉṟataṉāl varaintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟāḷām.
(mē-ḷ) `poḻutu māṟum'1 eṉṟa cūttirattup `pakal vāral' eṉṟataṟkup `pakalvariṟ kavvai yañcutum' eṉpataṉai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
119. pālai
(celavuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ coṟṟatu. tōḻi talai makaṭkuc coṟṟatūu mām.)
		nutalun tōḷun titalai yalkulum
		vaṇṇamum vaṉappum variyum vāṭa
		varuntuvaḷ ivaḷeṉat tiruntupu nōkki
		varaivunaṉ ṟeṉṉā takaliṉum avarvaṟitu
	5.	āṟucel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭai
		ēṟupeṟu pāmpiṉ paintuṇi kaṭuppa
		neṟiyayal tiraṅkum attam veṟikoḷa
		umaṇcāt tiṟanta oḻikal aṭuppiṉ
		nōṉcilai maḻavar ūṉpuḻuk kayarum
	10.	curaṉvaḻak kaṟṟa teṉṉā turañciṟantu
		neytal uruviṉ aitilaṅ kakalilait
		toṭaiyamai pīlip polinta kaṭikai
		maṭaiyamai tiṇcurai mākkāḻ vēloṭu
		taṇiyamar aḻuvam tammoṭu tuṇaippat
	15.	tuṇikuvar kollō tāmē 1tuṇikoḷa
		maṟappuli uḻanta vacipaṭu ceṉṉi
		uṟunōy varuttamo ṭuṇīiya maṇṭip
		paṭimuḻam ūṉṟiya neṭunal yāṉai
		kaitōyt tuyirkkum vaṟuñcuṉai
	20.	maitōy cimaiya malaimuta lāṟē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) (tōḻi!)
1-4. avar - nam talaivar, varaivu naṉṟu eṉṉātu akaliṉum - varaintu kōṭal takkatu eṉṉātu pirintiṭiṉum, nutalum tōḷum titalai alkulum vaṇṇamum vaṉappum variyum vāṭa - neṟṟiyum tōḷum tēmalaiyuṭaiya alkulum niṟamum aḻakum variyum vāṭa, varuntuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉat tiruntupu nōkki - ivaḷ varuntuvāḷ eṉa naṉku ārāyntuṇarntu;
15-20. maṟappuli uḻanta tuṇikoḷa vacipaṭu ceṉṉi - maṟattaiyuṭaiya puliyuṭaṉ porutu uḻantamaiyāl tuṇṭamākap piḷavupaṭṭa ceṉṉiyiṉkaṇ, uṟu nōy varuttamoṭu - uṟṟa nōyālāya varuttattuṭaṉ, uṇīiya maṇṭi - nīr uṇṭaṟku viraintu ceṉṟu, paṭi muḻam ūṉṟiya neṭu nal yāṉai - maṇṇil muḻaṅkālai maṭittūṉṟiya neṭiya nalla yāṉaiyāṉatu, kai tōyttu uyirkkum vaṟuñ cuṉai - taṉatu kaiyāl tōyttum nīriṉmaiyāl perumūc ceṟiyum vaṟiya cuṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, mai tōy cimaiya malai mutal āṟu - mēkam paṭiyum ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya malaiyiṭattuc cellum neṟiyāya;
4-7. āṟu cel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭai - neṟiyiṟ cellum makkaḷ aṟuttup pōkaṭṭa piraṇṭaik koṭi, ēṟu peṟu pāmpiṉ pain tuṇi kaṭuppa - iṭiyāṟ ṟākkutal peṟṟa pāmpiṉatu paciya tuṇṭupōla, neṟi ayal vaṟitu tiraṅkum attam - vaḻiyiṉ pakkattē payaṉiṉṟi vataṅkik kiṭakkum kāṭṭil;
7-10. umaṇ cāttu iṟanta oḻi kal aṭuppil - uppu vaṇikar kūṭṭam viṭṭoḻinta kal aṭuppil, nōṉ cilai maḻavar - valiya villaiyuṭaiya maḻavar, veṟi koḷa ūṉ puḻukku ayarum curaṉ - nāṟṟamuṇṭāka ūṉaip puḻukki yuṇṇum iṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya curamāṉatu, vaḻakku aṟṟatu eṉṉātu - peṇṭiroṭu iyaṅkutaṟku uriyatallatu eṉṟu niṉaiyātu;
10-15. uram ciṟantu - ūkkam mikku, neytal uruviṉ aitu ilaṅku akal ilai - neytaṟpūp pōlum uruviṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakitāka viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa ilaiyiṉaiyum, toṭai amai pīlip polinta kaṭikai - toṭuttal amainta mayiṟṟōkaiyāl viḷakkamuṟṟa kāmpiṉaiyum, maṭai amai tiṇ curai - mūṭṭuvāy amainta tiṇṇiya curaiyiṉaiyum, mā kāḻ vēloṭu - kariya taṇṭiṉaiyu muṭaiya vēlē tuṇaiyāka, taṇi amar aḻuvam - pakaiyait taṇivikkac cellum pōrkkaḷattiṟku, tammoṭu tuṇaippa - nāmum tammoṭu tuṇaiyākic cella, tuṇikuvar kollō - tuṇivarō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talaivar varaivu naṉṟeṉṉātu akaliṉum, varuntuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉat tiruntupu nōkki malaimutal āṟu āya curaṉ vaḻakkaṟṟa teṉṉātu, urañ ciṟantu amar aḻuvam tammoṭu tuṇaiyākak koṇṭu cellat tuṇikuvar kollō?
piraṇṭai tiraṅkum attattil aṭuppil maḻavar ūṉpuḻuk kayarum curaṉ eṉka.
(vi-rai) vaṟitu tiraṅkum eṉavum, veṟikoḷa ūṉ puḻukkayarum eṉavum kūṭṭuka. ēṟu - iṭi yēṟu eṉavum, eṟital eṉavum iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalākak koḷka. cāttu - vaṇikar tiraḷ. tuṉikoḷa eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku veṟuppuṇṭāka eṉṟuraikka.
(u-ṟai) `āṟu cellum mākkaḷāl aṟuttup pōkaṭap paṭṭup piraṇṭai vaṟitē tiraṅkuvatupōlat talaivarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭu em aḻaku payaṉpaṭaliṉṟiyē kaḻiyā niṉṟatu' `cāttarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭa kallaṭuppu maḻavarkku ūṉpuḻuk kayarntu makiḻtaṟkuk karuviyāṉāṟ pōlat talaivarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭa yām ampaṟ peṇṭirkku alartūṟṟi makiḻaṟku ilakkāyiṉōm.'
`puliyoṭuḻantu ceṉṉiyiṭattu vaṭuppaṭṭu varuntiya yāṉai nīruṇṇac cuṉaiyiṭattu muḻa mūṉṟik kaiyait tōyttum nīr peṟā tuyirttāṟpōl, aṉṉaiyiṉ koṭuñ collālum ampaṟ peṇṭiriṉ alarālum neñcu puṇṇuṟṟa yām, annōy tīra varaiyumāṟu talaimakaṉ kaḻalkaḷaip paṇintu vēṇṭiyum aruḷ peṟāmal uyirkkiṉṟōm' eṉṟāḷām. (irācakōpālai yaṅkār.)
120. neytal
(tōḻi pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talaimakaḷai iṭattuyttu vantu talaimakaṉai 
etirppaṭṭu niṉṟu colliyatu.)
		neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōlac
		cevvāy vāṉan tīṇṭimīṉ aruntum
		paiṅkāṟ 1kokkiṉa niraipaṟai yukappa
		ellai paippayak kaḻippik kuṭavayiṉ
	5.	kalcērn taṉṟē palkatir ñāyiṟu
		matareḻil maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻa ivaḷē
		perunāṇ aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal
		māṇalam citaiya ēṅki yāṉātu
		aḻaltoṭaṅ kiṉaḷē peruma ataṉāl
	10.	kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṭṭāḷ attiri
		neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelin tacaii
		valvil iḷaiyaro ṭellic cellātu
		cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuva tuṇṭō
		peṇṇai ōṅkiya veṇmaṇaṟ paṭappai
	15.	aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ
		ciṟukural neytalem peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē.
- nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōla - murukak kaṭavuḷ mārpiṉiṭattu muttārampōla, cevvāy vāṉam tīṇṭi - cevvāṉattiṭam porunti, mīṉ aruntum paiṅkāl kokku iṉam niraipaṟai ukappa - mīṉai aruntum paciya kālaiyuṭaiya kokkiṉatu iṉam varicaiyākap paṟattal uyarntiṭa, ellai paip payak kaḻippi - pakaṟpoḻutai mella mellap pōkki, pal katir ñāyiṟu kuṭavayiṉ kal cērntaṉṟu - pala katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya ñāyiṟu mēṟṟicaiyil maṟaiyum malaiyai aṭaintatu;
6-9. ivaḷē perunāṇ aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal - mikka nāṇaik koṇṭa ciṟiya melliya cāyaliṉaiyuṭaiya ivaḷ, māṇ nalam citaiya- māṇpuṟṟa aḻaku keṭa, ēṅki - ēkkamuṟṟu, matar eḻil maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻa - matartta aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya kuḷirnta kaṇ kalaṅkiṭa, āṉātu aḻal toṭaṅkiṉaḷē - amaiyātu aḻutalait toṭaṅkiyuḷḷāḷ;
9-12. peruma - talaiva!, ataṉāl - ataṉālum, kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṇ tāḷ attiri - uppaṅkaḻiyiluḷḷa cuṟāmīṉ eṟitalāluṟṟa puṇṇuṟṟa tāḷiṉaiyuṭaiya kōvēṟu kaḻutai, neṭunīr irukaḻi pari melintu - nīṇṭa nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kariya kaḻiyil cellutal melintiṭalālum, val vil iḷaiyaroṭu ellic cellātu acaii - valiya villiṉaiyuṭaiya niṉ ēvalaroṭu ivviraviṟ cellātu iḷaippāṟa;
14-16. peṇṇai ōṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappai - paṉai maraṅkaḷ ōṅkiya veḷḷiya maṇal paranta tōṭṭaṅkaḷil, aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ - aṉṟil taṉ tuṇaiyai aḻaikkum avviṭattē, ciṟu kural neytal em peruṅ kaḻi nāṭṭu - ciṟiya pūṅkottiṉai yuṭaiya neytal poruntiya ematu periya kaḻi cūḻnta nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ;
13. cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuvatu uṇṭō - nī taṅkic celliṉ keṭuvatoṉṟu uḷatō?
(muṭipu) peruma! pal katir ñāyiṟu kal cērntaṉṟu; ivaḷ aḻal toṭaṅkiṉaḷ; ataṉālum, attiri parimelin tiṭalālum iḷaiyaroṭu ellic cellātu acaii, em peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭuc cērntaṉai celiṉ, citaikuvatu uṇṭō?
(vi-rai) murukak kaṭavuḷ mikka cenniṟamuṭaiya reṉpatu cēy, cevvēḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷālum, "palarpukaḻ ñāyiṟu kaṭaṟ kaṇṭāṅku"1 "pavaḻat taṉṉa mēṉi"2 eṉpavaṟṟāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. cevvāṉattiṟku neṭuvēḷiṉ cemmēṉiyum, avvāṉattai yoṭṭi varicaiyākap paṟantu cellum kokkiṉattiṟku avaratu mārpilaṇinta muttāramum uvamamāyiṉa; āṟṟuppaṭaiyāl muruka vēḷai yēttiya āciriyar nakkīraṉāratu aṉpu kaṉinta tiruvuḷḷattil cevvāṉaiyum ataṉai yoṭṭip paṟantu cellum kokkiṉ niraiyaiyum kaṇṭuḻic cevvēḷiṉ tirumēṉiyum avaratu mārpiṉ muttāramum tōṉṟuvatu iyalpēyām. kokkiṉ nirai eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ kokkiṉ kūṭṭam eṉka. ukappu - uyarvu, paṟai yukappa - uyarntu paṟakka veṉak koḷka. cāyal ivaḷ eṉak kūṭṭuka. melintu eṉpataṉai meliya veṉat tirikka. aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ eṉṟataṉāl talaivaṉ varāviṭil talaivikkuṇṭām ētam kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. "elli........ aṉṟil, tuṇaiyoṉṟu piriyiṉun tuñcā kāṇ"1 eṉpataṉ urai kāṇka.
itu neytaliṟ kaḷavu.  "tiṇaimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilai yilavē"2 eṉpataṉāl amaintatu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, tōḻi talaivaṉai vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭikkōṭaṟku ic ceyyuḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, iḷam.
`vaikuṟu viṭiyal'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ, pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ iṭattuyttut talaivaṉai etirppaṭṭu uraittatu eṉṟum, īṇṭu neytaṟku eṟpāṭu vantateṉṟum, `poḻutum  āṟum kāppum'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `valvi liḷaiyaroṭu........... peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē' eṉpatu, `iraviṉum pakaliṉum nī vā' eṉat tōḻi kūṟuvataṟku utāraṇamākum eṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam kaḷavu aṟivuṟumeṉṟu añcātu varuka eṉṟaliṉ vaḻuvēṉum talaivaṉ varuttam paṟṟik kūṟaliṉ amaittār eṉṟum, `tērum yāṉaiyum kutiraiyum, piṟavum ūrntaṉar iyaṅkalum, uriyaṉ eṉpa'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `piṟa' āvaṉa kōvēṟu kaḻutaiyum civikaiyum mutaliyaṉavām eṉṟum, `kaḻiccuṟā veṟinta puṭṭā ḷattiri.......... parimelin tacaii' eṉa varutal ataṟku utāraṇamāku meṉṟum, iṉṉum iccūttirattu (iyaṅkalum eṉṟa) ummaiyāl iḷaiyarōṭu vantu taṉittuk kūṭutaluṅ koḷka; itaṟku `valvi liḷaiyaroṭu...... citaikuva tuṇṭō' eṉpatu utāraṇamāku meṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai nirai muṟṟum.
akanāṉūṟu - kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai nirai
